=== src/ui/FileChoosers.h ===
1: #pragma once
2: 
3: // LYNX's file choosers (issue #72: "File chooser fails to open or load the selected file", Arch Linux).
4: //
5: // JUCE's native chooser on Linux runs zenity (or kdialog in a KDE session / without zenity) as a child process and reads
6: // the chosen path from its output. JUCE looks for them with `which` (not installed on every distribution: Arch's base
7: // has none) and never checks that the dialog really opened: a zenity or kdialog that cannot start (a broken GTK / Qt, a
8: // missing library, a display it cannot open) ends at once with nothing printed, which JUCE reports as "cancelled", so
9: // nothing opens and nothing loads. LYNX now:
10: //  - on Linux uses the native dialog only when the program JUCE would run is on PATH (searched directly, no `which`)
11: //    and answers `--version` (exit code 0) within 5 s; otherwise JUCE's built-in chooser (always works);
12: //  - when a native dialog ends without a file less than a second after it was launched (it never showed: nobody picks
13: //    or cancels that fast), uses the built-in chooser for the rest of the session and opens it at once in its place;
14: //  - gives zenity / kdialog each filter pattern in upper case too (they match case-sensitively: "*.wav" hid "LOOP.WAV");
15: //  - LYNX_FILE_DIALOG=builtin or =native forces one (troubleshooting).
16: // Windows and macOS keep their native dialogs. Every chooser of the UI goes through makeFileChooser / launchChooser.
17: // Message thread only.
18: 
19: #include <juce_gui_basics/juce_gui_basics.h>
20: 
21: #include <functional>
22: #include <array>
23: #include <map>
24: #include <memory>
25: #include <vector>
26: 
27: #if JUCE_LINUX || JUCE_BSD
28:  #include <unistd.h>
29: #endif
30: 
31: namespace lynx::ui
32: {
33: namespace filechooser
34: {
35: // what a chooser was made with (to open the built-in one in its place) and whether it is native
36: struct Made
37: {
38:     juce::String title, filters;
39:     juce::File start;
40:     bool native = false;
41: };
42: inline std::map<const juce::FileChooser*, Made>& made()
43: {
44:     static std::map<const juce::FileChooser*, Made> table;
45:     return table;
46: }
47: inline bool& brokenThisSession()
48: {
49:     static bool broken = false;
50:     return broken;
51: }
52: // a native dialog that ended sooner than this without a file never showed
53: constexpr double quickFailMs = 1000.0;
54: 
55: #if JUCE_LINUX || JUCE_BSD
56: // an executable `name` in one of PATH's folders (what `which` would find, without needing `which`)
57: inline bool onPath (const juce::String& name)
58: {
59:     for (const auto& dir : juce::StringArray::fromTokens (juce::SystemStats::getEnvironmentVariable ("PATH", "/usr/local/bin:/usr/bin:/bin"), ":", ""))
60:         if (dir.isNotEmpty())
61:             if (const auto f = juce::File (dir).getChildFile (name); f.existsAsFile() && ::access (f.getFullPathName().toRawUTF8(), X_OK) == 0)
62:                 return true;
63:     return false;
64: }
65: 
66: // the program runs: `<name> --version` exits with 0 within 5 s
67: inline bool answers (const juce::String& name)
68: {
69:     juce::ChildProcess p;
70:     if (! p.start (juce::StringArray { name, "--version" }, juce::ChildProcess::wantStdOut))
71:         return false;
72:     if (! p.waitForProcessToFinish (5000))
73:     {
74:         p.kill();
75:         return false;
76:     }
77:     return p.getExitCode() == 0;
78: }
79: 
80: // The program JUCE's native chooser runs (juce_FileChooser_linux.cpp): kdialog in a KDE session or without zenity, else
81: // zenity. Empty when neither is on PATH.
82: inline juce::String nativeProgram (bool zenity, bool kdialog)
83: {
84:     const auto kde = juce::SystemStats::getEnvironmentVariable ("KDE_FULL_SESSION", {}).equalsIgnoreCase ("true");
85:     if (kdialog && (kde || ! zenity))
86:         return "kdialog";
87:     return zenity ? "zenity" : juce::String();
88: }
89: 
90: // zenity's and kdialog's file filters match case-sensitively (JUCE's own chooser does not): "*.wav" hid "LOOP.WAV".
91: // Each pattern also in upper case.
92: inline juce::String withUpperCase (const juce::String& filters)
93: {
94:     if (filters.isEmpty() || filters == "*" || filters == "*.*")
95:         return filters;
96:     juce::StringArray all;
97:     for (const auto& f : juce::StringArray::fromTokens (filters, ";,", "\""))
98:         if (const auto t = f.trim(); t.isNotEmpty())
99:             all.addIfNotAlreadyThere (t), all.addIfNotAlreadyThere (t.toUpperCase());
100:     return all.joinIntoString (";");
101: }
102: #endif
103: 
104: // Whether new choosers use the system's dialog (checked once per run on Linux)
105: inline bool nativeDialogs()
106: {
107:     const auto forced = juce::SystemStats::getEnvironmentVariable ("LYNX_FILE_DIALOG", {}).trim().toLowerCase();
108:     if (forced == "builtin" || forced == "built-in" || forced == "juce")
109:         return false;
110:     if (forced == "native")
111:         return true;
112:     if (brokenThisSession())
113:         return false;
114: #if JUCE_LINUX || JUCE_BSD
115:     static const bool usable = [] {
116:         const auto program = nativeProgram (onPath ("zenity"), onPath ("kdialog"));
117:         return program.isNotEmpty() && answers (program);
118:     }();
119:     return usable;
120: #else
121:     return true;
122: #endif
123: }
124: } // namespace filechooser
125: 
126: // SAF returns content:// URLs on Android, not local filesystem paths. Read selected documents off the message thread
127: // and return their bytes so parsers that require random access can consume a bounded in-memory copy.
128: struct ReadDocumentResult
129: {
130:     juce::MemoryBlock bytes;
131:     juce::String name;
132:     juce::String error;
133: };
134: 
135: inline void readDocumentAsync (juce::URL url, std::function<void (ReadDocumentResult)> callback)
136: {
137:     auto read = [url = std::move (url), callback] () mutable {
138:         ReadDocumentResult result;
139:         std::unique_ptr<juce::InputStream> input;
140: 
141:         if (url.isLocalFile())
142:         {
143:             const auto file = url.getLocalFile();
144:             result.name = file.getFileName();
145:             input = std::make_unique<juce::FileInputStream> (file);
146:         }
147:        #if JUCE_ANDROID
148:         else
149:         {
150:             auto document = juce::AndroidDocument::fromDocument (url);
151:             if (document)
152:             {
153:                 result.name = document.getInfo().getName();
154:                 input = document.createInputStream();
155:             }
156:         }
157:        #endif
158: 
159:         if (input == nullptr)
160:             result.error = "Could not open the selected document";
161:         else
162:         {
163:             juce::MemoryOutputStream bytes;
164:             std::array<char, 64 * 1024> buffer {};
165:             constexpr std::size_t maxDocumentBytes = 256u * 1024u * 1024u;
166:             while (result.error.isEmpty())
167:             {
168:                 const auto count = input->read (buffer.data(), (int) buffer.size());
169:                 if (count <= 0)
170:                     break;
171:                 if (bytes.getDataSize() + (std::size_t) count > maxDocumentBytes)
172:                 {
173:                     result.error = "The selected document is larger than 256 MB";
174:                     break;
175:                 }
176:                 bytes.write (buffer.data(), (std::size_t) count);
177:             }
178: 
179:             if (result.error.isEmpty())
180:             {
181:                 if (bytes.getDataSize() == 0)
182:                     result.error = "The selected document is empty or could not be read";
183:                 else
184:                     result.bytes = bytes.getMemoryBlock();
185:             }
186:         }
187: 
188:         juce::MessageManager::callAsync ([callback = std::move (callback), result = std::move (result)] () mutable {
189:             if (callback)
190:                 callback (std::move (result));
191:         });
192:     };
193: 
194:     if (! juce::Thread::launch (std::move (read)) && callback)
195:         callback ({ {}, {}, "Could not start the document reader" });
196: }
197: 
198: struct ImportDocumentResult
199: {
200:     juce::File file;
201:     juce::String name;
202:     juce::String error;
203: };
204: 
205: // Copies an Android SAF content:// URL (or returns an existing local file) into a persistent local file in
206: // `destFolder` off the message thread, preserving extension and streaming in chunks.
207: inline void importDocumentToFileAsync (juce::URL url, juce::File destFolder, juce::String fallbackName,
208:                                        std::function<void (ImportDocumentResult)> callback)
209: {
210:     auto job = [url = std::move (url), destFolder = std::move (destFolder), fallbackName = std::move (fallbackName), callback] () mutable {
211:         ImportDocumentResult result;
212:         if (url.isLocalFile())
213:         {
214:             result.file = url.getLocalFile();
215:             result.name = result.file.getFileName();
216:             if (! result.file.existsAsFile())
217:                 result.error = "Selected file does not exist";
218:         }
219:         else
220:         {
221:             std::unique_ptr<juce::InputStream> input;
222:            #if JUCE_ANDROID
223:             auto document = juce::AndroidDocument::fromDocument (url);
224:             if (document)
225:             {
226:                 result.name = document.getInfo().getName();
227:                 input = document.createInputStream();
228:             }
229:            #endif
230:             if (input == nullptr)
231:                 input = url.createInputStream (juce::URL::InputStreamOptions (juce::URL::ParameterHandling::inAddress));
232: 
233:             if (input == nullptr)
234:             {
235:                 result.error = "Could not open the selected document";
236:             }
237:             else
238:             {
239:                 if (result.name.isEmpty())
240:                     result.name = url.getFileName();
241:                 if (result.name.isEmpty())
242:                     result.name = fallbackName;
243: 
244:                 destFolder.createDirectory();
245:                 auto legal = juce::File::createLegalFileName (result.name);
246:                 if (legal.isEmpty())
247:                     legal = fallbackName;
248:                 const auto hash = juce::String::toHexString ((juce::int64) url.toString (false).hashCode64()).substring (0, 8);
249:                 const auto stem = juce::File (legal).getFileNameWithoutExtension();
250:                 auto ext = juce::File (legal).getFileExtension();
251:                 if (ext.isEmpty() && fallbackName.containsChar ('.'))
252:                     ext = juce::File (fallbackName).getFileExtension();
253:                 auto target = destFolder.getChildFile ((stem.isEmpty() ? juce::String ("imported") : stem) + "-" + hash + ext);
254: 
255:                 auto temp = target.getSiblingFile (target.getFileName() + ".tmp");
256:                 temp.deleteFile();
257:                 {
258:                     juce::FileOutputStream out (temp);
259:                     if (out.failedToOpen())
260:                     {
261:                         result.error = "Could not create local cache file";
262:                     }
263:                     else
264:                     {
265:                         std::array<char, 64 * 1024> buffer {};
266:                         std::uint64_t total = 0;
267:                         constexpr std::uint64_t maxBytes = 2ull * 1024ull * 1024ull * 1024ull;
268:                         while (result.error.isEmpty())
269:                         {
270:                             const auto n = input->read (buffer.data(), (int) buffer.size());
271:                             if (n < 0)
272:                             {
273:                                 result.error = "Failed reading the selected document";
274:                                 break;
275:                             }
276:                             if (n == 0)
277:                                 break;
278:                             total += (std::uint64_t) n;
279:                             if (total > maxBytes)
280:                             {
281:                                 result.error = "The selected file is too large";
282:                                 break;
283:                             }
284:                             if (! out.write (buffer.data(), (size_t) n))
285:                             {
286:                                 result.error = "Failed writing local cache file";
287:                                 break;
288:                             }
289:                         }
290:                         out.flush();
291:                         if (result.error.isEmpty() && total == 0)
292:                             result.error = "The selected document is empty or could not be read";
293:                     }
294:                 }
295:                 if (result.error.isEmpty())
296:                 {
297:                     target.deleteFile();
298:                     if (temp.moveFileTo (target))
299:                         result.file = target;
300:                     else
301:                         result.error = "Could not save local copy of document";
302:                 }
303:                 if (result.error.isNotEmpty())
304:                     temp.deleteFile();
305:             }
306:         }
307: 
308:         juce::MessageManager::callAsync ([callback = std::move (callback), result = std::move (result)] () mutable {
309:             if (callback)
310:                 callback (std::move (result));
311:         });
312:     };
313: 
314:     if (! juce::Thread::launch (std::move (job)) && callback)
315:         callback ({ {}, {}, "Could not start the document importer" });
316: }
317: 
318: // A juce::FileChooser: the system's dialog where it works, else JUCE's built-in one (see above)
319: inline std::unique_ptr<juce::FileChooser> makeFileChooser (const juce::String& title, const juce::File& start = {},
320:                                                            const juce::String& filters = {})
321: {
322:     const auto native = filechooser::nativeDialogs();
323: #if JUCE_LINUX || JUCE_BSD
324:     auto chooser = std::make_unique<juce::FileChooser> (title, start, native ? filechooser::withUpperCase (filters) : filters, native);
325: #else
326:     auto chooser = std::make_unique<juce::FileChooser> (title, start, filters, native);
327: #endif
328:     filechooser::made()[chooser.get()] = { title, filters, start, native };
329:     return chooser;
330: }
331: 
332: inline std::shared_ptr<juce::FileChooser> makeSharedFileChooser (const juce::String& title, const juce::File& start = {},
333:                                                                  const juce::String& filters = {})
334: {
335:     return makeFileChooser (title, start, filters);
336: }
337: 
338: namespace filechooser
339: {
340: // the built-in chooser in place of a native one that never showed; `replace` puts it where the old one was kept
341: template <typename Replace>
342: void launchFallback (const Made& m, int flags, std::function<void (const juce::FileChooser&)> callback, Replace&& replace)
343: {
344:     brokenThisSession() = true;
345:     juce::Logger::writeToLog ("File chooser: the system dialog did not open; using the built-in one from now on");
346:     auto next = std::make_unique<juce::FileChooser> (m.title, m.start, m.filters, false);
347:     auto* raw = next.get();
348:     replace (std::move (next));
349:     raw->launchAsync (flags, std::move (callback));
350: }
351: 
352: // the callback, watching a native dialog that ends at once without a file
353: template <typename Replace>
354: std::function<void (const juce::FileChooser&)> watched (const juce::FileChooser* chooser, int flags,
355:                                                          std::function<void (const juce::FileChooser&)> callback, Replace replace)
356: {
357:     const auto it = made().find (chooser);
358:     const auto info = it != made().end() ? it->second : Made {};
359:     if (it != made().end())
360:         made().erase (it); // (read once: the chooser launches once)
361:     const auto forced = juce::SystemStats::getEnvironmentVariable ("LYNX_FILE_DIALOG", {}).trim().equalsIgnoreCase ("native");
362:     if (! info.native || forced)
363:         return callback;
364:     const auto launched = juce::Time::getMillisecondCounterHiRes();
365:     return [info, flags, callback, replace, launched] (const juce::FileChooser& fc) mutable {
366:         if (fc.getURLResults().isEmpty() && juce::Time::getMillisecondCounterHiRes() - launched < quickFailMs)
367:         {
368:             launchFallback (info, flags, callback, replace);
369:             return;
370:         }
371:         if (callback)
372:             callback (fc);
373:     };
374: }
375: } // namespace filechooser
376: 
377: // chooser->launchAsync (flags, callback), with the built-in chooser opened in its place when the system's dialog never
378: // showed. `holder` keeps the chooser (and, after a fallback, the built-in one that replaced it).
379: inline void launchChooser (std::unique_ptr<juce::FileChooser>& holder, int flags, std::function<void (const juce::FileChooser&)> callback)
380: {
381:     if (holder == nullptr)
382:         return;
383:     auto* chooser = holder.get();
384:     chooser->launchAsync (flags, filechooser::watched (chooser, flags, std::move (callback), [&holder] (std::unique_ptr<juce::FileChooser> next) {
385:                               // (called from inside the old chooser's callback: it is deleted after it returns)
386:                               std::shared_ptr<juce::FileChooser> old (holder.release());
387:                               holder = std::move (next);
388:                               juce::MessageManager::callAsync ([old] {});
389:                           }));
390: }
391: 
392: // the same for a chooser kept alive by its own callback (a std::shared_ptr captured in it)
393: inline void launchChooser (const std::shared_ptr<juce::FileChooser>& chooser, int flags, std::function<void (const juce::FileChooser&)> callback)
394: {
395:     if (chooser == nullptr)
396:         return;
397:     chooser->launchAsync (flags, filechooser::watched (chooser.get(), flags, std::move (callback), [] (std::unique_ptr<juce::FileChooser> next) {
398:                               // the built-in one replacing it lives until the end of the session (at most one per
399:                               // dialog that failed: the later ones are built-in from the start)
400:                               static std::vector<std::unique_ptr<juce::FileChooser>> kept;
401:                               kept.push_back (std::move (next));
402:                           }));
403: }
404: } // namespace lynx::ui

656:     void layoutControls (juce::Rectangle<int> r)
657:     {
658:         const auto rowH = juce::jlimit (70, 96, (r.getHeight() - 70) / 3);
659:         const auto half = [] (juce::Rectangle<int>& row) {
660:             auto left = row.removeFromLeft (row.getWidth() / 2 - 4);
661:             row.removeFromLeft (8);
662:             return left;
663:         };
664:         const auto place = [this] (juce::Rectangle<int> box, const juce::String& title, std::initializer_list<juce::Component*> items) {
665:             sections.push_back ({ box.toFloat(), title });
666:             auto inner = box.reduced (6, 0).withTrimmedTop (18).withTrimmedBottom (4);
667:             const auto n = (int) items.size();
668:             const auto w = inner.getWidth() / juce::jmax (1, n);
669:             for (auto* c : items)
670:                 c->setBounds (inner.removeFromLeft (w).reduced (2, 0));
671:         };
672:         auto row1 = r.removeFromTop (rowH);
673:         place (half (row1), tr ("Envelope"), { attack, decay, sustain, release });
674:         place (row1, tr ("Pitch LFO"), { lfoDelay, lfoAmount, lfoSpeed, modWheel });
675:         r.removeFromTop (6);
676:         auto row2 = r.removeFromTop (rowH);
677:         place (half (row2), tr ("Filter"), { cutoff, cutMod, resonance });
678:         place (row2, tr ("Voice"), { volume, bend, poly, transpose });
679:         r.removeFromTop (6);
680:         auto row3 = r.removeFromTop (rowH);
681:         const auto fx = [this] (juce::Rectangle<int> box, const juce::String& title, juce::Component* send, juce::Component* over, juce::Component* builtIn, juce::Component* track) {
682:             sections.push_back ({ box.toFloat(), title });
683:             auto inner = box.reduced (6, 0).withTrimmedTop (18).withTrimmedBottom (4);
684:             send->setBounds (inner.removeFromLeft (juce::jmin (64, inner.getWidth() / 3)));
685:             inner.removeFromLeft (4);
686:             auto boxes = inner.removeFromLeft (inner.getWidth() / 2);
687:             const auto h = juce::jmin (22, boxes.getHeight() / 2);
688:             over->setBounds (boxes.removeFromTop (h));
689:             builtIn->setBounds (boxes.removeFromTop (h));
690:             inner.removeFromLeft (4);
691:             track->setBounds (inner.withSizeKeepingCentre (inner.getWidth(), juce::jmin (34, inner.getHeight())));
692:         };
693:         fx (half (row3), tr ("Reverb"), reverbSend, reverbOverride, reverbBuiltIn, reverbTrack);
694:         fx (row3, tr ("Chorus"), chorusSend, chorusOverride, chorusBuiltIn, chorusTrack);
695:         r.removeFromTop (6);
696:         // options
697:         sections.push_back ({ r.toFloat(), tr ("Options") });
698:         auto inner = r.reduced (8, 0).withTrimmedTop (18).withTrimmedBottom (4);
699:         auto line = inner.removeFromTop (juce::jmin (34, inner.getHeight() / 2));
700:         const auto w = line.getWidth() / 3;
701:         resampling->setBounds (line.removeFromLeft (w).reduced (3, 0));
702:         mode->setBounds (line.removeFromLeft (w).reduced (3, 0));
703:         curve->setBounds (line.reduced (3, 0));
704:         inner.removeFromTop (2);
705:         const auto tw = inner.getWidth() / 3;
706:         const auto th = juce::jmin (20, inner.getHeight());
707:         velFilter->setBounds (inner.removeFromLeft (tw).withHeight (th).reduced (3, 0));
708:         emu->setBounds (inner.removeFromLeft (tw).withHeight (th).reduced (3, 0));
709:         loops->setBounds (inner.withHeight (th).reduced (3, 0));
710:     }

742:     void chooseFont()
743:     {
744:         const auto start = currentPath().empty() ? juce::File::getSpecialLocation (juce::File::userDocumentsDirectory) : juce::File (u8 (currentPath()));
745:        #if JUCE_ANDROID
746:         const juce::String filter = "*";
747:        #else
748:         const juce::String filter = "*.sf2;*.sf3;*.sfz";
749:        #endif
750:         chooser = lynx::ui::makeFileChooser (tr ("Load SoundFont"), start, filter);
751:         lynx::ui::launchChooser (chooser, juce::FileBrowserComponent::openMode | juce::FileBrowserComponent::canSelectFiles,
752:                               [safe = juce::Component::SafePointer<SoundFontEditor> (this)] (const juce::FileChooser& fc) {
753:                                   const auto url = fc.getURLResult();
754:                                   if (safe == nullptr || url.toString (false).isEmpty())
755:                                       return;
756:                                   lynx::ui::importDocumentToFileAsync (
757:                                       url, PluginHost::settingsFolder().getChildFile ("imported-soundfonts"), "soundfont.sf2",
758:                                       [safe] (lynx::ui::ImportDocumentResult imported) {
759:                                           if (safe == nullptr)
760:                                               return;
761:                                           if (imported.error.isNotEmpty() || ! imported.file.existsAsFile())
762:                                           {
763:                                               juce::AlertWindow::showMessageBoxAsync (
764:                                                   juce::MessageBoxIconType::WarningIcon, tr ("Load SoundFont"),
765:                                                   imported.error.isNotEmpty() ? imported.error : tr ("Could not read the selected file."));
766:                                               return;
767:                                           }
768:                                           const auto path = std8 (imported.file.getFullPathName());
769:                                           int bank = 0, program = 0;
770:                                           if (! firstSoundFontPreset (path, bank, program))
771:                                           {
772:                                               juce::AlertWindow::showMessageBoxAsync (juce::MessageBoxIconType::WarningIcon, tr ("Load SoundFont"),
773:                                                                                       tr ("This file is not a SoundFont Radiant can read."));
774:                                               return;
775:                                           }
776:                                           loadInto (safe->engine, safe->channel, path, bank, program);
777:                                           sf::PresetIndex::shared().addFont (path);
778:                                           safe->refreshAll();
779:                                       });
780:                               });
781:     }
782: 
783:     void stepPreset (int delta)
784:     {
785:         if (fontRows.empty())
786:             return;
787:         const auto bank = (int) paramOf (engine, channel, sf::pBank), program = (int) paramOf (engine, channel, sf::pProgram);
788:         int at = 0;
789:         for (std::size_t i = 0; i < fontRows.size(); ++i)
790:             if (fontRows[i].bank == bank && fontRows[i].program == program)
791:                 at = (int) i;
792:         at = (at + delta + (int) fontRows.size()) % (int) fontRows.size();
793:         loadInto (engine, channel, currentPath(), fontRows[(std::size_t) at].bank, fontRows[(std::size_t) at].program);
794:         refreshAll();
795:     }

797:     void showPatchMenu()
798:     {
799:         juce::PopupMenu menu;
800:         const auto bank = (int) paramOf (engine, channel, sf::pBank), program = (int) paramOf (engine, channel, sf::pProgram);
801:         if (fontRows.empty())
802:             menu.addItem (tr ("(no SoundFont loaded)"), false, false, nullptr);
803:         for (const auto& r : fontRows)
804:             menu.addItem (juce::String::formatted ("%03d:%03d  ", r.bank, r.program) + u8 (r.name), true, r.bank == bank && r.program == program,
805:                           [this, r] {
806:                               loadInto (engine, channel, r.path, r.bank, r.program);
807:                               refreshAll();
808:                           });
809:         menu.showMenuAsync (juce::PopupMenu::Options().withTargetComponent (&patch).withMaximumNumColumns (3));
810:     }

812:     void showFoldersMenu()
813:     {
814:         juce::PopupMenu menu;
815:         menu.addSectionHeader (tr ("SoundFont folders"));
816:         for (const auto& f : soundFontFolders())
817:             menu.addItem (u8 (f), false, false, nullptr);
818:         menu.addSeparator();
819:         menu.addItem (tr ("Add folder..."), [this] {
820:             chooser = lynx::ui::makeFileChooser (tr ("Add a SoundFont folder"), juce::File::getSpecialLocation (juce::File::userDocumentsDirectory));
821:             lynx::ui::launchChooser (chooser, juce::FileBrowserComponent::openMode | juce::FileBrowserComponent::canSelectDirectories,
822:                                   [] (const juce::FileChooser& fc) {
823:                                       if (fc.getResult() == juce::File())
824:                                           return;
825:                                       auto lines = juce::StringArray::fromLines (soundFontFoldersFile().loadFileAsString());
826:                                       lines.removeEmptyStrings();
827:                                       lines.addIfNotAlreadyThere (fc.getResult().getFullPathName());
828:                                       soundFontFoldersFile().getParentDirectory().createDirectory();
829:                                       soundFontFoldersFile().replaceWithText (lines.joinIntoString ("\n") + "\n");
830:                                       ensureSoundFontIndex (true);
831:                                   });
832:         });
833:         juce::PopupMenu remove;
834:         for (const auto& line : juce::StringArray::fromLines (soundFontFoldersFile().loadFileAsString()))
835:             if (line.trim().isNotEmpty())
836:                 remove.addItem (line.trim(), [line] {
837:                     auto lines = juce::StringArray::fromLines (soundFontFoldersFile().loadFileAsString());
838:                     lines.removeString (line);
839:                     lines.removeEmptyStrings();
840:                     soundFontFoldersFile().replaceWithText (lines.joinIntoString ("\n") + "\n");
841:                     ensureSoundFontIndex (true);
842:                 });
843:         menu.addSubMenu (tr ("Remove folder"), remove, remove.getNumItems() > 0);
844:         menu.addItem (tr ("Rescan now"), [] { ensureSoundFontIndex (true); });
845:         menu.showMenuAsync (juce::PopupMenu::Options().withTargetComponent (&folders));
846:     }

848:     void playNote()
849:     {
850:         const auto drums = (int) paramOf (engine, channel, sf::pBank) == 128;
851:         if (noteKey >= 0)
852:             noteOff();
853:         if (channel.channel < 0) // a Patcher node: the note is rendered off-line (the node plays only what is cabled to it)
854:         {
855:             previewSoundFontPreset (engine, currentPath(), (int) paramOf (engine, channel, sf::pBank), (int) paramOf (engine, channel, sf::pProgram));
856:             return;
857:         }
858:         noteKey = drums ? 38 : 60;
859:         engine.sendLiveNote (channel.channel, noteKey, 0.8f, true);
860:         noteOffAt = juce::Time::getMillisecondCounter() + 450;
861:     }

863:     void showList (bool all)
864:     {
865:         searchMode = all;
866:         list.showFont = all;
867:         refreshLists();
868:     }

870:     void refreshHeader()
871:     {
872:         const auto path = currentPath();
873:         const auto bank = (int) paramOf (engine, channel, sf::pBank), program = (int) paramOf (engine, channel, sf::pProgram);
874:         juce::String name;
875:         for (const auto& r : fontRows)
876:             if (r.bank == bank && r.program == program)
877:                 name = u8 (r.name);
878:         patch.setButtonText (juce::String::formatted ("%03d:%03d  ", bank, program) + (name.isEmpty() ? juce::String ("--") : name));
879:         if (path.empty())
880:             fontTitle = tr ("No SoundFont loaded");
881:         else if (handle != nullptr && handle->finished() && handle->font() == nullptr)
882:             fontTitle = tr ("{0} (could not be loaded: {1})", { juce::File (u8 (path)).getFileName(), tr (u8 (handle->error())) });
883:         else if (handle != nullptr && ! handle->finished())
884:             fontTitle = tr ("{0} (loading...)", { juce::File (u8 (path)).getFileName() });
885:         else
886:             fontTitle = handle != nullptr && handle->font() != nullptr && ! handle->font()->name.empty()
887:                             ? u8 (handle->font()->name) + "  (" + juce::File (u8 (path)).getFileName() + ")"
888:                             : juce::File (u8 (path)).getFileName();
889:         repaint (fontNameArea);
890:     }
891: 
892:     void refreshLists()
893:     {
894:         const auto path = currentPath();
895:         const auto bank = (int) paramOf (engine, channel, sf::pBank), program = (int) paramOf (engine, channel, sf::pProgram);
896:         if (! searchMode)
897:         {
898:             auto rows = fontRows;
899:             const auto query = std8 (search.getText());
900:             if (! query.empty())
901:             {
902:                 std::vector<Row> kept;
903:                 const auto q = search::parse (query);
904:                 for (const auto& r : rows)
905:                     if (search::matches (q, r.name + " | " + r.category))
906:                         kept.push_back (r);
907:                 rows = std::move (kept);
908:             }
909:             list.setRows (std::move (rows), path, bank, program);
910:         }
911:         else
912:         {
913:             std::vector<Row> rows;
914:             for (const auto& h : sf::PresetIndex::shared().search (std8 (search.getText()), 2000))
915:                 rows.push_back ({ h.path, h.fontName, h.presetName, h.category, h.bank, h.program });
916:             list.setRows (std::move (rows), path, bank, program);
917:         }
918:         const auto& index = sf::PresetIndex::shared();
919:         const auto fonts = index.fonts().size();
920:         status.setText (searchMode ? tr ("{0} presets in {1} SoundFonts", { juce::String ((int) index.presetCount()), juce::String ((int) fonts) })
921:                                          + (index.busy() ? tr (" (indexing...)") : juce::String()) + "  -  "
922:                                          + tr ("{0} shown", { juce::String (list.rowCount()) })
923:                                    : tr ("{0} presets", { juce::String ((int) fontRows.size()) }),
924:                         juce::dontSendNotification);
925:     }
926: 
927:     void refreshAll()
928:     {
929:         const auto path = currentPath();
930:         if (path != loadedPath)
931:         {
932:             loadedPath = path;
933:             handle = path.empty() ? nullptr : sf::FontLibrary::instance().get (path);
934:             fontRows.clear();
935:             rowsFromFont = false;
936:         }
937:         if (! rowsFromFont && handle != nullptr && handle->font() != nullptr)
938:         {
939:             fontRows.clear();
940:             for (const auto& p : handle->font()->presets)
941:                 fontRows.push_back ({ path, handle->font()->name, p.name, sf::category (p.bank, p.program, p.name), p.bank, p.program });
942:             rowsFromFont = true;
943:         }
944:         refreshHeader();
945:         refreshLists();
946:         for (auto* c : getChildren())
947:             c->repaint();
948:     }
949: 
950:     void timerCallback() override
951:     {
952:         if (noteKey >= 0 && juce::Time::getMillisecondCounter() >= noteOffAt)
953:         {
954:             noteOff();
955:             noteKey = -1;
956:         }
957:         if (channelOf (engine, channel) == nullptr)
958:             return;
959:         const auto gen = sf::PresetIndex::shared().generation();
960:         const auto loading = handle != nullptr && ! rowsFromFont && handle->finished();
961:         const auto busy = sf::PresetIndex::shared().busy();
962:         if (gen != lastGeneration || loading || currentPath() != loadedPath || busy != wasBusy
963:             || (paramOf (engine, channel, sf::pBank) != lastBank || paramOf (engine, channel, sf::pProgram) != lastProgram))
964:         {
965:             lastGeneration = gen;
966:             wasBusy = busy;
967:             lastBank = paramOf (engine, channel, sf::pBank), lastProgram = paramOf (engine, channel, sf::pProgram);
968:             refreshAll();
969:         }
970:     }

979:     std::vector<std::unique_ptr<SfToggle>> toggles;
980:     std::vector<std::unique_ptr<SfChoice>> choices;
981:     SfKnob *attack {}, *decay {}, *sustain {}, *release {}, *lfoDelay {}, *lfoAmount {}, *lfoSpeed {}, *cutoff {}, *cutMod {}, *resonance {},
982:         *reverbSend {}, *chorusSend {}, *volume {}, *bend {}, *poly {}, *transpose {}, *modWheel {};
983:     SfChoice *reverbTrack {}, *chorusTrack {};
984:     SfToggle *reverbOverride {}, *reverbBuiltIn {}, *chorusOverride {}, *chorusBuiltIn {}, *velFilter {}, *emu {}, *loops {};
985:     SfChoice *resampling {}, *mode {}, *curve {};
986:     std::vector<std::pair<juce::Rectangle<float>, juce::String>> sections;
987:     juce::Rectangle<int> fontNameArea, listArea;
988:     juce::String fontTitle;
989:     std::unique_ptr<juce::FileChooser> chooser;
990:     std::shared_ptr<sf::FontHandle> handle;
991:     std::string loadedPath;
992:     std::vector<Row> fontRows;
993:     bool rowsFromFont = false, searchMode = false, wasBusy = false;
994:     int lastGeneration = -1, noteKey = -1;
995:     float lastBank = -1, lastProgram = -1;
996:     juce::uint32 noteOffAt = 0;
997: };
998: } // namespace

1015: std::vector<std::string> soundFontFolders()
1016: {
1017:     std::vector<std::string> out;
1018:     const auto add = [&out] (const juce::File& f) {
1019:         if (f.isDirectory())
1020:         {
1021:             const auto p = std8 (f.getFullPathName());
1022:             if (std::find (out.begin(), out.end(), p) == out.end())
1023:                 out.push_back (p);
1024:         }
1025:     };
1026:     const auto& roots = flStudioAccess().roots;
1027:     if (! roots.data.empty())
1028:         add (juce::File (u8 (roots.data)).getChildFile ("Patches").getChildFile ("Soundfonts"));
1029:     if (! roots.userData.empty())
1030:         add (juce::File (u8 (roots.userData)));
1031:     for (const auto& line : juce::StringArray::fromLines (soundFontFoldersFile().loadFileAsString()))
1032:         if (line.trim().isNotEmpty())
1033:             add (juce::File (line.trim()));
1034:     add (juce::File::getSpecialLocation (juce::File::userDocumentsDirectory));
1035:     add (juce::File::getSpecialLocation (juce::File::userMusicDirectory));
1036:     add (juce::File::getSpecialLocation (juce::File::userDesktopDirectory));
1037:     add (juce::File::getSpecialLocation (juce::File::userHomeDirectory).getChildFile ("Downloads"));
1038:     for (const auto& line : juce::StringArray::fromLines (PluginHost::settingsFolder().getChildFile ("browser-folders.txt").loadFileAsString()))
1039:         if (line.trim().isNotEmpty())
1040:             add (juce::File (line.trim()));
1041:     return out;
1042: }

1044: void ensureSoundFontIndex (bool force)
1045: {
1046:     static bool cacheSet = false;
1047:     auto& index = sf::PresetIndex::shared();
1048:     if (! cacheSet)
1049:     {
1050:         index.setCacheFile (std8 (PluginHost::settingsFolder().getChildFile ("soundfont-index.txt").getFullPathName()));
1051:         cacheSet = true;
1052:     }
1053:     index.setFolders (soundFontFolders(), force);
1054: }
1055: 
1056: void previewSoundFontPreset (AudioEngine& engine, const std::string& path, int bank, int program)
1057: {
1058:     static std::atomic<int> latest { 0 };
1059:     const auto ticket = ++latest;
1060:     std::thread ([&engine, path, bank, program, ticket] {
1061:         auto handle = sf::FontLibrary::instance().getNow (path);
1062:         if (handle->font() == nullptr || ticket != latest.load())
1063:             return;
1064:         constexpr double rate = 48000.0;
1065:         auto inst = sf::makeInstrument (handle);
1066:         inst->prepare (rate);
1067:         inst->configure (sf::makeChannel (path, bank, program), 120.0);
1068:         auto sample = std::make_shared<SampleData>();
1069:         sample->sampleRate = rate;
1070:         const auto total = (int) (rate * 1.6), held = (int) (rate * 0.7);
1071:         sample->left.assign ((std::size_t) total, 0.0f);
1072:         sample->right.assign ((std::size_t) total, 0.0f);
1073:         const auto key = bank == 128 ? 38 : 60;
1074:         inst->noteOn (key, 0.8f);
1075:         for (int at = 0; at < total; at += 256)
1076:         {
1077:             if (at >= held && at - 256 < held)
1078:                 inst->noteOff (key);
1079:             inst->render (sample->left.data() + at, sample->right.data() + at, std::min (256, total - at), 1.0f, 1.0f);
1080:         }
1081:         juce::MessageManager::callAsync ([&engine, sample, ticket] {
1082:             if (ticket == latest.load())
1083:                 engine.previewSample (sample);
1084:         });
1085:     }).detach();
1086: }
1087: 
1088: int addSoundFontChannel (AudioEngine& engine, const std::string& path, int bank, int program, int insertAt)
1089: {
1090:     auto c = sf::makeChannel (path, bank, program);
1091:     if (! path.empty())
1092:     {
1093:         // the channel is named after the preset
1094:         const auto info = sf::scanFontFile (path);
1095:         for (const auto& p : info.presets)
1096:             if (p.bank == bank && p.program == program && ! p.name.empty())
1097:                 c.name = p.name;
1098:     }
1099:     int index = 0;
1100:     engine.editSong ([&] (Song& s) {
1101:         c.mixerTrack = s.claimFreeInsert();
1102:         if (insertAt < 0 || insertAt > (int) s.channels.size())
1103:         {
1104:             s.channels.push_back (c);
1105:             index = (int) s.channels.size() - 1;
1106:         }
1107:         else
1108:         {
1109:             s.channels.insert (s.channels.begin() + insertAt, c);
1110:             index = insertAt;
1111:         }
1112:     });
1113:     engine.setSelectedChannel (index);
1114:     if (! path.empty())
1115:         sf::PresetIndex::shared().addFont (path);
1116:     return index;
1117: }
1118: 
1119: void loadSoundFontInto (AudioEngine& engine, int channel, const std::string& path, int bank, int program)
1120: {
1121:     loadInto (engine, SfTarget::rack (channel), path, bank, program);
1122: }
1123: 
1124: bool firstSoundFontPreset (const std::string& path, int& bank, int& program)
1125: {
1126:     const auto info = sf::scanFontFile (path);
1127:     if (! info.valid)
1128:         return false;
1129:     bank = 0, program = 0;
1130:     bool first = true;
1131:     for (const auto& p : info.presets)
1132:         if (first || p.bank < bank || (p.bank == bank && p.program < program))
1133:             bank = p.bank, program = p.program, first = false;
1134:     return true;
1135: }
1136: 
1137: bool openSoundFontFile (AudioEngine& engine, const juce::File& file, int channel)
1138: {
1139:     const auto path = std8 (file.getFullPathName());
1140:     int bank = 0, program = 0;
1141:     if (! firstSoundFontPreset (path, bank, program))
1142:         return false;
1143:     if (channelOf (engine, SfTarget::rack (channel)) != nullptr)
1144:     {
1145:         loadInto (engine, SfTarget::rack (channel), path, bank, program);
1146:         sf::PresetIndex::shared().addFont (path);
1147:     }
1148:     else
1149:         addSoundFontChannel (engine, path, bank, program);
1150:     return true;
1151: }
1152: 
1153: juce::String soundFontDragDescription (const std::string& path, int bank, int program)
1154: {
1155:     return juce::String (dragPrefix) + juce::String (bank) + "|" + juce::String (program) + "|" + u8 (path);
1156: }
1157: 
1158: bool parseSoundFontDrag (const juce::String& description, std::string& path, int& bank, int& program)
1159: {
1160:     if (! description.startsWith (dragPrefix))
1161:         return false;
1162:     const auto rest = description.substring ((int) std::strlen (dragPrefix));
1163:     bank = rest.upToFirstOccurrenceOf ("|", false, false).getIntValue();
1164:     const auto rest2 = rest.fromFirstOccurrenceOf ("|", false, false);
1165:     program = rest2.upToFirstOccurrenceOf ("|", false, false).getIntValue();
1166:     path = std8 (rest2.fromFirstOccurrenceOf ("|", false, false));
1167:     return ! path.empty();
1168: }
1169: } // namespace lynx


=== src/core/SoundFontLoad.cpp ===
1: // LYNX Soundfont: reading .sf2 / .sf3 files (round 158). TinySoundFont merges the generators into regions; LYNX reads
2: // and validates the RIFF chunks itself (so damaged files are rejected before TinySoundFont indexes them) and keeps the
3: // samples as 16-bit. See core/SoundFont.h.
4: 
5: #include "core/Sfz.h"
6: #include "core/SoundFont.h"
7: 
8: #include <algorithm>
9: #include <cmath>
10: #include <cstring>
11: #include <filesystem>
12: #include <fstream>
13: 
14: #if defined(__clang__)
15:  #pragma clang diagnostic push
16:  #pragma clang diagnostic ignored "-Weverything"
17: #elif defined(__GNUC__)
18:  #pragma GCC diagnostic push
19:  #pragma GCC diagnostic ignored "-Wall"
20:  #pragma GCC diagnostic ignored "-Wextra"
21:  #pragma GCC diagnostic ignored "-Wpedantic"
22:  #pragma GCC diagnostic ignored "-Wconversion"
23:  #pragma GCC diagnostic ignored "-Wsign-compare"
24:  #pragma GCC diagnostic ignored "-Wunused-function"
25:  #pragma GCC diagnostic ignored "-Wunused-parameter"
26:  #pragma GCC diagnostic ignored "-Wmisleading-indentation"
27:  #pragma GCC diagnostic ignored "-Wmaybe-uninitialized"
28: #elif defined(_MSC_VER)
29:  #pragma warning(push, 0)
30: #endif
31: #define STB_VORBIS_NO_STDIO
32: #define STB_VORBIS_NO_PUSHDATA_API
33: #include "../../third_party/stb_vorbis/stb_vorbis.c"
34: #define TSF_STATIC
35: #define TSF_NO_STDIO
36: #define TSF_IMPLEMENTATION
37: #include "../../third_party/tinysoundfont/tsf.h"
38: #if defined(__clang__)
39:  #pragma clang diagnostic pop
40: #elif defined(__GNUC__)
41:  #pragma GCC diagnostic pop
42: #elif defined(_MSC_VER)
43:  #pragma warning(pop)
44: #endif
45: 
46: namespace lynx::sf
47: {
48: namespace
49: {
50: std::uint32_t u32 (const std::uint8_t* p) noexcept { return (std::uint32_t) p[0] | ((std::uint32_t) p[1] << 8) | ((std::uint32_t) p[2] << 16) | ((std::uint32_t) p[3] << 24); }
51: std::uint16_t u16 (const std::uint8_t* p) noexcept { return (std::uint16_t) (p[0] | (p[1] << 8)); }
52: 
53: struct Span
54: {
55:     const std::uint8_t* data = nullptr;
56:     std::size_t size = 0;
57: };
58: 
59: // The chunks LYNX needs, located inside the file's bytes
60: struct Chunks
61: {
62:     Span inam, smpl, sm24;
63:     Span phdr, pbag, pmod, pgen, inst, ibag, imod, igen, shdr;
64:     int versionMajor = 2;
65:     bool ok = false;
66:     std::string error;
67: };
68: 
69: std::string clean20 (const std::uint8_t* p)
70: {
71:     std::string s;
72:     for (int i = 0; i < 20 && p[i] != 0; ++i)
73:         s.push_back ((char) (p[i] >= 32 && p[i] < 127 ? p[i] : '?'));
74:     while (! s.empty() && s.back() == ' ')
75:         s.pop_back();
76:     return s;
77: }
78: 
79: // Walks RIFF sfbk → LIST INFO / sdta / pdta. `data` may hold only the header part of a file (scan) when the sample
80: // chunk was skipped: then `smpl` points past the data that was read (size kept for the checks).
81: Chunks locate (const std::uint8_t* data, std::size_t size, bool needSamples)
82: {
83:     Chunks c;
84:     if (size < 12 || std::memcmp (data, "RIFF", 4) != 0 || std::memcmp (data + 8, "sfbk", 4) != 0)
85:     {
86:         c.error = "not a SoundFont (no RIFF sfbk header)";
87:         return c;
88:     }
89:     const auto riffEnd = std::min<std::size_t> (size, 8 + (std::size_t) u32 (data + 4));
90:     std::size_t pos = 12;
91:     while (pos + 8 <= riffEnd)
92:     {
93:         const auto* id = data + pos;
94:         const auto len = (std::size_t) u32 (data + pos + 4);
95:         const auto body = pos + 8;
96:         if (std::memcmp (id, "LIST", 4) == 0 && len >= 4 && body + 4 <= size)
97:         {
98:             const auto listEnd = std::min (body + len, size);
99:             const auto* type = data + body;
100:             std::size_t p = body + 4;
101:             while (p + 8 <= listEnd)
102:             {
103:                 const auto* sid = data + p;
104:                 const auto slen = (std::size_t) u32 (data + p + 4);
105:                 const auto sbody = p + 8;
106:                 const auto avail = sbody <= listEnd ? std::min (slen, listEnd - sbody) : 0;
107:                 const Span span { data + sbody, avail };
108:                 if (std::memcmp (type, "INFO", 4) == 0)
109:                 {
110:                     if (std::memcmp (sid, "INAM", 4) == 0)
111:                         c.inam = span;
112:                     else if (std::memcmp (sid, "ifil", 4) == 0 && avail >= 2)
113:                         c.versionMajor = u16 (data + sbody);
114:                 }
115:                 else if (std::memcmp (type, "sdta", 4) == 0)
116:                 {
117:                     if (std::memcmp (sid, "smpl", 4) == 0)
118:                         c.smpl = { data + sbody, needSamples ? avail : slen };
119:                     else if (std::memcmp (sid, "sm24", 4) == 0)
120:                         c.sm24 = span;
121:                 }
122:                 else if (std::memcmp (type, "pdta", 4) == 0)
123:                 {
124:                     static const char* names[] = { "phdr", "pbag", "pmod", "pgen", "inst", "ibag", "imod", "igen", "shdr" };
125:                     Span* spans[] = { &c.phdr, &c.pbag, &c.pmod, &c.pgen, &c.inst, &c.ibag, &c.imod, &c.igen, &c.shdr };
126:                     for (int k = 0; k < 9; ++k)
127:                         if (std::memcmp (sid, names[k], 4) == 0)
128:                             *spans[k] = span;
129:                 }
130:                 if (slen > listEnd - std::min (listEnd, sbody))
131:                     break;
132:                 p = sbody + slen + (slen & 1);
133:             }
134:         }
135:         if (len > riffEnd - std::min (riffEnd, body))
136:             break;
137:         pos = body + len + (len & 1);
138:     }
139: 
140:     const auto records = [] (const Span& s, std::size_t rec) { return s.data != nullptr && s.size % rec == 0 ? s.size / rec : 0; };
141:     if (records (c.phdr, 38) < 2 || records (c.pbag, 4) < 1 || records (c.pgen, 4) < 1 || records (c.inst, 22) < 2
142:         || records (c.ibag, 4) < 1 || records (c.igen, 4) < 1 || records (c.shdr, 46) < 2)
143:     {
144:         c.error = "incomplete SoundFont (preset data missing)";
145:         return c;
146:     }
147:     if (c.smpl.data == nullptr || c.smpl.size < 2)
148:     {
149:         c.error = "SoundFont without sample data";
150:         return c;
151:     }
152:     // the hydra's indices: every bag / generator range in bounds and in order
153:     const auto check = [] (const Span& owners, std::size_t rec, std::size_t at, std::size_t children) {
154:         const auto n = owners.size / rec;
155:         std::size_t last = 0;
156:         for (std::size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i)
157:         {
158:             const auto idx = (std::size_t) u16 (owners.data + i * rec + at);
159:             if (idx < last || idx > children)
160:                 return false;
161:             last = idx;
162:         }
163:         return true;
164:     };
165:     if (! check (c.phdr, 38, 24, c.pbag.size / 4 - 1) || ! check (c.pbag, 4, 0, c.pgen.size / 4) || ! check (c.inst, 22, 20, c.ibag.size / 4 - 1)
166:         || ! check (c.ibag, 4, 0, c.igen.size / 4))
167:     {
168:         c.error = "damaged SoundFont (preset data out of range)";
169:         return c;
170:     }
171:     c.ok = true;
172:     return c;
173: }
174: 
175: FontInfo infoOf (const Chunks& c)
176: {
177:     FontInfo info;
178:     if (! c.ok)
179:         return info;
180:     if (c.inam.data != nullptr)
181:         for (std::size_t i = 0; i < c.inam.size && c.inam.data[i] != 0 && i < 256; ++i)
182:             info.name.push_back ((char) c.inam.data[i]);
183:     const auto n = c.phdr.size / 38;
184:     for (std::size_t i = 0; i + 1 < n; ++i)
185:     {
186:         const auto* r = c.phdr.data + i * 38;
187:         info.presets.push_back ({ clean20 (r), (int) u16 (r + 22), (int) u16 (r + 20) });
188:     }
189:     info.valid = true;
190:     return info;
191: }
192: 
193: template <typename T, std::size_t Rec>
194: T* readArray (const Span& s, int& count, void (*read) (T*, const std::uint8_t*))
195: {
196:     count = (int) (s.size / Rec);
197:     auto* out = (T*) std::calloc ((std::size_t) std::max (1, count), sizeof (T));
198:     if (out != nullptr)
199:         for (int i = 0; i < count; ++i)
200:             read (out + i, s.data + (std::size_t) i * Rec);
201:     return out;
202: }
203: 
204: void rdPhdr (tsf_hydra_phdr* o, const std::uint8_t* p)
205: {
206:     std::memcpy (o->presetName, p, 20);
207:     o->preset = u16 (p + 20), o->bank = u16 (p + 22), o->presetBagNdx = u16 (p + 24);
208:     o->library = u32 (p + 26), o->genre = u32 (p + 30), o->morphology = u32 (p + 34);
209: }
210: void rdBag (tsf_hydra_pbag* o, const std::uint8_t* p) { o->genNdx = u16 (p), o->modNdx = u16 (p + 2); }
211: void rdIbag (tsf_hydra_ibag* o, const std::uint8_t* p) { o->instGenNdx = u16 (p), o->instModNdx = u16 (p + 2); }
212: void rdPmod (tsf_hydra_pmod* o, const std::uint8_t* p)
213: {
214:     o->modSrcOper = u16 (p), o->modDestOper = u16 (p + 2), o->modAmount = (std::int16_t) u16 (p + 4), o->modAmtSrcOper = u16 (p + 6), o->modTransOper = u16 (p + 8);
215: }
216: void rdImod (tsf_hydra_imod* o, const std::uint8_t* p)
217: {
218:     o->modSrcOper = u16 (p), o->modDestOper = u16 (p + 2), o->modAmount = (std::int16_t) u16 (p + 4), o->modAmtSrcOper = u16 (p + 6), o->modTransOper = u16 (p + 8);
219: }
220: void rdPgen (tsf_hydra_pgen* o, const std::uint8_t* p) { o->genOper = u16 (p), o->genAmount.wordAmount = u16 (p + 2); }
221: void rdIgen (tsf_hydra_igen* o, const std::uint8_t* p) { o->genOper = u16 (p), o->genAmount.wordAmount = u16 (p + 2); }
222: void rdInst (tsf_hydra_inst* o, const std::uint8_t* p) { std::memcpy (o->instName, p, 20), o->instBagNdx = u16 (p + 20); }
223: void rdShdr (tsf_hydra_shdr* o, const std::uint8_t* p)
224: {
225:     std::memcpy (o->sampleName, p, 20);
226:     o->start = u32 (p + 20), o->end = u32 (p + 24), o->startLoop = u32 (p + 28), o->endLoop = u32 (p + 32), o->sampleRate = u32 (p + 36);
227:     o->originalPitch = p[40], o->pitchCorrection = (std::int8_t) p[41], o->sampleLink = u16 (p + 42), o->sampleType = u16 (p + 44);
228: }
229: 
230: struct Hydra
231: {
232:     tsf_hydra h {};
233:     ~Hydra()
234:     {
235:         std::free (h.phdrs), std::free (h.pbags), std::free (h.pmods), std::free (h.pgens), std::free (h.insts);
236:         std::free (h.ibags), std::free (h.imods), std::free (h.igens), std::free (h.shdrs);
237:     }
238: };
239: 
240: float timecentsOrSeconds (float v) noexcept { return std::isfinite (v) ? v : 0.0f; }
241: 
242: Envelope envelopeOf (const tsf_envelope& e)
243: {
244:     Envelope o;
245:     o.delay = timecentsOrSeconds (e.delay), o.attack = timecentsOrSeconds (e.attack), o.hold = timecentsOrSeconds (e.hold);
246:     o.decay = timecentsOrSeconds (e.decay), o.sustain = timecentsOrSeconds (e.sustain), o.release = timecentsOrSeconds (e.release);
247:     o.keynumToHold = e.keynumToHold, o.keynumToDecay = e.keynumToDecay;
248:     return o;
249: }
250: } // namespace
251: 
252: std::shared_ptr<const Font> parseFont (const std::vector<std::uint8_t>& bytes, std::string* error)
253: {
254:     const auto fail = [error] (const std::string& why) -> std::shared_ptr<const Font> {
255:         if (error != nullptr)
256:             *error = why;
257:         return nullptr;
258:     };
259:     const auto c = locate (bytes.data(), bytes.size(), true);
260:     if (! c.ok)
261:         return fail (c.error);
262: 
263:     Hydra hy;
264:     auto& h = hy.h;
265:     h.phdrs = readArray<tsf_hydra_phdr, 38> (c.phdr, h.phdrNum, rdPhdr);
266:     h.pbags = readArray<tsf_hydra_pbag, 4> (c.pbag, h.pbagNum, rdBag);
267:     h.pmods = readArray<tsf_hydra_pmod, 10> (c.pmod, h.pmodNum, rdPmod);
268:     h.pgens = readArray<tsf_hydra_pgen, 4> (c.pgen, h.pgenNum, rdPgen);
269:     h.insts = readArray<tsf_hydra_inst, 22> (c.inst, h.instNum, rdInst);
270:     h.ibags = readArray<tsf_hydra_ibag, 4> (c.ibag, h.ibagNum, rdIbag);
271:     h.imods = readArray<tsf_hydra_imod, 10> (c.imod, h.imodNum, rdImod);
272:     h.igens = readArray<tsf_hydra_igen, 4> (c.igen, h.igenNum, rdIgen);
273:     h.shdrs = readArray<tsf_hydra_shdr, 46> (c.shdr, h.shdrNum, rdShdr);
274:     if (h.phdrs == nullptr || h.pbags == nullptr || h.pmods == nullptr || h.pgens == nullptr || h.insts == nullptr || h.ibags == nullptr
275:         || h.imods == nullptr || h.igens == nullptr || h.shdrs == nullptr)
276:         return fail ("out of memory");
277: 
278:     // references TinySoundFont follows without checking: neutralise the ones out of range
279:     for (int i = 0; i < h.pgenNum; ++i)
280:         if (h.pgens[i].genOper == 41 && h.pgens[i].genAmount.wordAmount >= h.instNum - 1)
281:             h.pgens[i].genOper = 14; // (an unused generator)
282:     for (int i = 0; i < h.igenNum; ++i)
283:         if (h.igens[i].genOper == 53 && h.igens[i].genAmount.wordAmount >= h.shdrNum - 1)
284:             h.igens[i].genOper = 14;
285: 
286:     auto font = std::make_shared<Font>();
287:     if (c.inam.data != nullptr)
288:         for (std::size_t i = 0; i < c.inam.size && c.inam.data[i] != 0 && i < 256; ++i)
289:             font->name.push_back ((char) c.inam.data[i]);
290: 
291:     bool compressed = false;
292:     for (int i = 0; i + 1 < h.shdrNum; ++i)
293:         if ((h.shdrs[i].sampleType & 0x30) != 0)
294:             compressed = true;
295: 
296:     std::uint32_t frames = 0;
297:     if (! compressed)
298:     {
299:         frames = (std::uint32_t) (c.smpl.size / 2);
300:         font->samples.resize ((std::size_t) frames + 8, 0);
301:         for (std::uint32_t i = 0; i < frames; ++i)
302:             font->samples[i] = (std::int16_t) u16 (c.smpl.data + 2 * (std::size_t) i);
303:     }
304:     else
305:     {
306:         // SF3: every compressed sample's Ogg stream must lie inside the sample chunk (the terminal record too:
307:         // TinySoundFont's decoder reads every record, so an out-of-range one would be read past the chunk)
308:         for (int i = 0; i < h.shdrNum; ++i)
309:             if ((h.shdrs[i].sampleType & 0x30) != 0 && (h.shdrs[i].start > h.shdrs[i].end || h.shdrs[i].end > c.smpl.size))
310:                 h.shdrs[i].start = h.shdrs[i].end = h.shdrs[i].startLoop = h.shdrs[i].endLoop = 0;
311:         float* decoded = nullptr;
312:         unsigned int count = (unsigned int) c.smpl.size;
313:         if (! tsf_decode_sf3_samples (c.smpl.data, &decoded, &count, &h))
314:         {
315:             std::free (decoded);
316:             return fail ("could not decode the SF3 samples");
317:         }
318:         frames = count;
319:         font->samples.resize ((std::size_t) frames + 8, 0);
320:         for (std::uint32_t i = 0; i < frames; ++i)
321:             font->samples[i] = (std::int16_t) std::lrint (std::clamp (decoded[i], -1.0f, 1.0f) * 32767.0f);
322:         std::free (decoded);
323:     }
324: 
325:     tsf parsed;
326:     std::memset (&parsed, 0, sizeof (parsed));
327:     if (! tsf_load_presets (&parsed, &h, frames))
328:         return fail ("out of memory");
329: 
330:     font->presets.reserve ((std::size_t) parsed.presetNum);
331:     for (int p = 0; p < parsed.presetNum; ++p)
332:     {
333:         const auto& src = parsed.presets[p];
334:         Preset preset;
335:         preset.name = clean20 ((const std::uint8_t*) src.presetName);
336:         preset.bank = src.bank, preset.program = src.preset;
337:         for (int r = 0; r < src.regionNum; ++r)
338:         {
339:             const auto& s = src.regions[r];
340:             Region o;
341:             o.loKey = s.lokey, o.hiKey = s.hikey, o.loVel = s.lovel, o.hiVel = s.hivel;
342:             if (o.loKey > o.hiKey || o.loVel > o.hiVel)
343:                 continue;
344:             o.loop = s.loop_mode == TSF_LOOPMODE_CONTINUOUS ? LoopMode::continuous : s.loop_mode == TSF_LOOPMODE_SUSTAIN ? LoopMode::untilRelease : LoopMode::none;
345:             o.exclusiveClass = s.group;
346:             o.start = std::min (s.offset, frames);
347:             o.end = std::min (std::max (s.end, o.start), frames);
348:             if (o.end <= o.start + 1)
349:                 continue;
350:             o.loopStart = s.loop_start;
351:             o.loopEnd = s.loop_end + 1; // (TinySoundFont keeps the last frame of the loop)
352:             if (o.loopStart < o.start || o.loopEnd > o.end || o.loopEnd < o.loopStart + 2)
353:                 o.loop = LoopMode::none, o.loopStart = o.start, o.loopEnd = o.end;
354:             o.sampleRate = s.sample_rate >= 400 && s.sample_rate <= 400000 ? s.sample_rate : 44100;
355:             o.rootKey = std::clamp (s.pitch_keycenter, 0, 127);
356:             o.keyTrack = std::clamp (s.pitch_keytrack, 0, 1200);
357:             o.transpose = std::clamp (s.transpose, -120, 120), o.tune = std::clamp (s.tune, -9999, 9999);
358:             o.attenuation = std::clamp (s.attenuation * 100.0f, 0.0f, 1440.0f); // (TinySoundFont scales it by 0.01)
359:             o.pan = std::clamp (s.pan, -0.5f, 0.5f);
360:             o.ampEnv = envelopeOf (s.ampenv), o.modEnv = envelopeOf (s.modenv);
361:             o.filterFc = s.initialFilterFc, o.filterQ = s.initialFilterQ;
362:             o.modEnvToPitch = s.modEnvToPitch, o.modEnvToFilterFc = s.modEnvToFilterFc, o.modLfoToFilterFc = s.modLfoToFilterFc;
363:             o.modLfoToVolume = s.modLfoToVolume;
364:             o.delayModLfo = timecentsOrSeconds (s.delayModLFO), o.delayVibLfo = timecentsOrSeconds (s.delayVibLFO);
365:             o.freqModLfo = s.freqModLFO, o.freqVibLfo = s.freqVibLFO, o.modLfoToPitch = s.modLfoToPitch, o.vibLfoToPitch = s.vibLfoToPitch;
366:             o.chorusSend = std::clamp (s.chorusSend / 1000.0f, 0.0f, 1.0f), o.reverbSend = std::clamp (s.reverbSend / 1000.0f, 0.0f, 1.0f);
367:             preset.regions.push_back (o);
368:         }
369:         font->presets.push_back (std::move (preset));
370:     }
371:     for (int p = 0; p < parsed.presetNum; ++p)
372:         std::free (parsed.presets[p].regions);
373:     std::free (parsed.presets);
374: 
375:     if (font->presets.empty())
376:         return fail ("SoundFont without presets");
377:     return font;
378: }
379: 
380: std::shared_ptr<const Font> loadFontFile (const std::string& path, std::string* error)
381: {
382:     if (sfz::isSfzPath (path)) // an SFZ instrument (round 167o): its regions and samples as a one-preset font
383:         return sfz::loadFile (path, error);
384:     std::ifstream in (std::filesystem::path ((const char8_t*) path.c_str()), std::ios::binary);
385:     if (! in)
386:     {
387:         if (error != nullptr)
388:             *error = "file not found";
389:         return nullptr;
390:     }
391:     in.seekg (0, std::ios::end);
392:     const auto size = (std::size_t) std::max<std::streamoff> (0, in.tellg());
393:     in.seekg (0);
394:     std::vector<std::uint8_t> bytes;
395:     try
396:     {
397:         bytes.resize (size);
398:     }
399:     catch (const std::bad_alloc&)
400:     {
401:         if (error != nullptr)
402:             *error = "out of memory";
403:         return nullptr;
404:     }
405:     in.read ((char*) bytes.data(), (std::streamsize) size);
406:     if ((std::size_t) in.gcount() != size)
407:     {
408:         if (error != nullptr)
409:             *error = "could not read the file";
410:         return nullptr;
411:     }
412:     std::shared_ptr<const Font> font;
413:     try
414:     {
415:         font = parseFont (bytes, error);
416:     }
417:     catch (const std::bad_alloc&)
418:     {
419:         if (error != nullptr)
420:             *error = "out of memory";
421:         return nullptr;
422:     }
423:     if (font != nullptr)
424:     {
425:         auto f = std::const_pointer_cast<Font> (font);
426:         f->path = path;
427:         if (f->name.empty())
428:             f->name = std::filesystem::path ((const char8_t*) path.c_str()).stem().string();
429:     }
430:     return font;
431: }
432: 
433: FontInfo scanFont (const std::vector<std::uint8_t>& bytes)
434: {
435:     return infoOf (locate (bytes.data(), bytes.size(), true));
436: }
437: 
438: FontInfo scanFontFile (const std::string& path)
439: {
440:     if (sfz::isSfzPath (path))
441:         return sfz::scanFile (path);
442:     // reads the chunk headers and every chunk but the sample data
443:     std::ifstream in (std::filesystem::path ((const char8_t*) path.c_str()), std::ios::binary);
444:     if (! in)
445:         return {};
446:     in.seekg (0, std::ios::end);
447:     const auto fileSize = (std::uint64_t) std::max<std::streamoff> (0, in.tellg());
448:     in.seekg (0);
449:     std::uint8_t head[12];
450:     if (fileSize < 12 || ! in.read ((char*) head, 12) || std::memcmp (head, "RIFF", 4) != 0 || std::memcmp (head + 8, "sfbk", 4) != 0)
451:         return {};
452:     // a copy of the file with the sample chunk's contents left out (its header kept, its size intact)
453:     std::vector<std::uint8_t> bytes (head, head + 12);
454:     std::uint64_t pos = 12;
455:     while (pos + 12 <= fileSize && bytes.size() < (64u << 20))
456:     {
457:         std::uint8_t h[12];
458:         in.seekg ((std::streamoff) pos);
459:         if (! in.read ((char*) h, 12))
460:             break;
461:         const auto len = (std::uint64_t) u32 (h + 4);
462:         if (std::memcmp (h, "LIST", 4) == 0 && std::memcmp (h + 8, "sdta", 4) == 0)
463:         {
464:             // keep only the sub-chunk headers
465:             bytes.insert (bytes.end(), h, h + 12);
466:             const auto listAt = bytes.size() - 12;
467:             std::uint64_t p = pos + 12;
468:             const auto end = std::min (fileSize, pos + 8 + len);
469:             while (p + 8 <= end)
470:             {
471:                 std::uint8_t s[8];
472:                 in.seekg ((std::streamoff) p);
473:                 if (! in.read ((char*) s, 8))
474:                     break;
475:                 bytes.insert (bytes.end(), s, s + 8);
476:                 const auto slen = (std::uint64_t) u32 (s + 4);
477:                 p += 8 + slen + (slen & 1);
478:             }
479:             // the LIST's own size now covers only what was copied (the sample sizes stay in their headers)
480:             const auto copied = (std::uint32_t) (bytes.size() - listAt - 8);
481:             for (int k = 0; k < 4; ++k)
482:                 bytes[listAt + 4 + (std::size_t) k] = (std::uint8_t) (copied >> (8 * k));
483:         }
484:         else
485:         {
486:             const auto take = std::min<std::uint64_t> (8 + len, fileSize - pos);
487:             if (take > (64u << 20))
488:                 break;
489:             const auto at = bytes.size();
490:             bytes.resize (at + (std::size_t) take);
491:             in.seekg ((std::streamoff) pos);
492:             in.read ((char*) bytes.data() + at, (std::streamsize) take);
493:             if (len & 1)
494:                 bytes.push_back (0);
495:         }
496:         pos += 8 + len + (len & 1);
497:     }
498:     // the RIFF size of the shortened copy
499:     const auto riff = (std::uint32_t) (bytes.size() - 8);
500:     for (int k = 0; k < 4; ++k)
501:         bytes[4 + (std::size_t) k] = (std::uint8_t) (riff >> (8 * k));
502:     // the sample chunk keeps its real size (its data was not read); locate() only checks it is there
503:     return infoOf (locate (bytes.data(), bytes.size(), false));
504: }
505: 
506: // --- a tiny generated SoundFont (tests, demo) -------------------------------------------------------------------------
507: 
508: namespace
509: {
510: struct Writer
511: {
512:     std::vector<std::uint8_t> b;
513:     void bytes (const void* p, std::size_t n) { b.insert (b.end(), (const std::uint8_t*) p, (const std::uint8_t*) p + n); }
514:     void w16 (std::uint32_t v) { b.push_back ((std::uint8_t) v), b.push_back ((std::uint8_t) (v >> 8)); }
515:     void w32 (std::uint32_t v) { w16 (v & 0xffff), w16 (v >> 16); }
516:     void id (const char* s) { bytes (s, 4); }
517:     void name20 (const std::string& s)
518:     {
519:         char n[20] {};
520:         std::memcpy (n, s.data(), std::min<std::size_t> (s.size(), 19));
521:         bytes (n, 20);
522:     }
523:     void chunk (const char* cid, const std::vector<std::uint8_t>& body)
524:     {
525:         id (cid), w32 ((std::uint32_t) body.size()), bytes (body.data(), body.size());
526:         if (body.size() & 1)
527:             b.push_back (0);
528:     }
529: };
530: } // namespace
531: 
532: std::vector<std::uint8_t> makeTestFont (const std::vector<TestPreset>& presets, int layers, int sampleRate)
533: {
534:     layers = std::clamp (layers, 1, 4);
535:     // samples: per preset, per key zone (low / high), per layer: 0.25 s of a sine whose amplitude encodes the layer,
536:     // with a loop over whole periods
537:     struct S { std::uint32_t start, end, loopStart, loopEnd; int root; };
538:     std::vector<std::int16_t> pcm;
539:     std::vector<S> shdrs;
540:     std::vector<std::string> sampleNames;
541:     const int len = sampleRate / 4;
542:     for (std::size_t p = 0; p < presets.size(); ++p)
543:         for (int zone = 0; zone < 2; ++zone)
544:             for (int layer = 0; layer < layers; ++layer)
545:             {
546:                 const int root = zone == 0 ? 48 : 72;
547:                 const double hz = 440.0 * std::pow (2.0, (root - 69) / 12.0);
548:                 const double amp = (0.2 + 0.6 * (layer + 1) / layers) / (1.0 + (double) p); // louder layer = higher velocity; preset p at 1/(1+p)
549:                 S s;
550:                 s.start = (std::uint32_t) pcm.size();
551:                 for (int i = 0; i < len; ++i)
552:                     pcm.push_back ((std::int16_t) std::lrint (32767.0 * amp * std::sin (2.0 * 3.14159265358979 * hz * i / sampleRate)));
553:                 s.end = (std::uint32_t) pcm.size();
554:                 const auto period = (double) sampleRate / hz;
555:                 const auto periods = std::floor ((len * 0.5) / period);
556:                 s.loopStart = s.start + (std::uint32_t) std::lrint (period * 4);
557:                 s.loopEnd = s.loopStart + (std::uint32_t) std::lrint (period * periods);
558:                 s.root = root;
559:                 for (int i = 0; i < 46; ++i)
560:                     pcm.push_back (0); // SF2: 46 zero frames after each sample
561:                 shdrs.push_back (s);
562:                 sampleNames.push_back ("s" + std::to_string (shdrs.size()));
563:             }
564: 
565:     Writer pdta;
566:     pdta.id ("pdta");
567:     // instruments: one per preset; zones = 2 keys × layers velocity
568:     Writer phdr, pbag, pmod, pgen, inst, ibag, imod, igen, shdr;
569:     int pgenCount = 0, igenCount = 0, ibagCount = 0;
570:     const auto gen = [] (Writer& w, int op, int amount) { w.w16 ((std::uint32_t) op), w.w16 ((std::uint32_t) (amount & 0xffff)); };
571:     const auto range = [] (Writer& w, int op, int lo, int hi) { w.w16 ((std::uint32_t) op), w.b.push_back ((std::uint8_t) lo), w.b.push_back ((std::uint8_t) hi); };
572:     for (std::size_t p = 0; p < presets.size(); ++p)
573:     {
574:         phdr.name20 (presets[p].name), phdr.w16 ((std::uint32_t) presets[p].program), phdr.w16 ((std::uint32_t) presets[p].bank), phdr.w16 ((std::uint32_t) p);
575:         phdr.w32 (0), phdr.w32 (0), phdr.w32 (0);
576:         pbag.w16 ((std::uint32_t) pgenCount), pbag.w16 (0);
577:         gen (pgen, 41, (int) p), ++pgenCount;
578:         inst.name20 ("i" + std::to_string (p)), inst.w16 ((std::uint32_t) ibagCount);
579:         for (int zone = 0; zone < 2; ++zone)
580:             for (int layer = 0; layer < layers; ++layer)
581:             {
582:                 ibag.w16 ((std::uint32_t) igenCount), ibag.w16 (0), ++ibagCount;
583:                 range (igen, 43, zone == 0 ? 0 : 60, zone == 0 ? 59 : 127), ++igenCount;
584:                 range (igen, 44, layer * 128 / layers, (layer + 1) * 128 / layers - 1), ++igenCount;
585:                 gen (igen, 34, -7973), ++igenCount; // attack 10 ms
586:                 gen (igen, 38, -3986), ++igenCount; // release 100 ms
587:                 if (presets[p].exclusiveClass != 0)
588:                     gen (igen, 57, presets[p].exclusiveClass), ++igenCount;
589:                 gen (igen, 54, presets[p].loop ? 1 : 0), ++igenCount;
590:                 gen (igen, 53, (int) (p * 2 * (std::size_t) layers + (std::size_t) zone * (std::size_t) layers + (std::size_t) layer)), ++igenCount;
591:             }
592:     }
593:     phdr.name20 ("EOP"), phdr.w16 (0), phdr.w16 (0), phdr.w16 ((std::uint32_t) presets.size()), phdr.w32 (0), phdr.w32 (0), phdr.w32 (0);
594:     pbag.w16 ((std::uint32_t) pgenCount), pbag.w16 (0);
595:     pmod.b.assign (10, 0);
596:     gen (pgen, 0, 0);
597:     inst.name20 ("EOI"), inst.w16 ((std::uint32_t) ibagCount);
598:     ibag.w16 ((std::uint32_t) igenCount), ibag.w16 (0);
599:     imod.b.assign (10, 0);
600:     gen (igen, 0, 0);
601:     for (std::size_t i = 0; i < shdrs.size(); ++i)
602:     {
603:         const auto& s = shdrs[i];
604:         shdr.name20 (sampleNames[i]), shdr.w32 (s.start), shdr.w32 (s.end), shdr.w32 (s.loopStart), shdr.w32 (s.loopEnd);
605:         shdr.w32 ((std::uint32_t) sampleRate), shdr.b.push_back ((std::uint8_t) s.root), shdr.b.push_back (0), shdr.w16 (0), shdr.w16 (1);
606:     }
607:     shdr.name20 ("EOS"), shdr.w32 (0), shdr.w32 (0), shdr.w32 (0), shdr.w32 (0), shdr.w32 (0), shdr.b.push_back (0), shdr.b.push_back (0), shdr.w16 (0), shdr.w16 (0);
608:     pdta.chunk ("phdr", phdr.b), pdta.chunk ("pbag", pbag.b), pdta.chunk ("pmod", pmod.b), pdta.chunk ("pgen", pgen.b);
609:     pdta.chunk ("inst", inst.b), pdta.chunk ("ibag", ibag.b), pdta.chunk ("imod", imod.b), pdta.chunk ("igen", igen.b), pdta.chunk ("shdr", shdr.b);
610: 
611:     Writer info;
612:     info.id ("INFO");
613:     {
614:         Writer v;
615:         v.w16 (2), v.w16 (1);
616:         info.chunk ("ifil", v.b);
617:         const std::string n = "LYNX test font";
618:         std::vector<std::uint8_t> z (n.begin(), n.end());
619:         z.push_back (0);
620:         info.chunk ("INAM", z);
621:     }
622:     Writer sdta;
623:     sdta.id ("sdta");
624:     {
625:         std::vector<std::uint8_t> raw (pcm.size() * 2);
626:         for (std::size_t i = 0; i < pcm.size(); ++i)
627:             raw[2 * i] = (std::uint8_t) (pcm[i] & 0xff), raw[2 * i + 1] = (std::uint8_t) ((std::uint16_t) pcm[i] >> 8);
628:         sdta.chunk ("smpl", raw);
629:     }
630: 
631:     Writer out;
632:     out.id ("RIFF");
633:     out.w32 ((std::uint32_t) (4 + 8 + info.b.size() + 8 + sdta.b.size() + 8 + pdta.b.size()));
634:     out.id ("sfbk");
635:     out.chunk ("LIST", info.b);
636:     out.chunk ("LIST", sdta.b);
637:     out.chunk ("LIST", pdta.b);
638:     return out.b;
639: }
640: } // namespace lynx::sf


=== src/core/Sfz.h ===
1: #pragma once
2: 
3: // SFZ instruments (round 167o, issue #22; https://sfzformat.com): an .sfz file is read into a LYNX Soundfont font
4: // (core/SoundFont.h) and played by its voices. FL Studio loads SFZ through DirectWave (its import list has .sfz,
5: // docs/analise/REA_rodada167o.md); in LYNX SHARE an .sfz opens as a channel of LYNX's own sampler-player.
6: //
7: // Supported: the headers <control> <global> <master> <group> <region> with their inheritance, comments, #define and
8: // #include, default_path / note_offset / octave_offset, note names (c4 = 60), and the opcodes
9: //   sample (WAV, and what the app's decoder reads: FLAC, OGG, AIFF, MP3…), lokey / hikey / key, lovel / hivel,
10: //   pitch_keycenter, pitch_keytrack, transpose, tune / pitch, volume / gain, amplitude, pan, offset, end,
11: //   loop_mode / loopmode (no_loop, one_shot, loop_continuous, loop_sustain), loop_start / loop_end (and the v1
12: //   loopstart / loopend; the sample's own smpl loop otherwise), ampeg_delay / attack / hold / decay / sustain /
13: //   release, amp_veltrack, fil_type (low-pass) / cutoff / resonance, fileg_* and pitcheg_* (one modulation envelope),
14: //   amplfo_*, fillfo_*, pitchlfo_* (delay, freq, depth), group / off_by / off_mode, seq_length / seq_position,
15: //   lorand / hirand, trigger (attack, release), sw_lokey / sw_hikey / sw_last / sw_default, delay.
16: // Unknown opcodes and headers are skipped (counted in the report).
17: 
18: #include <cstdint>
19: #include <functional>
20: #include <map>
21: #include <memory>
22: #include <optional>
23: #include <string>
24: #include <vector>
25: 
26: #include "core/SoundFont.h"
27: 
28: namespace lynx::sfz
29: {
30: // One region after the headers' inheritance (global → master → group → region): its opcodes, the last value wins.
31: struct ParsedRegion
32: {
33:     std::map<std::string, std::string> opcodes;
34:     std::string get (const std::string& name, const std::string& fallback = {}) const;
35: };
36: 
37: struct Parsed
38: {
39:     std::vector<ParsedRegion> regions;
40:     std::map<std::string, std::string> control; // <control>
41:     int unknownHeaders = 0;
42: };
43: 
44: // Parses SFZ text. `readInclude` returns the text of an #include'd file (relative to the .sfz's folder), or nullopt.
45: Parsed parse (const std::string& text, const std::function<std::optional<std::string> (const std::string&)>& readInclude = {});
46: 
47: // "c4" → 60, "c#4" / "db4" → 61, "60" → 60; -1 when unreadable
48: int noteNumber (const std::string& text);
49: 
50: // A decoded sample file: channels of floats, its rate, the loop in its smpl chunk (-1: none)
51: struct Audio
52: {
53:     std::vector<std::vector<float>> channels;
54:     double sampleRate = 44100.0;
55:     std::int64_t loopStart = -1, loopEnd = -1; // [start, end)

27: std::string lower (std::string s)
28: {
29:     for (auto& c : s)
30:         if (c >= 'A' && c <= 'Z')
31:             c = (char) (c - 'A' + 'a');
32:     return s;
33: }

35: std::string trim (const std::string& s)
36: {
37:     const auto a = s.find_first_not_of (" \t\r\n");
38:     if (a == std::string::npos)
39:         return {};
40:     const auto b = s.find_last_not_of (" \t\r\n");
41:     return s.substr (a, b - a + 1);
42: }
43:
