62: 
63: // comments out (// to the end of the line, /* … */), then #include and #define expanded, line by line
64: std::string preprocess (const std::string& text, const std::function<std::optional<std::string> (const std::string&)>& readInclude,
65:                         std::vector<std::pair<std::string, std::string>>& defines, int depth)
66: {
67:     std::string noComments;
68:     noComments.reserve (text.size());
69:     for (std::size_t i = 0; i < text.size(); ++i)
70:     {
71:         if (text[i] == '/' && i + 1 < text.size() && text[i + 1] == '/')
72:         {
73:             while (i < text.size() && text[i] != '\n')
74:                 ++i;
75:             noComments += '\n';
76:             continue;
77:         }
78:         if (text[i] == '/' && i + 1 < text.size() && text[i + 1] == '*')
79:         {
80:             i += 2;
81:             while (i + 1 < text.size() && ! (text[i] == '*' && text[i + 1] == '/'))
82:             {
83:                 if (text[i] == '\n')
84:                     noComments += '\n';
85:                 ++i;
86:             }
87:             ++i;
88:             noComments += ' ';
89:             continue;
90:         }
91:         noComments += text[i];
92:     }
93: 
94:     std::string out;
95:     std::istringstream lines (noComments);
96:     std::string line;
97:     while (std::getline (lines, line))
98:     {
99:         // $NAME → its #define'd value (longest names first, so $A doesn't eat $AB)
100:         if (line.find ('$') != std::string::npos)
101:             for (const auto& [name, value] : defines)
102:                 for (std::size_t at = line.find (name); at != std::string::npos; at = line.find (name, at + value.size()))
103:                     line.replace (at, name.size(), value);
104:         const auto t = trim (line);
105:         if (t.rfind ("#define", 0) == 0)
106:         {
107:             std::istringstream words (t.substr (7));
108:             std::string name, value;
109:             words >> name;
110:             std::getline (words, value);
111:             value = trim (value);
112:             if (! name.empty() && name[0] == '$')
113:             {
114:                 defines.erase (std::remove_if (defines.begin(), defines.end(), [&] (const auto& d) { return d.first == name; }), defines.end());
115:                 defines.push_back ({ name, value });
116:                 std::sort (defines.begin(), defines.end(), [] (const auto& a, const auto& b) { return a.first.size() > b.first.size(); });
117:             }
118:             continue;
119:         }
120:         if (t.rfind ("#include", 0) == 0)
121:         {
122:             const auto q1 = t.find ('"'), q2 = t.rfind ('"');
123:             if (q1 != std::string::npos && q2 > q1 && readInclude && depth < 16)
124:                 if (auto included = readInclude (t.substr (q1 + 1, q2 - q1 - 1)))
125:                     out += preprocess (*included, readInclude, defines, depth + 1) + "\n";
126:             continue;
127:         }
128:         out += line;
129:         out += '\n';
130:     }
131:     return out;
132: }
133: 
134: float toFloat (const std::string& s, float fallback)
135: {
136:     try
137:     {
138:         std::size_t used = 0;
139:         const auto v = std::stof (s, &used);
140:         return used > 0 ? v : fallback;
141:     }
142:     catch (...)
143:     {
144:         return fallback;
145:     }
146: }
147: 
148: int toInt (const std::string& s, int fallback)
149: {
150:     try
151:     {
152:         std::size_t used = 0;
153:         const auto v = std::stol (s, &used);
154:         return used > 0 ? (int) v : fallback;
155:     }
156:     catch (...)
157:     {
158:         return fallback;
159:     }
160: }

171: int noteNumber (const std::string& textIn)
172: {
173:     const auto text = lower (trim (textIn));
174:     if (text.empty())
175:         return -1;
176:     if ((text[0] >= '0' && text[0] <= '9') || text[0] == '-')
177:         return toInt (text, -1);
178:     static const int base[] = { 9, 11, 0, 2, 4, 5, 7 }; // a b c d e f g
179:     if (text[0] < 'a' || text[0] > 'g')
180:         return -1;
181:     int n = base[text[0] - 'a'];
182:     std::size_t i = 1;
183:     if (i < text.size() && text[i] == '#')
184:         ++n, ++i;
185:     else if (i < text.size() && text[i] == 'b' && i + 1 < text.size() && (text[i + 1] == '-' || (text[i + 1] >= '0' && text[i + 1] <= '9')))
186:         --n, ++i;
187:     if (i >= text.size())
188:         return -1;
189:     const auto octave = toInt (text.substr (i), -100);
190:     if (octave == -100)
191:         return -1;
192:     return n + (octave + 1) * 12; // c4 = 60
193: }
194:

=== src/ui/WindowOwner.h ===
1: #pragma once
2: 
3: #include <functional>
4: 
5: #include <juce_gui_basics/juce_gui_basics.h>
6: 
7: namespace lynx
8: {
9: // Secondary windows (plugin, channel settings, EQ, automation) always stay above the LYNX main window,
10: // without staying above other programs: on Windows, the main window becomes their "owner".
11: void setMainWindowForOwnership (juce::Component* mainWindow);
12: void keepAboveMainWindow (juce::Component& window); // call after the window is visible; sets the LYNX icon
13: // (it stays owned when JUCE recreates the native window later, e.g. setResizable after it is shown)
14: // The main window's native handle (HWND / X11 Window id; nullptr without one): a plugin's window in a separate
15: // process is kept above it (round 162d).
16: void* mainWindowHandle();
17: 
18: // Keys a secondary window does not use (no component in it took them) go to the main window's global shortcuts, as in
19: // FL (round 165q / issue #12: Space, F12 or a user's key did nothing while the Undo history, Settings, a plugin window...
20: // had the focus). keepAboveMainWindow installs the forwarding on the window; the main window sets the handler. A window
21: // that handles every key itself (a detached panel) sets the property "lynxOwnKeys".
22: void setSecondaryWindowKeyHandler (std::function<bool (const juce::KeyPress&)> handler);
23: 
24: // Before a drag (a sample from the Browser...): JUCE finds the drop target in its own list of windows, where the main
25: // window comes first after any click in it, while the windows kept above it (channel settings, a plugin...) are on top
26: // on the screen. This puts those windows back above the main window in that list, in their screen order, so a drop on
27: // one of them lands there and not on the panel under it (issue #12: a sample dropped on the Sampler's waveform
28: // replaced the sample of the Channel Rack row under the window). Runs whenever the main window comes to the front
29: // (setMainWindowForOwnership listens), so also when the drag image hides at the drop and Windows activates the main window.
30: void matchWindowOrderToScreen();
31: 
32: // Makes the main window the active one (round 165q): a closing secondary window that is active calls it first, so
33: // the activation does not go to another program.
34: void activateMainWindow();
35: 
36: // True when `window` is currently owned by the main window (Windows; elsewhere: keepAboveMainWindow was called).
37: bool isOwnedByMainWindow (juce::Component& window);
38: // True when `window` is above the main window in the z-order (Windows; elsewhere: isOwnedByMainWindow).
39: bool isAboveMainWindow (juce::Component& window);
40: 
41: // Keeps a top-level window reachable (round 163, UI zoom 150%+): a resizable window larger than its screen shrinks
42: // to it, and the window moves so its title bar and close button are on the screen. keepAboveMainWindow does it the
43: // first time; the UI scale change does it for every LYNX window.
44: void fitWindowOnScreen (juce::Component& window);
45: void fitAllWindowsOnScreen();
46: 
47: // Round 164: the content of a fixed-size window (a tool window, a dialog) that is taller or wider than the usable
48: // screen (UI zoom 150%+, a small laptop screen) goes into a scrolling viewport that fits, so its buttons stay
49: // reachable; a content that fits is returned as it is. The viewport follows the content's later size changes.
50: std::unique_ptr<juce::Component> fitContentToScreen (std::unique_ptr<juce::Component> content);
51: 
52: // --windowtest only: makes `window` the foreground window even when LYNX was started in the background.
53: void forceForegroundForTest (juce::Component& window);
54: 
55: // Native window chrome in the LYNX colours (dark title bar on Windows). keepAboveMainWindow calls it.
56: void applyWindowChrome (juce::Component& window);
57: 
58: // Floating JUCE editors use a visible, touch-sized JUCE title bar on Android. The Android peer's native
59: // minimise operation is a no-op, so the title-bar minimise button collapses the editor content and can restore it.
60: // On desktop this keeps the existing native title-bar behavior.
61: void configureSecondaryWindowControls (juce::DocumentWindow& window);
62: 
63: // GPU rendering (round 157a). JUCE 8 draws every Windows window with Direct2D (the GPU driver's D3D11 path); a
64: // driver that crashes there (Intel igd10um64xe.dll on a hybrid-graphics laptop) takes LYNX down. Off = every LYNX
65: // window draws with GDI (the CPU), as JUCE 7 did. Saved in gpu-rendering.txt ("0" = off; default on). The change
66: // applies to open windows at once (a watcher re-applies it to new windows twice a second).
67: bool gpuRenderingEnabled();
68: void setGpuRenderingEnabled (bool on);
69: // starts the watcher (the app calls it once at start, after reading the setting)
70: void startRenderingWatch();
71: // the window's native peer uses the chosen renderer now (keepAboveMainWindow calls it)
72: void applyRenderingChoice (juce::Component& window);
73: 
74: // Dialogs with a text field (rename and color, the next empty pattern's name, preset and insert names...) open with
75: // the field focused and its text selected, so typing replaces the name at once, as in FL (round 165q). JUCE's
76: // AlertWindow gives its buttons an explicit focus order, so the dialog's focus went to its first button: the letters
77: // typed did nothing and Enter pressed OK. Installed once for the whole app (a Desktop focus listener): the first time
78: // the focus enters a dialog, it moves to the dialog's first editable text field when it landed anywhere else.
79: void installDialogTextFocus();
80: void removeDialogTextFocus();
81: // The dialog's first editable, visible text field (top to bottom, then left to right), or nullptr.
82: juce::TextEditor* firstTextField (juce::Component& dialog);
83: 
84: // The LYNX logo (256 px, with transparency).
85: juce::Image logoImage();
86: 
87: // The CPU time this process has used so far, in seconds (user + kernel; --editorcpu).
88: double processCpuSeconds();
89: // The CPU time the calling thread has used so far, in seconds (user + kernel; --uiperf: the message thread's share).
90: double threadCpuSeconds();
91: } // namespace lynx

21:  #include <windows.h>
22: #endif
23: 
24: namespace lynx
25: {
26: namespace
27: {
28: juce::Component::SafePointer<juce::Component>& mainWindow()
29: {
30:     static juce::Component::SafePointer<juce::Component> window;
31:     return window;
32: }
33: } // namespace
34: 
35: void* mainWindowHandle()
36: {
37:     auto* main = mainWindow().getComponent();
38:     return main != nullptr && main->getPeer() != nullptr ? main->getWindowHandle() : nullptr;
39: }
40: 
41: namespace
42: {
43: // Every time the main window comes to the front (a click in it, the drag image hiding at a drop...), JUCE moves it to
44: // the top of its list; the windows kept above it go back above it there (matchWindowOrderToScreen, issue #12)
45: struct OrderKeeper final : juce::ComponentListener
46: {
47:     void componentBroughtToFront (juce::Component&) override { matchWindowOrderToScreen(); }
48: };
49: 
50: OrderKeeper& orderKeeper()
51: {
52:     static OrderKeeper keeper;
53:     return keeper;
54: }
55: } // namespace
56: 
57: void setMainWindowForOwnership (juce::Component* window)
58: {
59:     if (auto* before = mainWindow().getComponent())
60:         before->removeComponentListener (&orderKeeper());
61:     mainWindow() = window;
62:     if (window != nullptr)
63:         window->addComponentListener (&orderKeeper());
64: }
65: 
66: void activateMainWindow()
67: {
68:     auto* main = mainWindow().getComponent();
69:     if (main == nullptr || ! main->isShowing())
70:         return;
71:     auto* top = main->getTopLevelComponent();
72:     if (auto* peer = top->getPeer(); peer != nullptr && ! peer->isMinimised())
73:         top->toFront (true);
74: }
75: 
76: double processCpuSeconds()
77: {
78:    #if JUCE_WINDOWS
79:     FILETIME created {}, exited {}, kernel {}, user {};
80:     if (GetProcessTimes (GetCurrentProcess(), &created, &exited, &kernel, &user))
81:         return (double) ((((std::uint64_t) kernel.dwHighDateTime << 32) | kernel.dwLowDateTime) + (((std::uint64_t) user.dwHighDateTime << 32) | user.dwLowDateTime)) * 1e-7;
82:     return 0.0;
83:    #else
84:     return (double) std::clock() / CLOCKS_PER_SEC;
85:    #endif
86: }
87: 
88: double threadCpuSeconds()
89: {
90:    #if JUCE_WINDOWS
91:     FILETIME created {}, exited {}, kernel {}, user {};
92:     if (GetThreadTimes (GetCurrentThread(), &created, &exited, &kernel, &user))
93:         return (double) ((((std::uint64_t) kernel.dwHighDateTime << 32) | kernel.dwLowDateTime) + (((std::uint64_t) user.dwHighDateTime << 32) | user.dwLowDateTime)) * 1e-7;
94:     return 0.0;
95:    #elif defined (CLOCK_THREAD_CPUTIME_ID)
96:     timespec t {};
97:     return clock_gettime (CLOCK_THREAD_CPUTIME_ID, &t) == 0 ? (double) t.tv_sec + (double) t.tv_nsec * 1e-9 : 0.0;
98:    #else
99:     return 0.0;
100:    #endif
101: }
102: 
103: juce::TextEditor* firstTextField (juce::Component& dialog)
104: {
105:     juce::TextEditor* best = nullptr;
106:     juce::Point<int> bestAt;
107:     std::function<void (juce::Component&)> walk = [&] (juce::Component& c) {
108:         for (auto* child : c.getChildren())
109:         {
110:             if (! child->isVisible() || ! child->isEnabled())
111:                 continue;
112:             if (auto* editor = dynamic_cast<juce::TextEditor*> (child); editor != nullptr && ! editor->isReadOnly() && editor->getWantsKeyboardFocus())
113:             {
114:                 const auto at = dialog.getLocalPoint (editor, juce::Point<int>());
115:                 if (best == nullptr || at.y < bestAt.y || (at.y == bestAt.y && at.x < bestAt.x))
116:                 {
117:                     best = editor;
118:                     bestAt = at;
119:                 }
120:                 continue;
121:             }
122:             walk (*child);
123:         }
124:     };
125:     walk (dialog);
126:     return best;
127: }
128: 
129: namespace
130: {
131: struct DialogTextFocus final : juce::FocusChangeListener
132: {
133:     void globalFocusChanged (juce::Component* focused) override
134:     {
135:         if (focused == nullptr)
136:             return;
137:         auto* dialog = dynamic_cast<juce::AlertWindow*> (focused->getTopLevelComponent());
138:         if (dialog == nullptr || ! dialog->isShowing())
139:             return;
140:         auto& props = dialog->getProperties();
141:         static const juce::Identifier done ("lynxTextFocused");
142:         if (props.contains (done)) // only the dialog's first focus: Tab and clicks move it freely afterwards
143:             return;
144:         props.set (done, true);
145:         if (dynamic_cast<juce::TextEditor*> (focused) != nullptr)
146:             return;
147:         if (auto* field = firstTextField (*dialog))
148:             field->grabKeyboardFocus(); // (AlertWindow's fields select all their text when focused)
149:     }
150: };
151: 
152: std::unique_ptr<DialogTextFocus>& dialogTextFocus()
153: {
154:     static std::unique_ptr<DialogTextFocus> listener;
155:     return listener;
156: }
157: } // namespace
158: 
159: void installDialogTextFocus()
160: {
161:     if (dialogTextFocus() != nullptr)
162:         return;
163:     dialogTextFocus() = std::make_unique<DialogTextFocus>();
164:     juce::Desktop::getInstance().addFocusChangeListener (dialogTextFocus().get());
165: }
166: 
167: void removeDialogTextFocus()
168: {
169:     if (dialogTextFocus() == nullptr)
170:         return;
171:     juce::Desktop::getInstance().removeFocusChangeListener (dialogTextFocus().get());
172:     dialogTextFocus() = nullptr;
173: }
174: 
175: juce::Image logoImage()
176: {
177:     juce::Image image (juce::Image::ARGB, 256, 256, true);
178:     juce::Graphics g (image);
179:     g.setColour (juce::Colour::fromRGB (0x14, 0x12, 0x18));
180:     g.fillRoundedRectangle (juce::Rectangle<float> (4.0f, 4.0f, 248.0f, 248.0f), 60.0f);
181: 
182:     g.setColour (juce::Colour::fromRGB (0xEF, 0xB8, 0xC8));
183:     g.drawLine (128.0f, 24.0f, 128.0f, 43.0f, 8.0f);
184:     g.drawLine (128.0f, 213.0f, 128.0f, 232.0f, 8.0f);
185:     g.drawLine (24.0f, 128.0f, 43.0f, 128.0f, 8.0f);
186:     g.drawLine (213.0f, 128.0f, 232.0f, 128.0f, 8.0f);
187:     g.drawLine (54.0f, 54.0f, 68.0f, 68.0f, 8.0f);
188:     g.drawLine (188.0f, 188.0f, 202.0f, 202.0f, 8.0f);
189:     g.drawLine (202.0f, 54.0f, 188.0f, 68.0f, 8.0f);
190:     g.drawLine (68.0f, 188.0f, 54.0f, 202.0f, 8.0f);
191: 
192:     g.setColour (juce::Colour::fromRGB (0xD0, 0xBC, 0xFF));
193:     g.fillEllipse (juce::Rectangle<float> (54.0f, 54.0f, 148.0f, 148.0f));
194:     g.setColour (juce::Colour::fromRGB (0x67, 0x50, 0xA4));
195:     g.fillEllipse (juce::Rectangle<float> (78.0f, 78.0f, 100.0f, 100.0f));
196: 
197:     g.setColour (juce::Colours::white);
198:     const int heights[] { 28, 48, 68, 44, 30 };
199:     for (int i = 0; i < 5; ++i)
200:     {
201:         const auto height = (float) heights[i];
202:         g.fillRoundedRectangle (juce::Rectangle<float> (100.0f + 14.0f * i, 128.0f - height * 0.5f, 8.0f, height), 4.0f);
203:     }
204:     return image;
205: }
206: 
207: void applyWindowChrome (juce::Component& window)
208: {
209:    #if JUCE_WINDOWS
210:     // dark native title bar (Windows 10 1809+), tinted with the LYNX background on Windows 11
211:     auto* hwnd = (HWND) window.getWindowHandle();
212:     if (hwnd == nullptr)
213:         return;
214: 
215:     using SetAttribute = HRESULT (WINAPI*) (HWND, DWORD, LPCVOID, DWORD);
216:     static const auto setAttribute = [] {
217:         auto* dwm = LoadLibraryW (L"dwmapi.dll");
218:         return dwm != nullptr ? (SetAttribute) (void*) GetProcAddress (dwm, "DwmSetWindowAttribute") : nullptr;
219:     }();
220: 
221:     if (setAttribute == nullptr)
222:         return;
223: 
224:     const BOOL dark = TRUE;
225:     if (FAILED (setAttribute (hwnd, 20 /* DWMWA_USE_IMMERSIVE_DARK_MODE */, &dark, sizeof (dark))))
226:         setAttribute (hwnd, 19 /* same attribute before Windows 10 20H1 */, &dark, sizeof (dark));
227: 
228:     auto colorRef = [] (juce::Colour c) { return (DWORD) (c.getRed() | (c.getGreen() << 8) | (c.getBlue() << 16)); };
229:     const auto caption = colorRef (theme::colour::bg1);
230:     const auto text = colorRef (theme::colour::textPrimary);
231:     const auto border = colorRef (theme::colour::border);
232:     setAttribute (hwnd, 35 /* DWMWA_CAPTION_COLOR */, &caption, sizeof (caption));
233:     setAttribute (hwnd, 36 /* DWMWA_TEXT_COLOR */, &text, sizeof (text));
234:     setAttribute (hwnd, 34 /* DWMWA_BORDER_COLOR */, &border, sizeof (border));
235:    #else
236:     juce::ignoreUnused (window);
237:    #endif
238: }
239: 
240: namespace
241: {
242: void applyOwnership (juce::Component& window)
243: {
244:     applyWindowChrome (window);
245: 
246:     if (auto* top = dynamic_cast<juce::TopLevelWindow*> (&window))
247:         if (auto* peer = top->getPeer())
248:             peer->setIcon (logoImage());
249:    #if JUCE_WINDOWS
250:     auto* owner = mainWindow().getComponent();
251:     if (owner == nullptr || owner == &window || window.getWindowHandle() == nullptr || owner->getWindowHandle() == nullptr)
252:         return;
253:     // "owned" window: Windows keeps it above its owner and minimizes it along with it
254:     SetWindowLongPtrW ((HWND) window.getWindowHandle(), GWLP_HWNDPARENT, (LONG_PTR) owner->getWindowHandle());
255:    #endif
256: }
257: 
258: // JUCE recreates a window's native peer when its style changes (setResizable, setUsingNativeTitleBar...
259: // after it is on screen). The new native window has no owner, so it fell behind the main window
260: // (issue #4: the Audio editor opened behind LYNX). This re-applies the ownership to every new peer.
261: class OwnershipKeeper final : public juce::ComponentListener
262: {
263: public:
264:     explicit OwnershipKeeper (juce::Component& w) : window (&w) { w.addComponentListener (this); }
265:     ~OwnershipKeeper() override
266:     {
267:         if (window != nullptr)
268:             window->removeComponentListener (this);
269:     }
270: 
271:     void componentParentHierarchyChanged (juce::Component& c) override
272:     {
273:         if (c.getPeer() != nullptr) // (also called while the old peer is still there: harmless)
274:             applyOwnership (c);
275:     }
276: 
277:     // round 170h: an owned window that goes away while it is the active one (closed, hidden, deleted) gives the
278:     // activation back to the main window, not to whatever program Windows picks next (the shortcuts and Space did
279:     // nothing after closing a dialog until a click in LYNX). Windows that do it themselves first are not affected.
280:     void componentVisibilityChanged (juce::Component& c) override
281:     {
282:         if (! c.isVisible())
283:             if (auto* top = dynamic_cast<juce::TopLevelWindow*> (&c); top != nullptr && top->isActiveWindow())
284:                 activateMainWindow();
285:     }
286: 
287:     void componentBeingDeleted (juce::Component& c) override;
288: 
289:     juce::Component* window;
290: };
291: 
292: std::map<juce::Component*, std::unique_ptr<OwnershipKeeper>>& keepers()
293: {
294:     static std::map<juce::Component*, std::unique_ptr<OwnershipKeeper>> all;
295:     return all;
296: }
297: 
298: void OwnershipKeeper::componentBeingDeleted (juce::Component& c)
299: {
300:    #if JUCE_WINDOWS
301:     // (deleted while still shown and active: hand the activation over before its native window goes)
302:     if (c.isVisible() && c.getPeer() != nullptr && GetForegroundWindow() == (HWND) c.getWindowHandle())
303:         activateMainWindow();
304:    #endif
305:     c.removeComponentListener (this);
306:     window = nullptr;
307:     keepers().erase (&c); // deletes this
308: }
309: } // namespace
310: 
311: bool isOwnedByMainWindow (juce::Component& window)
312: {
313:    #if JUCE_WINDOWS
314:     auto* owner = mainWindow().getComponent();
315:     return owner != nullptr && window.getWindowHandle() != nullptr && owner->getWindowHandle() != nullptr
316:            && GetWindow ((HWND) window.getWindowHandle(), GW_OWNER) == (HWND) owner->getWindowHandle();
317:    #else
318:     return keepers().count (&window) > 0;
319:    #endif
320: }
321: 
322: bool isAboveMainWindow (juce::Component& window)
323: {
324:    #if JUCE_WINDOWS
325:     auto* owner = mainWindow().getComponent();
326:     if (owner == nullptr || window.getWindowHandle() == nullptr || owner->getWindowHandle() == nullptr)
327:         return false;
328:     for (auto h = GetWindow ((HWND) window.getWindowHandle(), GW_HWNDNEXT); h != nullptr; h = GetWindow (h, GW_HWNDNEXT))
329:         if (h == (HWND) owner->getWindowHandle())
330:             return true;
331:     return false;
332:    #else
333:     return isOwnedByMainWindow (window);
334:    #endif
335: }
336: 
337: void forceForegroundForTest (juce::Component& window)
338: {
339:    #if JUCE_WINDOWS
340:     // a process started in the background may not take the foreground: join the input of the foreground window's
341:     // thread for a moment (what UI automation tools do)
342:     auto* hwnd = (HWND) window.getWindowHandle();
343:     auto* foreground = GetForegroundWindow();
344:     if (hwnd == nullptr || foreground == hwnd)
345:         return;
346:     const auto theirs = GetWindowThreadProcessId (foreground, nullptr), ours = GetCurrentThreadId();
347:     AttachThreadInput (theirs, ours, TRUE);
348:     SetForegroundWindow (hwnd);
349:     SetFocus (hwnd);
350:     AttachThreadInput (theirs, ours, FALSE);
351:    #else
352:     window.toFront (true);
353:    #endif
354: }
355: 
356: namespace
357: {
358: int gpuSetting = -1; // −1 not read yet
359: 
360: void applyToPeer (juce::ComponentPeer& peer, bool gpu)
361: {
362:    #if JUCE_WINDOWS
363:     const auto engines = peer.getAvailableRenderingEngines();
364:     if (engines.size() < 2)
365:         return;
366:     const auto wanted = gpu ? engines.size() - 1 : 0; // JUCE 8: 0 = GDI ("Software Renderer"), last = Direct2D
367:     if (peer.getCurrentRenderingEngine() != wanted)
368:         peer.setCurrentRenderingEngine (wanted);
369:    #else
370:     (void) peer;
371:     (void) gpu;
372:    #endif
373: }
374: 
375: struct RenderingWatch final : juce::Timer
376: {
377:     void timerCallback() override
378:     {
379:         if (gpuRenderingEnabled())
380:             return; // (JUCE's default: nothing to change)
381:         for (int i = juce::ComponentPeer::getNumPeers(); --i >= 0;)
382:             if (auto* peer = juce::ComponentPeer::getPeer (i))
383:                 applyToPeer (*peer, false);
384:     }
385: };
386: } // namespace
387: 
388: bool gpuRenderingEnabled()
389: {
390:     if (gpuSetting < 0)
391:         gpuSetting = platform::settingsFile ("gpu-rendering.txt").loadFileAsString().trim() == "0" ? 0 : 1;
392:     return gpuSetting != 0;
393: }
394: 
395: void setGpuRenderingEnabled (bool on)
396: {
397:     gpuSetting = on ? 1 : 0;
398:     platform::settingsFile ("gpu-rendering.txt").replaceWithText (on ? "1" : "0");
399:     for (int i = juce::ComponentPeer::getNumPeers(); --i >= 0;)
400:         if (auto* peer = juce::ComponentPeer::getPeer (i))
401:         {
402:             applyToPeer (*peer, on && ! peer->getComponent().getProperties()["lynxGdi"]); // (plugin windows stay on GDI)
403:             peer->getComponent().repaint();
404:         }
405: }
406: 
407: void startRenderingWatch()
408: {
409:    #if JUCE_WINDOWS
410:     static RenderingWatch watch;
411:     watch.startTimer (500);
412:    #endif
413: }
414: 
415: void applyRenderingChoice (juce::Component& window)
416: {
417:     if (! gpuRenderingEnabled())
418:         if (auto* peer = window.getPeer())
419:             applyToPeer (*peer, false);
420: }
421: 
422: namespace
423: {
424: bool closeOnEscape (juce::Component* window);
425: 
426: struct SecondaryKeyForwarder final : juce::KeyListener
427: {
428:     std::function<bool (const juce::KeyPress&)> handler;
429: 
430:     bool keyPressed (const juce::KeyPress& key, juce::Component* window) override
431:     {
432:         if (window != nullptr && (bool) window->getProperties().getWithDefault ("lynxOwnKeys", false))
433:             return false;
434:         if (key == juce::KeyPress::escapeKey)
435:             return closeOnEscape (window);
436:         if (handler == nullptr)
437:             return false;
438:         return handler (key);
439:     }
440: };
441: 
442: // FL closes the focused plugin / channel / tool window with Esc (round 170h, FL 2026 driven: a 808 HiHat channel
443: // window, Sawer, Fruity Parametric EQ 2). LYNX's own editors that use Esc (a text field, a selection) take it first;
444: // only an Esc nothing in the window used closes it. Async: the window may delete itself in closeButtonPressed.
445: bool closeOnEscape (juce::Component* window)
446: {
447:     auto* doc = dynamic_cast<juce::DocumentWindow*> (window);
448:     if (doc == nullptr || ! doc->isVisible())
449:         return false;
450:     juce::MessageManager::callAsync ([safe = juce::Component::SafePointer<juce::DocumentWindow> (doc)] {
451:         if (auto* w = safe.getComponent(); w != nullptr && w->isVisible())
452:             w->closeButtonPressed();
453:     });
454:     return true;
455: }
456: 
457: SecondaryKeyForwarder& keyForwarder()
458: {
459:     static SecondaryKeyForwarder forwarder;
460:     return forwarder;
461: }
462: } // namespace
463: 
464: #if JUCE_WINDOWS
465: // A hosted plugin's own view (VST3 / VST2 / CLAP) gets the keys itself: with the focus in its native child window the
466: // keys never reach JUCE, and with the focus on JUCE's wrapper the wrapper swallows them all. FL still runs its
467: // shortcuts there (Space plays), so a thread keyboard hook hands such a key to the main window's global actions when
468: // it is one, before the plugin sees it (round 165q / issue #12: Space did nothing over Pro-Q 4). Plain letters and
469: // digits stay the plugin's (its own keys and text fields), and so does everything typed into a native Edit control.
470: namespace
471: {
472: int juceKeyCodeOf (WPARAM vk)
473: {
474:     if ((vk >= 'A' && vk <= 'Z') || (vk >= '0' && vk <= '9'))
475:         return (int) vk;
476:     if (vk >= VK_F1 && vk <= VK_F12)
477:         return juce::KeyPress::F1Key + (int) (vk - VK_F1);
478:     if (vk >= VK_NUMPAD0 && vk <= VK_NUMPAD9)
479:         return juce::KeyPress::numberPad0 + (int) (vk - VK_NUMPAD0);
480:     switch (vk)
481:     {
482:         case VK_SPACE: return juce::KeyPress::spaceKey;
483:         case VK_RETURN: return juce::KeyPress::returnKey;
484:         case VK_TAB: return juce::KeyPress::tabKey;
485:         case VK_DELETE: return juce::KeyPress::deleteKey;
486:         case VK_BACK: return juce::KeyPress::backspaceKey;
487:         case VK_INSERT: return juce::KeyPress::insertKey;
488:         case VK_UP: return juce::KeyPress::upKey;
489:         case VK_DOWN: return juce::KeyPress::downKey;
490:         case VK_LEFT: return juce::KeyPress::leftKey;
491:         case VK_RIGHT: return juce::KeyPress::rightKey;
492:         case VK_HOME: return juce::KeyPress::homeKey;
493:         case VK_END: return juce::KeyPress::endKey;
494:         case VK_PRIOR: return juce::KeyPress::pageUpKey;
495:         case VK_NEXT: return juce::KeyPress::pageDownKey;
496:         case VK_ADD: return juce::KeyPress::numberPadAdd;
497:         case VK_SUBTRACT: return juce::KeyPress::numberPadSubtract;
498:         default: return 0;
499:     }
500: }
501: 
502: // The focus is in a hosted plugin's view inside one of LYNX's secondary windows (not the main window)
503: bool focusIsInPluginView()
504: {
505:     const auto focus = GetFocus();
506:     if (focus == nullptr)
507:         return false;
508:     const auto root = GetAncestor (focus, GA_ROOT);
509:     auto* main = mainWindow().getComponent();
510:     if (root == nullptr || (main != nullptr && main->getWindowHandle() == (void*) root))
511:         return false;
512:     juce::Component* window = nullptr;
513:     for (int i = 0; i < juce::ComponentPeer::getNumPeers(); ++i)
514:         if (auto* peer = juce::ComponentPeer::getPeer (i); peer != nullptr && peer->getNativeHandle() == (void*) root)
515:             window = &peer->getComponent();
516:     if (window == nullptr)
517:         return false; // (not a LYNX window)
518:     if (focus != root)
519:     {
520:         // a native child: the plugin's view, unless it is a native text field
521:         wchar_t cls[64] = {};
522:         GetClassNameW (focus, cls, 63);
523:         const juce::String name (cls);
524:         return ! name.containsIgnoreCase ("edit");
525:     }
526:     // JUCE's own focus: a hosted plugin's editor wrapper (it swallows every key)
527:     auto* focused = juce::Component::getCurrentlyFocusedComponent();
528:     auto* editor = focused != nullptr ? (dynamic_cast<juce::AudioProcessorEditor*> (focused) != nullptr ? dynamic_cast<juce::AudioProcessorEditor*> (focused)
529:                                                                                                       : focused->findParentComponentOfClass<juce::AudioProcessorEditor>())
530:                                       : nullptr;
531:     return editor != nullptr && dynamic_cast<juce::AudioPluginInstance*> (editor->getAudioProcessor()) != nullptr;
532: }
533: 
534: HHOOK pluginKeyHook = nullptr;
535: 
536: LRESULT CALLBACK pluginKeyProc (int code, WPARAM vk, LPARAM info)
537: {
538:     const auto down = (info & (1u << 31)) == 0, repeat = (info & (1 << 30)) != 0;
539:     if (code == HC_ACTION && down && ! repeat && keyForwarder().handler != nullptr && vk != VK_ESCAPE)
540:         if (const auto keyCode = juceKeyCodeOf (vk); keyCode != 0)
541:         {
542:             int mods = 0;
543:             if (GetKeyState (VK_CONTROL) & 0x8000) mods |= juce::ModifierKeys::ctrlModifier;
544:             if (GetKeyState (VK_SHIFT) & 0x8000) mods |= juce::ModifierKeys::shiftModifier;
545:             if (GetKeyState (VK_MENU) & 0x8000) mods |= juce::ModifierKeys::altModifier;
546:             const auto plainCharacter = ((vk >= 'A' && vk <= 'Z') || (vk >= '0' && vk <= '9'))
547:                                         && (mods & (juce::ModifierKeys::ctrlModifier | juce::ModifierKeys::altModifier)) == 0;
548:             if (! plainCharacter && focusIsInPluginView() && keyForwarder().handler (juce::KeyPress (keyCode, juce::ModifierKeys (mods), 0)))
549:                 return 1; // ran a LYNX action: the plugin doesn't get the key too
550:         }
551:     return CallNextHookEx (pluginKeyHook, code, vk, info);
552: }
553: } // namespace
554: #endif
555: 
556: void setSecondaryWindowKeyHandler (std::function<bool (const juce::KeyPress&)> handler)
557: {
558:     keyForwarder().handler = std::move (handler);
559:    #if JUCE_WINDOWS
560:     if (keyForwarder().handler != nullptr && pluginKeyHook == nullptr)
561:         pluginKeyHook = SetWindowsHookExW (WH_KEYBOARD, pluginKeyProc, nullptr, GetCurrentThreadId());
562:     else if (keyForwarder().handler == nullptr && pluginKeyHook != nullptr)
563:     {
564:         UnhookWindowsHookEx (pluginKeyHook);
565:         pluginKeyHook = nullptr;
566:     }
567:    #endif
568: }
569: 
570: void keepAboveMainWindow (juce::Component& window)
571: {
572:     window.addKeyListener (&keyForwarder()); // (JUCE adds it once)
573:     applyRenderingChoice (window);
574:     auto& all = keepers();
575:     auto& keeper = all[&window];
576:     if (keeper == nullptr)
577:     {
578:         keeper = std::make_unique<OwnershipKeeper> (window);
579:         fitWindowOnScreen (window);
580:     }
581:     applyOwnership (window);
582: }
583: 
584: void fitWindowOnScreen (juce::Component& window)
585: {
586:     if (! window.isOnDesktop() || ! window.isVisible())
587:         return;
588:     auto* resizable = dynamic_cast<juce::ResizableWindow*> (&window);
589:     if (resizable != nullptr && (resizable->isFullScreen() || resizable->isMinimised()))
590:         return;
591: 
592:     juce::BorderSize<int> frame;
593:     if (auto* peer = window.getPeer())
594:         if (const auto size = peer->getFrameSizeIfPresent())
595:             frame = *size;
596: 
597:     const auto bounds = window.getBounds();
598:     const auto& displays = juce::Desktop::getInstance().getDisplays();
599:     const auto* display = displays.getDisplayForRect (frame.addedTo (bounds));
600:     if (display == nullptr)
601:         display = displays.getPrimaryDisplay();
602:     if (display == nullptr)
603:         return;
604:     const auto area = display->userArea;
605: 
606:     auto content = bounds;
607:     if (resizable != nullptr && resizable->isResizable())
608:     {
609:         auto w = juce::jmin (content.getWidth(), area.getWidth() - frame.getLeftAndRight());
610:         auto h = juce::jmin (content.getHeight(), area.getHeight() - frame.getTopAndBottom());
611:         if (auto* limits = resizable->getConstrainer())
612:         {
613:             w = juce::jmax (w, limits->getMinimumWidth());
614:             h = juce::jmax (h, limits->getMinimumHeight());
615:         }
616:         content.setSize (juce::jmax (1, w), juce::jmax (1, h));
617:     }
618:     const auto outer = frame.addedTo (content);
619:     const auto placed = windowfit::place ({ outer.getX(), outer.getY(), outer.getWidth(), outer.getHeight() },
620:                                           { area.getX(), area.getY(), area.getWidth(), area.getHeight() });
621:     content.setPosition (placed.x + frame.getLeft(), placed.y + frame.getTop());
622:     if (content != bounds)
623:         window.setBounds (content);
624: }
625: 
626: void fitAllWindowsOnScreen()
627: {
628:     auto& desktop = juce::Desktop::getInstance();
629:     for (int i = desktop.getNumComponents(); --i >= 0;)
630:         if (auto* window = dynamic_cast<juce::ResizableWindow*> (desktop.getComponent (i)))
631:             fitWindowOnScreen (*window);
632: }
633: 
634: void configureSecondaryWindowControls (juce::DocumentWindow& window)
635: {
636:    #if JUCE_ANDROID
637:     // Android has no desktop-style title bar for JUCE peers. Draw Radiant's JUCE title bar so close and minimize
638:     // controls remain in the app, and keep an existing fullscreen button where the window already requested one.
639:     window.setUsingNativeTitleBar (false);
640:     const auto hasMaximiseButton = window.getMaximiseButton() != nullptr;
641:     const auto buttons = juce::DocumentWindow::closeButton | juce::DocumentWindow::minimiseButton
642:                          | (hasMaximiseButton ? juce::DocumentWindow::maximiseButton : 0);
643:     window.setTitleBarButtonsRequired (buttons, false);
644:     window.setTitleBarHeight (26);
645: 
646:     if (auto* button = window.getMinimiseButton())
647:     {
648:         struct MinimiseState
649:         {
650:             int contentWidth = 0;
651:             int contentHeight = 0;
652:             bool contentWasVisible = true;
653:             bool collapsed = false;
654:         };
655: 
656:         auto state = std::make_shared<MinimiseState>();
657:         auto safeWindow = juce::Component::SafePointer<juce::DocumentWindow> (&window);
658:         button->setClickingTogglesState (true);
659:         button->setTooltip ("Minimize window");
660:         button->onClick = [safeWindow, state]
661:         {
662:             auto* current = safeWindow.getComponent();
663:             auto* content = current != nullptr ? current->getContentComponent() : nullptr;
664:             if (current == nullptr || content == nullptr)
665:                 return;
666: 
667:             if (! state->collapsed)
668:             {
669:                 state->contentWidth = content->getWidth();
670:                 state->contentHeight = content->getHeight();
671:                 state->contentWasVisible = content->isVisible();
672:                 content->setVisible (false);
673:                 current->setContentComponentSize (juce::jmax (1, state->contentWidth), 1);
674:                 state->collapsed = true;
675:                 if (auto* minimise = current->getMinimiseButton())
676:                     minimise->setTooltip ("Restore window");
677:             }
678:             else
679:             {
680:                 content->setVisible (state->contentWasVisible);
681:                 current->setContentComponentSize (juce::jmax (1, state->contentWidth), juce::jmax (1, state->contentHeight));
682:                 state->collapsed = false;
683:                 if (auto* minimise = current->getMinimiseButton())
684:                     minimise->setTooltip ("Minimize window");
685:             }
686: 
687:             fitWindowOnScreen (*current);
688:         };
689:     }
690:    #else
691:     window.setUsingNativeTitleBar (true);
692:    #endif
693: }
694: namespace
695: {
696: // the usable screen of the main window (or the primary one), in logical pixels
697: juce::Rectangle<int> usableScreen()
698: {
699:     const auto& displays = juce::Desktop::getInstance().getDisplays();
700:     const juce::Displays::Display* display = nullptr;
701:     if (auto* main = juce::TopLevelWindow::getActiveTopLevelWindow())
702:         display = displays.getDisplayForRect (main->getScreenBounds());
703:     if (display == nullptr)
704:         display = displays.getPrimaryDisplay();
705:     return display != nullptr ? display->userArea : juce::Rectangle<int> (0, 0, 1280, 720);
706: }
707: 
708: // a window's frame (title bar and borders, logical px): this window's own once it is on the screen, else another
709: // LYNX window's (the same window manager draws them), else a typical native title bar
710: juce::BorderSize<int> windowFrame (juce::Component* window)
711: {
712:     auto frameOf = [] (juce::Component* c) -> std::optional<juce::BorderSize<int>> {
713:         if (c != nullptr)
714:             if (auto* peer = c->getPeer())
715:                 if (const auto f = peer->getFrameSizeIfPresent(); f && f->getTop() > 0)
716:                     return *f;
717:         return std::nullopt;
718:     };
719:     if (const auto own = frameOf (window))
720:         return *own;
721:     for (int i = 0; i < juce::TopLevelWindow::getNumTopLevelWindows(); ++i)
722:         if (const auto other = frameOf (juce::TopLevelWindow::getTopLevelWindow (i)))
723:             return *other;
724:     return { 32, 8, 8, 8 };
725: }
726: 
727: class ScrollingContent final : public juce::Component,
728:                                private juce::ComponentListener
729: {
730: public:
731:     explicit ScrollingContent (std::unique_ptr<juce::Component> c) : content (c.release())
732:     {
733:         setName (content->getName());
734:         viewport.setViewedComponent (content, true); // (the viewport deletes it)
735:         viewport.setScrollBarThickness (12);
736:         addAndMakeVisible (viewport);
737:         content->addComponentListener (this);
738:         fit();
739:     }
740: 
741:     ~ScrollingContent() override
742:     {
743:         if (content != nullptr)
744:             content->removeComponentListener (this);
745:     }
746: 
747:     void resized() override { viewport.setBounds (getLocalBounds()); }
748: 
749:     // on the screen: fitted again with the window's real frame, and the window placed so it is all on the screen
750:     void parentHierarchyChanged() override
751:     {
752:         juce::MessageManager::callAsync ([safe = juce::Component::SafePointer<ScrollingContent> (this)] {
753:             if (safe != nullptr && safe->getPeer() != nullptr)
754:                 safe->fit();
755:         });
756:     }
757: 
758:     void fit()
759:     {
760:         if (content == nullptr)
761:             return;
762:         const auto area = usableScreen();
763:         auto* window = getTopLevelComponent() != this ? getTopLevelComponent() : nullptr;
764:         const auto frame = windowFrame (window);
765:         const auto f = windowfit::scrollFit (content->getWidth(), content->getHeight(), area.getWidth(), area.getHeight(), frame.getLeftAndRight(),
766:                                              frame.getTopAndBottom(), viewport.getScrollBarThickness());
767:         viewport.setScrollBarsShown (f.scrollsV, f.scrollsH);
768:         setSize (f.w, f.h);
769:         if (window != nullptr && window->isOnDesktop())
770:             fitWindowOnScreen (*window);
771:     }
772: 
773: private:
774:     void componentMovedOrResized (juce::Component&, bool, bool wasResized) override
775:     {
776:         if (wasResized)
777:             fit(); // (the window follows its content's size)
778:     }
779: 
780:     juce::Component::SafePointer<juce::Component> content;
781:     juce::Viewport viewport;
782: };
783: } // namespace
784: 
785: std::unique_ptr<juce::Component> fitContentToScreen (std::unique_ptr<juce::Component> content)
786: {
787:     if (content == nullptr)
788:         return content;
789:     const auto area = usableScreen();
790:     const auto frame = windowFrame (nullptr);
791:     const auto f = windowfit::scrollFit (content->getWidth(), content->getHeight(), area.getWidth(), area.getHeight(), frame.getLeftAndRight(),
792:                                          frame.getTopAndBottom(), 12);
793:     if (! f.scrolls())
794:         return content;
795:     return std::make_unique<ScrollingContent> (std::move (content));
796: }
797: 
798: void matchWindowOrderToScreen()
799: {
800:     auto* owner = mainWindow().getComponent();
801:     if (owner == nullptr)
802:         return;
803: 
804:     // the windows above the main window, from the lowest up (raising them in that order keeps their order)
805:     std::vector<juce::Component*> above;
806:     auto& desktop = juce::Desktop::getInstance();
807:     for (int i = 0; i < desktop.getNumComponents(); ++i)
808:         if (auto* c = desktop.getComponent (i); c != owner && c->isVisible() && ! c->isAlwaysOnTop() && isAboveMainWindow (*c))
809:             above.push_back (c);
810: 
811:    #if JUCE_WINDOWS
812:     std::map<HWND, int> depth; // 0 = the topmost window on the screen
813:     int d = 0;
814:     for (auto h = GetTopWindow (nullptr); h != nullptr; h = GetWindow (h, GW_HWNDNEXT))
815:         depth[h] = d++;
816:     const auto depthOf = [&depth] (juce::Component* c) {
817:         const auto it = depth.find ((HWND) c->getWindowHandle());
818:         return it == depth.end() ? std::numeric_limits<int>::max() : it->second;
819:     };
820:     std::stable_sort (above.begin(), above.end(), [&] (juce::Component* a, juce::Component* b) { return depthOf (a) > depthOf (b); });
821:    #endif
822: 
823:     // JUCE's list already in that order after the main window: nothing to do (no window is touched)
824:     const auto indexOf = [&desktop] (juce::Component* c) {
825:         for (int i = 0; i < desktop.getNumComponents(); ++i)
826:             if (desktop.getComponent (i) == c)
827:                 return i;
828:         return -1;
829:     };
830:     auto inOrder = true;
831:     auto last = indexOf (owner);
832:     for (auto* c : above)
833:     {
834:         const auto at = indexOf (c);
835:         inOrder = inOrder && at > last;
836:         last = at;
837:     }
838:     if (inOrder)
839:         return;
840: 
841:     for (auto* c : above)
842:         c->toFront (false);
843: }
844: 
845: } // namespace lynx

39: // FL's plugin wrapper settings page (REA rounds 146a / 149), in LYNX's style with the tabs and groups of FL's wrapper
40: // form (Fruity Wrapper's DFM [DATA], FL 2026's page [EXEC]): Settings (Info with the latency, MIDI ports), Processing
41: // (Options) and Troubleshooting (Compatibility: the fixed buffers; Location). Options that re-create the plugin keep
42: // its state and close this window (open it again from the channel or the slot).
43: class WrapperSettingsPage final : public juce::Component, private juce::ChangeListener
44: {
45: public:
46:     WrapperSettingsPage (AudioEngine& e, std::function<PluginRef* (Song&)> f, juce::AudioPluginInstance& p, bool channel)
47:         : engine (e), find (std::move (f)), plugin (p)
48:     {
49:         const char* tabNames[] = { TR_NOOP ("Settings"), TR_NOOP ("Processing"), TR_NOOP ("Troubleshooting") };
50:         for (int t = 0; t < 3; ++t)
51:         {
52:             auto* b = tabs.add (new juce::TextButton (tr (tabNames[t])));
53:             b->setClickingTogglesState (false); // LYNX's tab look (as the channel window's): the shown one filled
54:             b->onClick = [this, t] { showTab (t); };
55:             addAndMakeVisible (b);
56:         }
57:         const auto& options = wrapperOptions();
58:         for (std::size_t i = 0; i < options.size(); ++i)
59:         {
60:             auto* t = toggles.add (new juce::ToggleButton (tr (options[i].label)));
61:             t->setTooltip (tr (options[i].hint));
62:             t->setColour (juce::ToggleButton::textColourId, theme::colour::textPrimary);
63:             t->setColour (juce::ToggleButton::tickColourId, theme::colour::accent);
64:             t->onClick = [this, i] {
65:                 auto* en = &engine;
66:                 auto fn = find;
67:                 auto* inst = &plugin;
68:                 const auto& o = wrapperOptions()[i];
69:                 juce::MessageManager::callAsync ([en, fn, inst, member = o.member, re = o.recreates] { toggleWrapperOption (*en, fn, inst, member, re); });
70:             };
71:             addChildComponent (t);
72:         }
73:         for (auto* box : { &inPort, &outPort })
74:         {
75:             box->addItem (juce::String::fromUTF8 ("\xe2\x80\x94"), 1);
76:             for (int i = 0; i < 256; ++i)
77:                 box->addItem (juce::String (i), i + 2);
78:             addChildComponent (box);
79:         }
80:         for (int st = 1; st <= 48; ++st)
81:             bendRange.addItem (juce::String (st), st);
82:         bendRange.setTooltip (tr ("The pitch bend range sent to the plugin (semitones)"));
83:         bendRange.onChange = [this] {
84:             engine.editSong ([fn = find, v = bendRange.getSelectedId()] (Song& song) {
85:                 if (auto* ref = fn (song); ref != nullptr && v > 0)
86:                     ref->bendRange = v;
87:             });
88:         };
89:         addChildComponent (bendRange);
90:         inPort.setTooltip (tr ("MIDI input port number"));
91:         inPort.onChange = [this] { setPort (inPort.getSelectedId() - 2, false); };
92:         // FL's Output port: the notes the plugin sends out play the plugins on that input port (instruments in the
93:         // Channel rack that send MIDI)
94:         outPort.setTooltip (tr ("MIDI output port number: the notes this plugin sends play the plugins whose input port it is"));
95:         outPort.onChange = [this] { setPort (outPort.getSelectedId() - 2, true); };
96:         outPort.setEnabled (channel && plugin.producesMidi());
97:         // FL's Info › Latency: what the plugin reports, or set by hand (menu: Reset, Set in ms / samples / beats)
98:         latency.setTooltip (tr ("Latency used by the delay compensation: what the plugin reports, or a value set by hand"));
99:         latency.onClick = [this] { showLatencyMenu(); };
100:         addChildComponent (latency);
101:         // FL's Settings › Options › "Bit size" (round 176a, Windows x64): which build of a plugin installed as both
102:         // 32-bit and 64-bit loads [UI fl36c]. Changing it re-creates the plugin with its state, like "Make bridged".
103:         bitSize.addItem (tr ("Native"), 1);
104:         bitSize.addItem (tr ("32 bit"), 2);
105:         bitSize.addItem (tr ("64 bit"), 3);
106:         bitSize.setTooltip (tr ("Which build of the plugin loads when both a 32-bit and a 64-bit one are installed"));
107:         bitSize.onChange = [this] {
108:             const auto value = bitSize.getSelectedId() == 2 ? 32 : bitSize.getSelectedId() == 3 ? 64 : 0;
109:             auto* ref = find (const_cast<Song&> (engine.getSong()));
110:             if (ref == nullptr || ref->bitSize == value)
111:                 return;
112:             juce::MessageManager::callAsync ([en = &engine, fn = find, inst = &plugin, value] {
113:                 if (PluginWindows::isOpen (*inst))
114:                     PluginWindows::close (*inst);
115:                 en->capturePluginStates(); // the other build starts from the current settings
116:                 en->editSong ([fn, value] (Song& song) {
117:                     if (auto* r = fn (song))
118:                         r->bitSize = value;
119:                 });
120:             });
121:         };
122:         addChildComponent (bitSize);
123:         locate.setButtonText (tr ("Locate"));
124:         locate.setTooltip (tr ("Show the plugin's file in the file manager"));
125:         locate.onClick = [this] { juce::File (plugin.getPluginDescription().fileOrIdentifier).revealToUser(); };
126:         addChildComponent (locate);
127:         engine.addChangeListener (this);
128:         refresh();
129:         showTab (0);
130:         setSize (520, preferredHeight());
131:     }
132: 
133:     ~WrapperSettingsPage() override { engine.removeChangeListener (this); }
134: 
135:     int preferredHeight() const { return bridge32Supported() ? 474 : 410; } // (+ Options › Bit size where it applies)

137:     void paint (juce::Graphics& g) override
138:     {
139:         using namespace theme;
140:         g.fillAll (colour::bg1);
141:         g.setColour (colour::textSecondary);
142:         g.setFont (font (type::caption));
143:         // the plugin's own version; nothing when it reports none (VST2's effGetVendorVersion 0 arrives as "0")
144:         if (const auto version = plugin.getPluginDescription().version.trim(); version.containsAnyOf ("123456789"))
145:             g.drawText (tr ("version {0}", { version }), getWidth() - 200, 8, 184, tabHeight, juce::Justification::centredRight);
146:         for (const auto& [area, title] : groups)
147:         {
148:             g.setColour (colour::bg2);
149:             g.fillRoundedRectangle (area.toFloat(), radius::md);
150:             g.setColour (colour::textSecondary);

152:             g.drawText (caps (title), area.withHeight (headerHeight).reduced (10, 0), juce::Justification::centredLeft);
153:         }
154:         g.setFont (font (type::body));
155:         for (const auto& [area, text, primary] : labels)
156:         {
157:             g.setColour (primary ? colour::textPrimary : colour::textSecondary);
158:             g.drawFittedText (text, area, juce::Justification::centredLeft, 1);
159:         }

162:     void resized() override
163:     {
164:         groups.clear();
165:         labels.clear();
166:         auto area = getLocalBounds().reduced (10, 8);
167:         auto bar = area.removeFromTop (tabHeight);
168:         for (auto* t : tabs)
169:             t->setBounds (bar.removeFromLeft (juce::jmax (80, t->getBestWidthForHeight (tabHeight) + 16)));
170:         area.removeFromTop (8);
171:         for (auto* c : getChildren())
172:             if (! tabs.contains (dynamic_cast<juce::TextButton*> (c)))
173:                 c->setVisible (false);
174: 
175:         auto group = [this] (juce::Rectangle<int>& from, int height, const juce::String& title) {
176:             auto r = from.removeFromTop (height);
177:             from.removeFromTop (8);
178:             groups.push_back ({ r, title });
179:             return r.withTrimmedTop (headerHeight).reduced (10, 4);
180:         };
181:         auto label = [this] (juce::Rectangle<int> r, const juce::String& text, bool primary = false) { labels.push_back ({ r, text, primary }); };
182:         auto show = [] (juce::Component& c, juce::Rectangle<int> r) {
183:             c.setBounds (r);
184:             c.setVisible (true);
185:         };
186: 
187:         if (tab == 0)
188:         {
189:             const auto d = plugin.getPluginDescription();
190:             auto info = group (area, headerHeight + 6 * rowHeight + 8, tr ("Info"));
191:             const std::pair<juce::String, juce::String> lines[] = {
192:                 { tr ("Name"), d.name + (d.manufacturerName.isNotEmpty() ? " (" + d.manufacturerName + ")" : juce::String()) },
193:                 { tr ("Type"), d.pluginFormatName + (d.isInstrument ? "  " + tr ("instrument") : "  " + tr ("effect")) + bitsText() },
194:                 { tr ("Category"), d.category },
195:                 { tr ("Inputs / outputs"), juce::String (plugin.getTotalNumInputChannels()) + " / " + juce::String (plugin.getTotalNumOutputChannels()) },
196:                 { tr ("Parameters"), juce::String (plugin.getParameters().size()) },
197:             };
198:             for (const auto& [name, value] : lines)
199:             {
200:                 auto row = info.removeFromTop (rowHeight);
201:                 label (row.removeFromLeft (110), name);
202:                 label (row, value, true);
203:             }
204:             auto row = info.removeFromTop (rowHeight);
205:             label (row.removeFromLeft (110), tr ("Latency"));
206:             show (latency, row.removeFromLeft (260).reduced (0, 2));
207: 
208:             int midiRows = 0;
209:             for (const auto& o : wrapperOptions())
210:                 midiRows += juce::String (o.section) == "MIDI" ? 1 : 0;
211:             auto midi = group (area, headerHeight + (1 + midiRows) * rowHeight + 8, tr ("MIDI"));
212:             auto line = midi.removeFromTop (rowHeight);
213:             label (line.removeFromLeft (80), tr ("Input port"));
214:             show (inPort, line.removeFromLeft (70).reduced (0, 2));
215:             line.removeFromLeft (30);
216:             label (line.removeFromLeft (90), tr ("Output port"));
217:             show (outPort, line.removeFromLeft (70).reduced (0, 2));
218:             // FL's MIDI options (round 151): release velocity, the pitch bend range RPN (with its value), MOD X as
219:             // polyphonic aftertouch, All Notes Off on stop
220:             for (std::size_t i = 0; i < wrapperOptions().size(); ++i)
221:                 if (juce::String (wrapperOptions()[i].section) == "MIDI")
222:                 {
223:                     auto row = midi.removeFromTop (rowHeight);
224:                     if (wrapperOptions()[i].member == &PluginRef::sendBendRange)
225:                         show (bendRange, row.removeFromRight (70).reduced (0, 2));
226:                     show (*toggles[(int) i], row);
227:                 }
228:             if (bridge32Supported())
229:             {
230:                 auto options = group (area, headerHeight + rowHeight + 8, tr ("Options"));
231:                 auto row = options.removeFromTop (rowHeight);
232:                 label (row.removeFromLeft (80), tr ("Bit size"));
233:                 show (bitSize, row.removeFromLeft (110).reduced (0, 2));
234:             }
235:         }
236:         else
237:         {
238:             const juce::String section = tab == 1 ? "Processing" : "Compatibility";
239:             int rows = 0;
240:             for (std::size_t i = 0; i < wrapperOptions().size(); ++i)
241:                 rows += section == wrapperOptions()[i].section && ! hiddenOption (i) ? 1 : 0;
242:             auto box = group (area, headerHeight + rows * rowHeight + 8, tr (tab == 1 ? "Options" : "Compatibility"));
243:             for (std::size_t i = 0; i < wrapperOptions().size(); ++i)
244:                 if (section == wrapperOptions()[i].section && ! hiddenOption (i))
245:                     show (*toggles[(int) i], box.removeFromTop (rowHeight).withTrimmedLeft (wrapperOptions()[i].needsFixed ? 22 : 0));
246:             if (tab == 2)
247:             {
248:                 auto where = group (area, headerHeight + rowHeight + 8, tr ("Location"));
249:                 show (locate, where.removeFromRight (90).withHeight (rowHeight).reduced (0, 2));
250:                 label (where.removeFromLeft (50).withHeight (rowHeight), tr ("Path"));
251:                 label (where.withHeight (rowHeight), plugin.getPluginDescription().fileOrIdentifier, true);
252:             }
253:         }
254:         repaint();
255:     }

259: 
260:     // A 32-bit plugin always runs bridged: FL's Processing page has no "Make bridged" for it [UI fl25c vs fl34c]
261:     bool hiddenOption (std::size_t i) const
262:     {
263:         return wrapperOptions()[i].member == &PluginRef::separateProcess && isBridged32 (plugin);
264:     }
265: 
266:     // FL's Info › Type ends with the build and how it runs: "(32bit - bridged)", "(64bit - native)" [UI fl23c, fl33c]
267:     juce::String bitsText() const
268:     {
269:         if (! bridge32Supported())
270:             return {};
271:         if (isBridged32 (plugin))
272:             return "  (" + tr ("32bit - bridged") + ")";
273:         return "  (" + (sandboxedPluginProcessId (plugin) != 0 || isSandboxedPluginDead (plugin) ? tr ("64bit - bridged") : tr ("64bit - native")) + ")";
274:     }

276:     void showTab (int t)
277:     {
278:         tab = t;
279:         for (int k = 0; k < tabs.size(); ++k)
280:             tabs[k]->setToggleState (k == t, juce::dontSendNotification);

284:     void setPort (int value, bool output)
285:     {
286:         engine.editSong ([fn = find, value, output] (Song& song) {
287:             if (auto* ref = fn (song))
288:                 (output ? ref->midiOutPort : ref->midiInPort) = value;
289:         });
290:     }

292:     double rate() const { return plugin.getSampleRate() > 0 ? plugin.getSampleRate() : 44100.0; }
293: 
294:     void setLatencyMs (float ms)
295:     {
296:         engine.editSong ([fn = find, ms] (Song& song) {
297:             if (auto* ref = fn (song))
298:                 ref->latencyOverrideMs = ms;
299:         });
300:     }
301: 
302:     void showLatencyMenu()
303:     {
304:         // FL's latency menu [EXEC]: Reset, Set in ms, Set in samples, Set in beats (beats at the song's tempo)
305:         juce::PopupMenu menu;
306:         menu.addItem (tr ("Reset"), [this] { setLatencyMs (-1.0f); });
307:         auto ask = [this] (int unit) {
308:             auto* ref = find (const_cast<Song&> (engine.getSong()));
309:             const auto bpm = juce::jmax (10.0, (double) engine.getSong().bpm);
310:             const auto ms = ref != nullptr && ref->latencyOverrideMs >= 0.0f ? (double) ref->latencyOverrideMs
311:                                                                               : plugin.getLatencySamples() * 1000.0 / rate();
312:             const auto current = unit == 0 ? ms : unit == 1 ? ms * rate() / 1000.0 : ms * bpm / 60000.0;
313:             const char* titles[] = { TR_NOOP ("Latency in ms"), TR_NOOP ("Latency in samples"), TR_NOOP ("Latency in beats") };
314:             auto* window = new juce::AlertWindow (tr ("Latency"), tr (titles[unit]), juce::MessageBoxIconType::NoIcon);
315:             window->addTextEditor ("value", juce::String (current, unit == 1 ? 0 : 3));
316:             window->addButton (tr ("OK"), 1, juce::KeyPress (juce::KeyPress::returnKey));
317:             window->addButton (tr ("Cancel"), 0, juce::KeyPress (juce::KeyPress::escapeKey));
318:             juce::Component::SafePointer<WrapperSettingsPage> safe (this);
319:             window->enterModalState (true, juce::ModalCallbackFunction::create ([safe, window, unit, bpm] (int result) {
320:                 if (result != 1 || safe == nullptr)
321:                     return;
322:                 const auto v = juce::jmax (0.0, window->getTextEditorContents ("value").getDoubleValue());
323:                 const auto ms = unit == 0 ? v : unit == 1 ? v * 1000.0 / safe->rate() : v * 60000.0 / bpm;
324:                 safe->setLatencyMs ((float) juce::jlimit (0.0, 2000.0, ms));
325:             }), true);
326:         };
327:         menu.addItem (tr ("Set in ms..."), [ask] { ask (0); });
328:         menu.addItem (tr ("Set in samples..."), [ask] { ask (1); });
329:         menu.addItem (tr ("Set in beats..."), [ask] { ask (2); });
330:         menu.showMenuAsync (juce::PopupMenu::Options().withTargetComponent (&latency));
331:     }
332: 
333:     void changeListenerCallback (juce::ChangeBroadcaster*) override { refresh(); }
334: 
335:     void refresh()
336:     {
337:         auto* ref = find (const_cast<Song&> (engine.getSong()));
338:         if (ref == nullptr)
339:             return;
340:         const auto& options = wrapperOptions();
341:         for (std::size_t i = 0; i < options.size(); ++i)
342:         {
343:             toggles[(int) i]->setToggleState (ref->*(options[i].member), juce::dontSendNotification);
344:             toggles[(int) i]->setEnabled (! options[i].needsFixed || ref->fixedBuffers);
345:         }
346:         // with Options › "Run plugins in a separate process" every plugin already runs bridged
347:         for (std::size_t i = 0; i < options.size(); ++i)
348:             if (options[i].member == &PluginRef::separateProcess && pluginSandboxEnabled())
349:                 toggles[(int) i]->setToggleState (true, juce::dontSendNotification), toggles[(int) i]->setEnabled (false);
350:         bitSize.setSelectedId (ref->bitSize == 32 ? 2 : ref->bitSize == 64 ? 3 : 1, juce::dontSendNotification);
351:         inPort.setSelectedId (ref->midiInPort + 2, juce::dontSendNotification);
352:         bendRange.setSelectedId (ref->bendRange, juce::dontSendNotification);
353:         bendRange.setEnabled (ref->sendBendRange);
354:         outPort.setSelectedId (ref->midiOutPort + 2, juce::dontSendNotification);
355:         const auto reported = plugin.getLatencySamples();
356:         const auto text = ref->latencyOverrideMs >= 0.0f
357:                               ? tr ("{0} ms (set by hand)", { juce::String (ref->latencyOverrideMs, 2) })
358:                               : (reported > 0 ? tr ("{0} samples ({1} ms)", { juce::String (reported), juce::String (reported * 1000.0 / rate(), 1) })
359:                                               : tr ("none"));
360:         latency.setButtonText (text + juce::String::fromUTF8 ("  \xe2\x96\xbe"));
361:     }
362: 
363:     struct Label
364:     {
365:         juce::Rectangle<int> area;
366:         juce::String text;
367:         bool primary = false;
368:     };
369: 
370:     AudioEngine& engine;
371:     std::function<PluginRef* (Song&)> find;
372:     juce::AudioPluginInstance& plugin;
373:     juce::OwnedArray<juce::TextButton> tabs;
374:     juce::OwnedArray<juce::ToggleButton> toggles;
375:     juce::ComboBox inPort, outPort, bendRange, bitSize;
376:     juce::TextButton latency, locate;
377:     int tab = 0;
378:     std::vector<std::pair<juce::Rectangle<int>, juce::String>> groups;
379:     std::vector<Label> labels;
380: };

382: // The window's content with FL's wrapper header: the plugin's editor, or the wrapper settings page (gear).
383: class WrappedEditor final : public juce::Component
384: {
385: public:
386:     WrappedEditor (juce::AudioProcessorEditor* e, AudioEngine& engine, std::function<PluginRef* (Song&)> find, juce::AudioPluginInstance& plugin,
387:                    bool channel)
388:         : editor (e), page (engine, std::move (find), plugin, channel), presets (plugin, [&engine] { engine.markModified(); })
389:     {
390:         addAndMakeVisible (presets); // FL's wrapper presets: ◀ ▶ and the menu (round 162d, issue #8)
391:         for (auto* b : { &pluginTab, &settingsTab })
392:         {
393:             b->setClickingTogglesState (false); // LYNX's tab look (as the channel window's): the shown one filled
394:             addAndMakeVisible (b);
395:         }
396:         pluginTab.setTooltip (tr ("The plugin's own editor"));
397:         settingsTab.setTooltip (tr ("Plugin wrapper settings: processing, bridging and MIDI options"));
398:         pluginTab.onClick = [this] { show (false); };
399:         settingsTab.onClick = [this] { show (true); };
400:         addAndMakeVisible (editor.get());
401:         addChildComponent (page);
402:         pluginTab.setToggleState (true, juce::dontSendNotification);
403:         fitEditor();
404:     }
405: 
406:     ~WrappedEditor() override
407:     {
408:         editor = nullptr; // the editor goes first (the plugin may be released right after the window)
409:     }
410: 
411:     bool isEditorResizable() const { return editor != nullptr && editor->isResizable(); }
412: 
413:     void paint (juce::Graphics& g) override
414:     {
415:         g.setColour (theme::colour::bg0);
416:         g.fillRect (getLocalBounds().removeFromTop (headerHeight));
417:     }
418: 
419:     void resized() override
420:     {
421:         auto area = getLocalBounds();
422:         auto header = area.removeFromTop (headerHeight).reduced (4, 3);
423:         pluginTab.setBounds (header.removeFromLeft (80));
424:         header.removeFromLeft (4);
425:         settingsTab.setBounds (header.removeFromLeft (130));
426:         header.removeFromLeft (8);
427:         presets.setBounds (header.removeFromLeft (juce::jmin (presets.preferredWidth(), header.getWidth())));
428:         page.setBounds (area);
429:         if (editor != nullptr && ! settings)
430:         {
431:             const juce::ScopedValueSetter<bool> guard (laying, true);
432:             if (editor->isResizable())
433:                 editor->setBounds (area);
434:             else
435:                 editor->setTopLeftPosition (area.getPosition());
436:         }
437:     }
438: 
439:     void childBoundsChanged (juce::Component* child) override
440:     {
441:         if (child == editor.get() && ! laying && ! settings)
442:             fitEditor();
443:     }
444: 
445: private:
446:     static constexpr int headerHeight = 30, minWidth = 4 + 80 + 4 + 130 + 8 + 250 + 4;
447: 
448:     void fitEditor()
449:     {
450:         if (editor != nullptr)
451:             setSize (juce::jmax (minWidth, editor->getWidth()), editor->getHeight() + headerHeight);
452:     }
453: 
454:     void show (bool showSettings)
455:     {
456:         settings = showSettings;
457:         pluginTab.setToggleState (! settings, juce::dontSendNotification);
458:         settingsTab.setToggleState (settings, juce::dontSendNotification);
459:         page.setVisible (settings);
460:         if (editor != nullptr)
461:         {
462:             editor->setVisible (! settings);
463:             // A plugin editor embedded as a native child window (VST3 / LV2 through JUCE's XEmbedComponent and CLAP /
464:             // VST2 through NativeEmbed on Linux) is not unmapped when its component is hidden: it stayed over the
465:             // Wrapper settings page and made it unreachable (issue #8). Moving it outside the window clips it away on
466:             // every platform; resized() puts it back.
467:             if (settings)
468:                 editor->setTopLeftPosition (-12000, -12000); // (X11 positions are 16-bit, also when scaled)
469:         }
470:         if (settings)
471:             setSize (juce::jmax (getWidth(), page.getWidth()), juce::jmax (getHeight(), page.preferredHeight() + headerHeight));
472:         else
473:             fitEditor();
474:         resized();
475:         if (editor != nullptr)
476:             followNativeChildren (*editor);
477:     }
478: 
479:     // JUCE's XEmbedComponent only moves its X window when it is itself moved, not when a parent (the editor) is:
480:     // tell every one inside the editor (round 162d)
481:     static void followNativeChildren (juce::Component& c)
482:     {
483:        #if JUCE_LINUX || JUCE_BSD
484:         if (auto* x = dynamic_cast<juce::XEmbedComponent*> (&c))
485:             x->updateEmbeddedBounds();
486:        #endif
487:         for (auto* child : c.getChildren())
488:             followNativeChildren (*child);
489:     }
490: 
491:     std::unique_ptr<juce::AudioProcessorEditor> editor;
492:     WrapperSettingsPage page;
493:     pluginpresetui::PresetBar presets;
494:     juce::TextButton pluginTab { tr ("Plugin") }, settingsTab { juce::String::fromUTF8 ("\xe2\x9a\x99 ") + tr ("Wrapper settings") };
495:     bool settings = false, laying = false;
496: };
497: 
498: class PluginWindow final : public juce::DocumentWindow, private juce::AudioProcessorListener, private juce::Timer
499: {
500: public:
501:     PluginWindow (std::shared_ptr<void> keepAlive, juce::AudioPluginInstance& p, const juce::String& title, PluginWindows::WrapperContext context)
502:         : DocumentWindow (title, theme::colour::bg1, DocumentWindow::closeButton | DocumentWindow::minimiseButton),
503:           owner (std::move (keepAlive)),
504:           plugin (p)
505:     {
506:         configureSecondaryWindowControls (*this);
507:         useClassicRendering();
508: 
509:         crashguard::note (("opening the editor of " + plugin.getName()).toStdString()); // session breadcrumb (round 111)
510:         juce::AudioProcessorEditor* editor = plugin.createEditorIfNeeded();
511:         if (editor == nullptr)
512:             editor = new juce::GenericAudioProcessorEditor (plugin); // plugin without a UI
513:         crashguard::note (("editor open: " + plugin.getName()).toStdString());
514: 
515:         const auto resizable = editor->isResizable();
516:         shownEditor = editor;
517:         thumbKey = plugin.getPluginDescription().createIdentifierString().toStdString();
518:         if (context.engine != nullptr && context.find) // FL's wrapper header and settings page (round 146a)
519:             setContentOwned (new WrappedEditor (editor, *context.engine, std::move (context.find), plugin, context.channel), true);
520:         else
521:             setContentOwned (editor, true);
522:         setResizable (resizable, false);
523:         centreWithSize (getWidth(), getHeight());
524:         setVisible (true);
525:         keepAboveMainWindow (*this);
526:         crashguard::note (("editor shown: " + plugin.getName()).toStdString()); // (its OpenGL / GPU setup runs when shown)
527:         plugin.addListener (this); // the editor's gestures: Last tweaked, automation recording (REA round 143)
528:         startTimerHz (10);         // its moves without gestures (round 162d)
529:     }
530: 
531:     // round 157a: the window that holds a third-party editor draws LYNX's few pixels with GDI, not Direct2D (JUCE 8's
532:     // default). A plugin's OpenGL child window then sits in a classic redirected top-level window, as in every other
533:     // host — a Direct2D / DXGI swap-chain parent is where Intel's OpenGL driver crashed in SetPixelFormat (her
534:     // Auto-Tune Pro editor, 2026-10-25). (Re-applied whenever the native window is re-created.)
535:     void addToDesktop (int styleFlags, void* nativeWindowToAttachTo) override
536:     {
537:         DocumentWindow::addToDesktop (styleFlags, nativeWindowToAttachTo);
538:         useClassicRendering();
539:     }
540: 
541:     void useClassicRendering()
542:     {
543:         getProperties().set ("lynxGdi", true);
544:        #if JUCE_WINDOWS
545:         if (auto* peer = getPeer())
546:             if (const auto engines = peer->getAvailableRenderingEngines(); engines.size() > 1 && peer->getCurrentRenderingEngine() != 0)
547:                 peer->setCurrentRenderingEngine (0); // "Software Renderer" (GDI)
548:        #endif
549:     }

358: class ModulationPanel final : public juce::Component,
359:                               private juce::ChangeListener
360: {
361: public:
362:     ModulationPanel (AudioEngine& e, ChannelRef c)
363:         : engine (e),
364:           ref (std::move (c))
365:     {
366:         envelopes.onChange = [this] { apply(); };
367:         lfos.onChange = [this] { apply(); };
368:         addAndMakeVisible (envelopes);
369:         addAndMakeVisible (lfos);
370:         refresh();
371:         engine.addChangeListener (this);
372:         setSize (660, 20 + ModEnvelopeEditor::preferredHeight + 16 + LfoEditor::preferredHeight);
373:     }
374: 
375:     ~ModulationPanel() override { engine.removeChangeListener (this); }
376: 
377:     void resized() override
378:     {
379:         auto area = getLocalBounds().reduced (10);
380:         envelopes.setBounds (area.removeFromTop (ModEnvelopeEditor::preferredHeight));
381:         area.removeFromTop (16);
382:         lfos.setBounds (area.removeFromTop (LfoEditor::preferredHeight));
383:     }
384: 
385:     int getChannel() const noexcept { return ref.index; }
386: 
387: private:
388:     const ChannelDef* current() const
389:     {
390:         return ref.get (engine);
391:     }
392: 
393:     bool isSynth() const { return current() != nullptr && current()->instrument == InstrumentKind::synth; }
394: 
395:     void refresh()
396:     {
397:         const auto* c = current();
398: 
399:         if (c == nullptr)
400:             return;
401: 
402:         if (isSynth())
403:         {
404:             envelopes.show (c->synth.filterEnv, c->synth.pitchEnv);
405:             lfos.show (c->synth.lfo);
406:         }
407:         else
408:         {
409:             envelopes.show (c->sampler.filterEnv, c->sampler.pitchEnv);
410:             lfos.show (c->sampler.lfo);
411:         }
412: 
413:         // only its own window: as a tab of the channel's window it left that window titled "Modulation - ..." while the
414:         // Sampler page showed (issue #12)
415:         if (auto* w = findParentComponentOfClass<juce::DocumentWindow>(); w != nullptr && w->getContentComponent() == this)
416:             w->setName (tr ("Modulation - {0}", { juce::String::fromUTF8 (c->name.c_str()) }));
417:     }
418: 
419:     void apply()
420:     {
421:         if (current() == nullptr)
422:             return;
423: 
424:         const auto filter = envelopes.filter(), pitch = envelopes.pitch();
425:         const auto lfo = lfos.read();
426:         const auto synth = isSynth();
427: 
428:         ref.edit (engine, [synth, filter, pitch, lfo] (ChannelDef& channelDef) {
429:             auto& ch = channelDef;
430: 
431:             if (synth)
432:             {
433:                 ch.synth.filterEnv = filter;
434:                 ch.synth.pitchEnv = pitch;
435:                 ch.synth.lfo = lfo;
436:             }
437:             else
438:             {
439:                 ch.sampler.filterEnv = filter;
440:                 ch.sampler.pitchEnv = pitch;
441:                 ch.sampler.lfo = lfo;
442:             }
443:         });
444:     }
445: 
446:     void changeListenerCallback (juce::ChangeBroadcaster*) override { refresh(); }
447: 
448:     AudioEngine& engine;
449:     ChannelRef ref;
450:     ModEnvelopeEditor envelopes;
451:     LfoEditor lfos;
452: };
453: 
454: // A Sampler knob: right-click opens its automation menu instead of moving it (issue #12)
455: class MenuSlider final : public juce::Slider
456: {
457: public:
458:     std::function<void()> onMenu;
459: 
460:     void mouseDown (const juce::MouseEvent& e) override
461:     {
462:         menuClick = e.mods.isPopupMenu() && onMenu != nullptr;
463:         if (menuClick)
464:             onMenu();
465:         else
466:             juce::Slider::mouseDown (e);
467:     }
468:     void mouseDrag (const juce::MouseEvent& e) override
469:     {
470:         if (! menuClick)
471:             juce::Slider::mouseDrag (e);
472:     }
473:     void mouseUp (const juce::MouseEvent& e) override
474:     {
475:         if (! menuClick)
476:             juce::Slider::mouseUp (e);
477:         menuClick = false;
478:     }
479: 
480: private:
481:     bool menuClick = false;
482: };
