2823:     void load()
2824:     {
2825:         const auto* c = ref.get (engine);
2826:         if (c == nullptr)
2827:             return;
2828:         const juce::ScopedValueSetter<bool> guard (loading, true);
2829:         const auto& p = c->sampler.precompute;
2830:         removeDc.setToggleState (p.removeDc, juce::dontSendNotification);
2831:         normalize.setToggleState (p.normalize, juce::dontSendNotification);
2832:         polarity.setToggleState (p.reversePolarity, juce::dontSendNotification);
2833:         fadeStereo.setToggleState (p.fadeStereo, juce::dontSendNotification);
2834:         swapStereo.setToggleState (p.swapStereo, juce::dontSendNotification);
2835:         useLoops.setToggleState (c->sampler.useLoopPoints, juce::dontSendNotification);
2836:         pingPong.setToggleState (p.pingPong, juce::dontSendNotification);
2837:         fadeIn.setValue (p.fadeIn, juce::dontSendNotification);
2838:         fadeOut.setValue (p.fadeOut, juce::dontSendNotification);
2839:         crossfade.setValue (p.crossfade, juce::dontSendNotification);
2840:         trim.setValue (p.trim, juce::dontSendNotification);
2841:         boost.setValue (p.boost, juce::dontSendNotification);
2842:         eq.setValue (p.eq, juce::dontSendNotification);
2843:         ringMix.setValue (p.ringMix, juce::dontSendNotification);
2844:         ringFreq.setValue (p.ringFreq, juce::dontSendNotification);
2845:         pogo.setValue (p.pogo, juce::dontSendNotification);
2846:         cutoff.setValue (p.cutoff, juce::dontSendNotification);
2847:         resonance.setValue (p.resonance, juce::dontSendNotification);
2848:         stereoDelay.setValue (p.stereoDelay, juce::dontSendNotification);
2849:         reverbAmount.setValue (p.reverb, juce::dontSendNotification);
2850:         reverbType.setSelectedId (p.reverbType == 0 ? 1 : 2, juce::dontSendNotification);
2851:         clip.setToggleState (p.clip, juce::dontSendNotification);
2852:         declick.setSelectedId (juce::jlimit (0, 5, c->sampler.declickMode) + 1, juce::dontSendNotification);
2853:     }
2854: 
2855:     void apply()
2856:     {
2857:         if (loading)
2858:             return;
2859:         SamplePrecompute p;
2860:         p.removeDc = removeDc.getToggleState();
2861:         p.normalize = normalize.getToggleState();
2862:         p.reversePolarity = polarity.getToggleState();
2863:         p.fadeStereo = fadeStereo.getToggleState();
2864:         p.swapStereo = swapStereo.getToggleState();
2865:         p.pingPong = pingPong.getToggleState();
2866:         p.fadeIn = (int) fadeIn.getValue();
2867:         p.fadeOut = (int) fadeOut.getValue();
2868:         p.crossfade = (int) crossfade.getValue();
2869:         p.trim = (int) trim.getValue();
2870:         p.boost = (int) boost.getValue();
2871:         p.eq = (int) eq.getValue();
2872:         p.ringMix = (int) ringMix.getValue();
2873:         p.ringFreq = (int) ringFreq.getValue();
2874:         p.pogo = (int) pogo.getValue();
2875:         p.cutoff = (int) cutoff.getValue();
2876:         p.resonance = (int) resonance.getValue();
2877:         p.stereoDelay = (int) stereoDelay.getValue();
2878:         p.reverb = (int) reverbAmount.getValue();
2879:         p.reverbType = reverbType.getSelectedId() == 1 ? 0 : 1;
2880:         p.clip = clip.getToggleState();
2881:         const auto loops = useLoops.getToggleState();
2882:         const auto mode = juce::jlimit (0, 5, declick.getSelectedId() - 1);
2883:         const auto* c = ref.get (engine);
2884:         if (c == nullptr || (c->sampler.precompute == p && c->sampler.useLoopPoints == loops && c->sampler.declickMode == mode))
2885:             return;
2886:         ref.edit (engine, [p, loops, mode] (ChannelDef& d) {
2887:             d.sampler.precompute = p;
2888:             d.sampler.useLoopPoints = loops;
2889:             d.sampler.declickMode = mode;
2890:         });
2891:     }
2892: 
2893:     AudioEngine& engine;
2894:     ChannelRef ref;
2895:     bool loading = false;
2896:     juce::ToggleButton removeDc, normalize, polarity, fadeStereo, swapStereo, useLoops, pingPong;
2897:     juce::Slider fadeIn, fadeOut, crossfade, trim;
2898:     juce::Label fadeInLabel, fadeOutLabel, crossfadeLabel, trimLabel, declickLabel;
2899:     juce::ComboBox declick;
2900:     juce::Label legacyHeader;

2903:     juce::ComboBox reverbType;
2904:     juce::ToggleButton clip;
2905: };

3239:     // the Song's value of `param` (a knob that shows its automation must not write the automated value back)
3240:     static void keepSongValue (SamplerSettings& s, const SamplerSettings& song, int param) noexcept
3241:     {
3242:         switch (param)
3243:         {
3244:             case samplerparam::attack: s.attack = song.attack; break;
3245:             case samplerparam::decay: s.decay = song.decay; break;
3246:             case samplerparam::sustain: s.sustain = song.sustain; break;
3247:             case samplerparam::release: s.release = song.release; break;
3248:             case samplerparam::cutoff: s.cutoff = song.cutoff; break;
3249:             case samplerparam::resonance: s.resonance = song.resonance; break;
3250:             case samplerparam::start: s.regionStart = song.regionStart; break;
3251:             case samplerparam::end: s.regionEnd = song.regionEnd; break;
3252:             default: break;
3253:         }
3254:     }
3255: 
3256:     void apply (juce::Slider* changed = nullptr)
3257:     {
3258:         if (updating || current() == nullptr)
3259:             return;
3260: 
3261:         auto s = current()->sampler; // keeps what the panel does not show (slices)
3262:         s.pitch = (float) pitch.getValue();
3263:         s.gainDb = std::abs (gain.getValue()) < 0.05 ? 0.0f : (float) gain.getValue();
3264:         s.attack = (float) attack.getValue();
3265:         s.decay = (float) decay.getValue();
3266:         s.sustain = (float) sustain.getValue();
3267:         if (changed == &stretch || stretch.getValue() != shownStretch) // (unmoved: keep the exact value, not the knob's)
3268:             s.stretchBeats = (float) (shownStretch = stretch.getValue());
3269:         s.keepPitch = keepPitch.getToggleState();
3270:         s.stretchMethod = (StretchMethod) juce::jlimit (0, 2, stretchMethod.getSelectedId() - 1);
3271:         stretchMethod.setEnabled (s.keepPitch);
3272:         s.regionStart = (float) regionStart.getValue();
3273:         s.regionEnd = (float) regionEnd.getValue();
3274:         s.release = (float) release.getValue();
3275:         s.reverse = reverse.getToggleState();
3276:         s.cut = cut.getToggleState();
3277:         const auto cutChanged = s.cut != channelCutsItself (*current()); // (issue #68: the rack menu's cut-itself too)
3278:         s.porta = porta.getToggleState();
3279:         s.cutoff = (float) cutoff.getValue();
3280:         s.resonance = (float) resonance.getValue();
3281:         s.filterType = juce::jlimit (0, 3, filterType.getSelectedId() - 1);
3282:         for (const auto& [slider, param] : tweakTargets)
3283:             if (slider != changed && std::find (automatedShown.begin(), automatedShown.end(), slider) != automatedShown.end())
3284:                 keepSongValue (s, current()->sampler, param);
3285:         {
3286:             // an ADSR edited by hand replaces FL's imported volume envelope
3287:             const auto& old = current()->sampler;
3288:             auto moved = [] (float a, float b) { return std::abs (a - b) > 1.0e-4f; };
3289:             const auto adsr = moved (s.attack, old.attack) || moved (s.decay, old.decay) || moved (s.sustain, old.sustain) || moved (s.release, old.release);
3290:             if (adsr)
3291:                 s.flVolumeEnvelope.clear();
3292:             // so does an edited envelope or filter for FL's whole sampler voice (REA_rodada76)
3293:             if (adsr || moved (s.cutoff, old.cutoff) || moved (s.resonance, old.resonance) || s.filterType != old.filterType)
3294:                 s.flChannel.clear();
3295:         }
3296:         if (s == current()->sampler && ! cutChanged)
3297:             return;
3298: 
3299:         const auto before = current()->sampler;
3300:         ref.edit (engine, [s, cutChanged, index = ref.index] (ChannelDef& channelDef) {
3301:             channelDef.sampler = s;
3302:             if (cutChanged)
3303:                 setChannelCutsItself (channelDef, index, s.cut);
3304:         });
3305:         for (const auto& [slider, param] : tweakTargets)
3306:             if (std::abs (samplerparam::get (before, param) - samplerparam::get (s, param)) > 1.0e-6f)
3307:                 engine.noteTweaked ({ AutomationTargetKind::channelParam, ref.index, 0, param });
3308:     }

3331:     // FL's TIME knob menu (r168s c_f20): (none), Autodetect, Project tempo, 1 beat, 2 beats, 1 bar, 2 bars, 3 bars, 4 bars
3332:     struct StretchMenu final : juce::MouseListener
3333:     {
3334:         explicit StretchMenu (SettingsPanel& p) : panel (p) {}
3335:         void mouseDown (const juce::MouseEvent& e) override
3336:         {
3337:             if (e.mods.isPopupMenu())
3338:                 panel.showStretchMenu();
3339:         }
3340:         SettingsPanel& panel;
3341:     } stretchMenu { *this };
3342: 
3343:     void showStretchMenu()
3344:     {
3345:         const auto* c = current();
3346:         if (c == nullptr)
3347:             return;
3348:         const auto& song = engine.getSong();
3349:         const auto sample = engine.getSampleLibrary().load (juce::String::fromUTF8 (c->samplePath.c_str()));
3350:         const auto now = c->sampler.stretchBeats;
3351:         auto set = [this] (float beats) { ref.edit (engine, [beats] (ChannelDef& d) { d.sampler.stretchBeats = beats; }); };
3352:         juce::PopupMenu m;
3353:         m.addItem (tr ("(none)"), true, now <= 0.0f, [set] { set (0.0f); });
3354:         m.addItem (tr ("Autodetect"), sample != nullptr, false, [set, sample, bpm = song.bpm] { set (tempodetect::fitBeatsOf (sample.get(), bpm)); });
3355:         const auto atTempo = sample != nullptr && sample->sampleRate > 0.0 ? (float) ((double) sample->frames() / sample->sampleRate * song.bpm / 60.0) : 0.0f;
3356:         m.addItem (tr ("Project tempo"), atTempo > 0.0f, false, [set, atTempo] { set (atTempo); });
3357:         for (const auto preset : clipops::timePresetBeats (song.beatsPerBar))
3358:         {
3359:             const auto bars = song.beatsPerBar > 0 && preset >= (float) song.beatsPerBar ? preset / (float) song.beatsPerBar : 0.0f;
3360:             const auto label = bars > 0.0f ? (bars == 1.0f ? tr ("1 bar") : tr ("{0} bars", { juce::String (bars, 0) }))
3361:                                            : (preset == 1.0f ? tr ("1 beat") : tr ("{0} beats", { juce::String (preset, 0) }));
3362:             m.addItem (label, true, now == preset, [set, preset] { set (preset); });
3363:         }
3364:         m.showMenuAsync (juce::PopupMenu::Options().withTargetComponent (&stretch));
3365:     }
3366:     MenuSlider attack, release, decay, sustain, regionStart, regionEnd, cutoff, resonance;
3367:     std::vector<std::pair<MenuSlider*, int>> tweakTargets; // knob -> samplerparam
3368:     std::vector<juce::Slider*> automatedShown;             // knobs showing their automation's value now

=== ChannelSettings 4000-4160 ===
4000:             regionLow = low;
4001:             regionHigh = high;
4002:             repaint();
4003:         }
4004:     }
4005: 
4006:     void paint (juce::Graphics& g) override
4007:     {
4008:         using namespace theme;
4009:         const auto w = whiteWidth();
4010:         const auto top = (float) rulerHeight();
4011:         const auto keysH = (float) getHeight() - top;
4012:         if (onRegion)
4013:         {
4014:             const auto ruler = juce::Rectangle<float> (0.0f, 0.0f, (float) getWidth(), top - 2.0f);
4015:             g.setColour (colour::bg0);
4016:             g.fillRect (ruler);
4017:             const auto full = regionLow <= 0 && regionHigh >= 127;
4018:             const auto x0 = keyLeft (regionLow), x1 = keyRight (regionHigh);
4019:             g.setColour (full ? colour::bg5 : colour::accent);
4020:             g.fillRect (juce::Rectangle<float> (x0, 1.0f, juce::jmax (2.0f, x1 - x0), ruler.getHeight() - 2.0f));
4021:         }
4022:         for (int k = 0, white = 0; k < 128; ++k)
4023:             if (! isBlack (k))
4024:             {
4025:                 const auto r = juce::Rectangle<float> ((float) (white++) * w, top, w - 1.0f, keysH);
4026:                 const auto outside = onRegion && (k < regionLow || k > regionHigh);
4027:                 g.setColour (k == root ? colour::accent : k == hover ? juce::Colour (0xffc8ccd4) : outside ? juce::Colour (0xff9a9ea8) : juce::Colour (0xffe6e8ec));
4028:                 g.fillRect (r);
4029:                 if (k % 12 == 0 && w * 7.0f >= 14.0f) // the octave's C named (FL's C5 = 60)
4030:                 {
4031:                     g.setColour (k == root ? colour::onAccent : juce::Colour (0xff5a5f6a));
4032:                     g.setFont (font (8.5f));
4033:                     g.drawText ("C" + juce::String (k / 12), r.withTrimmedTop (r.getHeight() - 11.0f).expanded (6.0f, 0.0f).toNearestInt(),
4034:                                 juce::Justification::centred);
4035:                 }
4036:             }
4037:         for (int k = 0; k < 128; ++k)
4038:             if (isBlack (k))
4039:             {
4040:                 const auto x = (float) whitesBefore (k) * w - w * 0.32f;
4041:                 g.setColour (k == root ? colour::accent : k == hover ? colour::bg5 : colour::bg0);
4042:                 g.fillRect (juce::Rectangle<float> (x, top, w * 0.64f, keysH * 0.6f));
4043:             }
4044:     }
4045: 
4046:     void mouseMove (const juce::MouseEvent& e) override
4047:     {
4048:         const auto onRuler = onRegion && e.position.y < (float) rulerHeight();
4049:         if (const auto k = keyAt (e.position); k != hover || onRuler != hoverRuler)
4050:         {
4051:             hover = k;
4052:             hoverRuler = onRuler;
4053:             if (onRuler)
4054:                 setTooltip (tr ("Key region: {0} to {1} (drag to set, double-click for the whole keyboard)",
4055:                                 { keyName (regionLow), keyName (regionHigh) }));
4056:             else
4057:                 setTooltip (k >= 0 ? tr ("Root note: {0}", { keyName (k) }) : juce::String());
4058:             repaint();
4059:         }
4060:     }
4061:     void mouseExit (const juce::MouseEvent&) override
4062:     {
4063:         hover = -1;
4064:         repaint();
4065:     }
4066:     void mouseDown (const juce::MouseEvent& e) override
4067:     {
4068:         dragFrom = -1;
4069:         if (onRegion && e.position.y < (float) rulerHeight())
4070:         {
4071:             dragFrom = keyAtX (e.position.x);
4072:             if (dragFrom >= 0)
4073:                 onRegion (dragFrom, dragFrom);
4074:             return;
4075:         }
4076:         if (const auto k = keyAt (e.position); k >= 0 && onPick)
4077:             onPick (k);
4078:     }
4079:     void mouseDrag (const juce::MouseEvent& e) override
4080:     {
4081:         if (dragFrom < 0 || ! onRegion)
4082:             return;
4083:         const auto k = keyAtX (e.position.x);
4084:         if (k >= 0)
4085:             onRegion (juce::jmin (dragFrom, k), juce::jmax (dragFrom, k));
4086:     }
4087:     void mouseDoubleClick (const juce::MouseEvent& e) override
4088:     {
4089:         if (onRegion && e.position.y < (float) rulerHeight())
4090:             onRegion (0, 127);
4091:     }
4092: 
4093:     static juce::String keyName (int key)
4094:     {
4095:         return juce::String (notenames::keyName (key)); // Settings › General › Note names (round 165f)
4096:     }
4097: 
4098: private:
4099:     static bool isBlack (int k) noexcept
4100:     {
4101:         const auto n = k % 12;
4102:         return n == 1 || n == 3 || n == 6 || n == 8 || n == 10;
4103:     }
4104:     static int whitesBefore (int k) noexcept
4105:     {
4106:         int count = 0;
4107:         for (int i = 0; i < k; ++i)
4108:             count += isBlack (i) ? 0 : 1;
4109:         return count;
4110:     }
4111:     int rulerHeight() const noexcept { return onRegion ? 9 : 0; }
4112:     float whiteWidth() const noexcept { return (float) getWidth() / 75.0f; } // 75 white keys in 0…127
4113:     float keyLeft (int k) const noexcept
4114:     {
4115:         const auto w = whiteWidth();
4116:         return isBlack (k) ? (float) whitesBefore (k) * w - w * 0.32f : (float) whitesBefore (k) * w;
4117:     }
4118:     float keyRight (int k) const noexcept
4119:     {
4120:         const auto w = whiteWidth();
4121:         return isBlack (k) ? keyLeft (k) + w * 0.64f : keyLeft (k) + w;
4122:     }
4123:     // the key under x on the ruler (each key owns its own horizontal span; black keys win where they overlap)
4124:     int keyAtX (float x) const noexcept
4125:     {
4126:         for (int k = 0; k < 128; ++k)
4127:             if (isBlack (k) && x >= keyLeft (k) && x < keyRight (k))
4128:                 return k;
4129:         const auto white = (int) std::floor (x / whiteWidth());
4130:         for (int k = 0, n = 0; k < 128; ++k)
4131:             if (! isBlack (k) && n++ == white)
4132:                 return k;
4133:         return x < 0.0f ? 0 : 127;
4134:     }
4135:     int keyAt (juce::Point<float> p) const noexcept
4136:     {
4137:         const auto w = whiteWidth();
4138:         const auto top = (float) rulerHeight();
4139:         if (p.y < top)
4140:             return keyAtX (p.x);
4141:         if (p.y < top + ((float) getHeight() - top) * 0.6f)
4142:             for (int k = 0; k < 128; ++k)
4143:                 if (isBlack (k))
4144:                 {
4145:                     const auto x = (float) whitesBefore (k) * w - w * 0.32f;
4146:                     if (p.x >= x && p.x < x + w * 0.64f)
4147:                         return k;
4148:                 }
4149:         const auto white = (int) std::floor (p.x / w);
4150:         for (int k = 0, n = 0; k < 128; ++k)
4151:             if (! isBlack (k) && n++ == white)
4152:                 return k;
4153:         return -1;
4154:     }
4155: 
4156:     int root = 60, hover = -1, dragFrom = -1;
4157:     int regionLow = 0, regionHigh = 127;
4158:     bool hoverRuler = false;
4159: };

4728:         for (auto* b : { &prev, &next })
4729:         {
4730:             b->setTooltip (b == &prev ? tr ("Previous preset") : tr ("Next preset"));
4731:             b->setWantsKeyboardFocus (false);
4732:             addAndMakeVisible (*b);
4733:         }
4734:         prev.onClick = [this] { step (-1); };
4735:         next.onClick = [this] { step (1); };
4736:         ui::setHint (*this, tr ("Presets: [LMB] the list, the arrows load the previous / next one"));
4737:     }
4738: 
4739:     // Whether this channel's instrument has an FL preset folder (the bar is shown only then)
4740:     static bool wanted (const AudioEngine& engine, int channel)
4741:     {
4742:         const auto& song = engine.getSong();
4743:         if (! juce::isPositiveAndBelow (channel, (int) song.channels.size()))
4744:             return false;
4745:         const auto kind = song.channels[(std::size_t) channel].instrument;
4746:         return ! natives::presetFolderOf (kind).empty() || banks::hasBank (kind);
4747:     }
4748: 
4749:     void resized() override
4750:     {
4751:         auto r = getLocalBounds();
4752:         prev.setBounds (r.removeFromLeft (24));
4753:         next.setBounds (r.removeFromRight (24));
4754:         nameArea = r.reduced (4, 0);
4755:     }
4756: 
4757:     void paint (juce::Graphics& g) override
4758:     {
4759:         g.setColour (theme::colour::bg2);
4760:         g.fillRoundedRectangle (nameArea.toFloat(), theme::radius::sm);
4761:         g.setColour (current >= 0 ? theme::colour::textPrimary : theme::colour::textSecondary);
4762:         g.setFont (theme::font (theme::type::body));
4763:         g.drawText (current >= 0 ? currentName : tr ("Presets"), nameArea.reduced (6, 0), juce::Justification::centredLeft, true);
4764:     }
4765: 
4766:     void mouseUp (const juce::MouseEvent& e) override
4767:     {
4768:         if (nameArea.contains (e.getPosition()) && ! e.mouseWasDraggedSinceMouseDown())
4769:             showMenu();
4770:     }
4771: 
4772: private:
4773:     const ChannelDef* def() const
4774:     {
4775:         const auto& song = engine.getSong();
4776:         return juce::isPositiveAndBelow (channel, (int) song.channels.size()) ? &song.channels[(std::size_t) channel] : nullptr;
4777:     }
4778: 
4779:     const std::vector<presetcycle::Preset>& list()
4780:     {
4781:         const auto* d = def();
4782:         const auto kind = d != nullptr ? d->instrument : InstrumentKind::synth;
4783:         if (! loaded || kind != listedKind)
4784:         {
4785:             const auto folder = natives::presetFolderOf (kind);
4786:             presets = folder.empty() ? std::vector<presetcycle::Preset>()
4787:                                      : presetcycle::listPresets (presetcycle::pluginPresetFolders (flStudioAccess().roots, true, folder));
4788:             // LYNX's own bank after FL's presets (round 168n): the only presets without FL Studio
4789:             const auto& bank = banks::bankOf (kind);
4790:             for (int i = 0; i < (int) bank.size(); ++i)
4791:                 presets.push_back ({ banks::pathOf (i), banks::relativeOf (bank[(std::size_t) i]), bank[(std::size_t) i].name });
4792:             listedKind = kind;
4793:             loaded = true;
4794:             current = d != nullptr ? presetcycle::indexOfName (presets, d->name) : -1; // (a channel named after its preset)
4795:             currentName = current >= 0 ? juce::String::fromUTF8 (presets[(std::size_t) current].name.c_str()) : juce::String();
4796:         }
4797:         return presets;
4798:     }
4799: 
4800:     void step (int delta)
4801:     {
4802:         const auto& all = list();
4803:         const auto count = (int) all.size();
4804:         // FL's next index; a preset LYNX can't read (an unknown or damaged state) is passed over in the same
4805:         // direction, so the arrows keep going
4806:         auto at = presetcycle::step (current, delta, count);
4807:         for (int tries = 0; at >= 0 && tries < count; ++tries)
4808:         {
4809:             if (load (at, tries == count - 1))
4810:                 return;
4811:             at = presetcycle::step (at, delta, count);
4812:         }
4813:     }
4814: 
4815:     bool load (int index, bool reportFailure = true)
4816:     {
4817:         const auto& all = list();
4818:         const auto* d = def();
4819:         if (d == nullptr || ! juce::isPositiveAndBelow (index, (int) all.size()))
4820:             return false;
4821:         const auto& preset = all[(std::size_t) index];
4822:         const auto bankIndex = banks::indexOfPath (preset.path);
4823:         auto changed = bankIndex >= 0 ? banks::withPreset (*d, bankIndex) : natives::withPreset (*d, preset.path, preset.name, flStudioAccess());
4824:         if (! changed)
4825:         {
4826:             if (reportFailure)
4827:                 ui::postHint ({ tr ("This preset could not be read: {0}", { juce::String::fromUTF8 (preset.name.c_str()) }), {} }, 2500);
4828:             current = index; // (the next step goes on from it)
4829:             return false;
4830:         }
4831:         const auto kind = d->instrument;
4832:         const auto oldName = juce::String::fromUTF8 (d->name.c_str());
4833:         engine.editSong ([ch = channel, kind, c = std::move (*changed)] (Song& s) {
4834:             if (ch < (int) s.channels.size() && s.channels[(std::size_t) ch].instrument == kind)
4835:                 s.channels[(std::size_t) ch] = c;
4836:         });
4837:         if (onLoaded != nullptr)
4838:             onLoaded (oldName, juce::String::fromUTF8 (preset.name.c_str()));
4839:         current = index;
4840:         currentName = juce::String::fromUTF8 (preset.name.c_str());
4841:         ui::postHint ({ currentName, juce::String (presetcycle::positionText (index, (int) all.size())) }, 2500);
4842:         repaint();
4843:         return true;
4844:     }
4845: 
4846:     void showMenu()
4847:     {
4848:         const auto& all = list();
4849:         juce::PopupMenu menu;
4850:         std::map<std::string, juce::PopupMenu> folders;
4851:         std::vector<std::string> order;
4852:         for (int i = 0; i < (int) all.size(); ++i)
4853:         {
4854:             const auto& rel = all[(std::size_t) i].relative;
4855:             const auto slash = rel.find_last_of ('/');
4856:             const auto folder = slash == std::string::npos ? std::string() : rel.substr (0, slash);
4857:             if (folders.find (folder) == folders.end())
4858:                 order.push_back (folder);
4859:             folders[folder].addItem (juce::String::fromUTF8 (all[(std::size_t) i].name.c_str()), true, i == current, [this, i] { load (i); });
4860:         }
4861:         for (const auto& folder : order)
4862:             if (folder.empty())
4863:                 for (juce::PopupMenu::MenuItemIterator it (folders[folder]); it.next();)
4864:                     menu.addItem (it.getItem());
4865:             else
4866:                 menu.addSubMenu (juce::String::fromUTF8 (folder.c_str()), folders[folder]);
4867:         if (menu.getNumItems() == 0)
4868:             menu.addItem (tr ("No presets found (they come with FL Studio)"), false, false, [] {});
4869:         menu.showMenuAsync (juce::PopupMenu::Options().withTargetComponent (this));
4870:     }
4871: 
4872:     AudioEngine& engine;
4873:     int channel;
4874:     juce::TextButton prev { juce::String::fromUTF8 ("\xe2\x97\x80") }, next { juce::String::fromUTF8 ("\xe2\x96\xb6") };
4875:     juce::Rectangle<int> nameArea;
4876:     std::vector<presetcycle::Preset> presets;
4877:     InstrumentKind listedKind = InstrumentKind::synth;
4878:     bool loaded = false;
4879:     int current = -1;
4880:     juce::String currentName;
4881: };

5093:     void presetLoaded (bool hasPage, const juce::String& oldName, const juce::String& newName)
5094:     {

5105:             if (const auto title = window->getName(); oldName.isNotEmpty() && title.startsWith (oldName))
5106:                 window->setName (newName + title.substring (oldName.length()));
5107:     }

5184:         for (std::size_t i = 0; i < pages.size(); ++i)
5185:         {
5186:             const auto w = juce::jmin (body.getWidth(), sizes[i].x), h = juce::jmin (body.getHeight(), sizes[i].y);
5187:             pages[i]->setBounds (body.getX() + (body.getWidth() - w) / 2, body.getY(), w, h);
5188:         }
5189:     }
5190: 
5191: private:
5192:     void setMinimized (bool shouldMinimize)
5193:     {
5194:         if (node || minimized == shouldMinimize)
5195:             return;
5196: 
5197:         minimized = shouldMinimize;
5198:         minimiseButton.setIcon (minimized ? Icon::maximise : Icon::minimise);
5199:         minimiseButton.setTooltip (minimized ? tr ("Restore editor") : tr ("Minimize editor"));
5200:         for (int i = 0; i < (int) pages.size(); ++i)
5201:             pages[(std::size_t) i]->setVisible (! minimized && i == current);
5202: 
5203:         setSize (expandedWidth, minimized ? headerHeight : (headerVisible ? expandedHeight : juce::jmax (1, expandedHeight - headerHeight)));
5204:         if (auto* window = findParentComponentOfClass<juce::ResizableWindow>())
5205:             if (auto* content = window->getContentComponent())
5206:             {
5207:                 window->setContentComponentSize (content->getWidth(), content->getHeight());
5208:                 fitWindowOnScreen (*window);
5209:             }
5210:     }
5211: 
5212:     void refresh()
5213:     {
5214:         if (channel < (int) engine.getSong().channels.size())
5215:             on.setToggleState (! engine.getSong().channels[(std::size_t) channel].muted, juce::dontSendNotification);
5216:         for (auto& k : header)
5217:             k->repaint();
5218:     }
5219: 
5220:     void changeListenerCallback (juce::ChangeBroadcaster*) override { refresh(); }
5221: 
5222:     AudioEngine& engine;
5223:     int channel;
5224:     bool node = false;
5225:     int current = 0;
5226:     std::vector<std::unique_ptr<juce::TextButton>> tabs;
5227:     std::vector<std::unique_ptr<juce::Component>> pages;
5228:     std::vector<juce::Point<int>> sizes;
5229:     std::vector<std::unique_ptr<FrameKnob>> header;
5230:     juce::ToggleButton on;
5231:     IconButton minimiseButton { "Minimize editor", Icon::minimise }, closeButton { "Close editor", Icon::close };
5232:     std::unique_ptr<ChannelPresetBar> presetBar;
5233:     juce::TextButton toggleHeaderButton;
5234:     bool headerVisible = true;
5235:     bool instrumentPageFirst = false;
5236:     bool minimized = false;
5237:     int expandedWidth = 0, expandedHeight = 0;
5238: };
5239: 
5240: bool hasEnvelopeTab (InstrumentKind kind) noexcept
5241: {
5242:     return kind == InstrumentKind::synth || kind == InstrumentKind::sampler;
5243: }
5244: 
5245: void showModulationFor (juce::Component& from, AudioEngine& engine, int channel)
5246: {
5247:     if (auto* frame = from.findParentComponentOfClass<ChannelFrame>(); frame != nullptr && frame->tabCount() >= 2)
5248:         frame->showTab (1);
5249:     else if (channel >= 0)
5250:         openChannelModulation (engine, channel);
5251: }
5252: 
5253: bool isModelKind (InstrumentKind kind) noexcept
5254: {
5255:     return kind == InstrumentKind::sakura || kind == InstrumentKind::drumpad || kind == InstrumentKind::boobass
5256:            || kind == InstrumentKind::drumsynth || kind == InstrumentKind::harmless || kind == InstrumentKind::harmor
5257:            || kind == InstrumentKind::gms || kind == InstrumentKind::morphine
5258:            || kind == InstrumentKind::ogun || kind == InstrumentKind::minisynth
5259:            || kind == InstrumentKind::dx10 || kind == InstrumentKind::kepler || kind == InstrumentKind::keplerExo || kind == InstrumentKind::poizone
5260:            || kind == InstrumentKind::fruitkick || kind == InstrumentKind::bassdrum || kind == InstrumentKind::toxic
5261:            || kind == InstrumentKind::threeosc
5262:            || kind == InstrumentKind::autogun || kind == InstrumentKind::transistorbass
5263:            || kind == InstrumentKind::simsynth || kind == InstrumentKind::wasp || kind == InstrumentKind::plucked
5264:            || kind == InstrumentKind::beepmap || kind == InstrumentKind::sawer || kind == InstrumentKind::flkeys
5265:            || kind == InstrumentKind::granulizer || kind == InstrumentKind::drumaxx
5266:            || kind == InstrumentKind::slayer || kind == InstrumentKind::fruitydance || kind == InstrumentKind::sytrus;
5267: }
5268: 
5269: // The instrument page of a channel or a Patcher node (null: none of its own)
5270: std::unique_ptr<juce::Component> makeInstrumentPage (AudioEngine& engine, const ChannelRef& ref)
5271: {
5272:     const auto* def = ref.get (engine);
5273:     if (def == nullptr)
5274:         return nullptr;
5275:     const auto kind = def->instrument;
5276:     if (kind == InstrumentKind::fruitydance && ref.index >= 0)
5277:         return makeDanceEditor (engine, ref.index); // round 165e
5278:     if (kind == InstrumentKind::videoplayer)
5279:         return makeVideoPlayerEditor (engine, ref.locate, ref.index); // round 165g
5280:     if (kind == InstrumentKind::wavetraveller)
5281:         return makeWaveTravellerEditor (engine, ref.locate, ref.index); // round 167f
5282:     if (kind == InstrumentKind::synth)
5283:         return std::make_unique<SynthPanel> (engine, ref);
5284:     if (isModelKind (kind))
5285:         return std::make_unique<ModelPanel> (engine, ref);
5286:     if (kind == InstrumentKind::sampler)
5287:         return std::make_unique<SettingsPanel> (engine, ref);
5288:     return nullptr;
5289: }
5290: 
5291: // Where "Align all channel editors" put every channel editor (FL stores it per channel, so editors opened later come
5292: // up there too); empty = centred, as before.
5293: std::optional<juce::Point<int>>& alignedChannelEditorPosition()
5294: {
5295:     static std::optional<juce::Point<int>> at;
5296:     return at;
5297: }

5299: class SettingsWindow final : public juce::DocumentWindow
5300: {
5301: public:
5302:     SettingsWindow (AudioEngine& engine, int c, bool modulationWindow)
5303:         : juce::DocumentWindow (tr ("Channel settings"), theme::colour::bg2, DocumentWindow::closeButton),
5304:           channel (c),
5305:           modulation (modulationWindow)
5306:     {
5307:         setUsingNativeTitleBar (true);
5308:         configureSecondaryWindowControls (*this);
5309:         // FL names the window after the channel and its mixer track (REA round 149 [EXEC]: "FL Studio VSTi (Master)",
5310:         // "Synth Bass (Synth Bass)")
5311:         if (const auto& song = engine.getSong(); channel >= 0 && channel < (int) song.channels.size())
5312:         {
5313:             const auto& def = song.channels[(std::size_t) channel];
5314:             const auto t = def.mixerTrack;
5315:             const auto track = t <= 0 || t >= (int) song.mixer.size() ? tr ("Master")
5316:                                : song.mixer[(std::size_t) t].name.empty() ? tr ("Insert {0}", { juce::String (t) })
5317:                                                                           : juce::String::fromUTF8 (song.mixer[(std::size_t) t].name.c_str());
5318:             setName (juce::String::fromUTF8 (def.name.c_str()) + " (" + track + ")"
5319:                      + (modulation ? " - " + tr ("Envelope / instrument") : juce::String()));
5320:         }
5321: 
5322:         std::unique_ptr<juce::Component> content;
5323:         if (modulation)
5324:             content = std::make_unique<ModulationPanel> (engine, ChannelRef::rack (channel));
5325:         else if (! hasChannelSettings (engine.getSong().channels[(std::size_t) channel].instrument))
5326:             content = std::make_unique<ChannelFrame> (engine, ChannelRef::rack (channel), nullptr, nullptr); // the Misc tab alone
5327:         else
5328:             content = makeChannelFrame (engine, channel);
5329: 
5330:         // Native instrument pages such as Sakura are intentionally larger than their desktop design canvas. On a
5331:         // tablet with less available height, keep the page reachable inside a screen-fitted scrolling viewport.
5332:         setContentOwned (fitContentToScreen (std::move (content)).release(), true);
5333: 
5334:         centreWithSize (getWidth(), getHeight());
5335:         if (! modulation)
5336:             if (const auto& at = alignedChannelEditorPosition(); at.has_value())
5337:                 setTopLeftPosition (*at); // "Align all channel editors" set every channel's place (round 169k)
5338:         setVisible (true);
5339:         keepAboveMainWindow (*this);
5340:     }
5341: 
5342:     int getChannel() const { return channel; }
5343:     bool isModulation() const { return modulation; }
5344:     void closeButtonPressed() override;
5345: 
5346: private:
5347:     int channel;
5348:     bool modulation;
5349: };

5351: std::vector<std::unique_ptr<SettingsWindow>>& windows()
5352: {
5353:     static std::vector<std::unique_ptr<SettingsWindow>> open;
5354:     return open;
5355: }
5356: 
5357: void SettingsWindow::closeButtonPressed()
5358: {
5359:     auto* self = this;
5360:     juce::MessageManager::callAsync ([self] {
5361:         auto& open = windows();
5362:         open.erase (std::remove_if (open.begin(), open.end(), [self] (auto& w) { return w.get() == self; }), open.end());
5363:     });
5364: }
5365: } // namespace
5366: 
5367: namespace
5368: {
5369: void openWindow (AudioEngine& engine, int channel, bool modulation)
5370: {
5371:     for (auto& w : windows())
5372:         if (w->getChannel() == channel && w->isModulation() == modulation)
5373:         {
5374:             w->toFront (true);
5375:             return;
5376:         }
5377: 
5378:     windows().push_back (std::make_unique<SettingsWindow> (engine, channel, modulation));
5379: }
5380: } // namespace
5381: 
5382: void alignAllChannelEditors (int selectedChannel)
5383: {
5384:     // FL's AlignAllChannelEditorsAction (FUN_012317c0, REA_rodada169k §2.4): the focused channel editor (else the
5385:     // selected channel's, else the first open one) gives the place; every channel editor goes there.
5386:     auto& open = windows();
5387:     SettingsWindow* reference = nullptr;
5388:     for (auto& w : open)
5389:         if (! w->isModulation() && w->isActiveWindow())
5390:             reference = w.get();
5391:     for (auto& w : open)
5392:         if (reference == nullptr && ! w->isModulation() && w->getChannel() == selectedChannel)
5393:             reference = w.get();
5394:     for (auto& w : open)
5395:         if (reference == nullptr && ! w->isModulation())
5396:             reference = w.get();
5397:     if (reference == nullptr)
5398:         return;
5399:     const auto at = reference->getPosition();
5400:     alignedChannelEditorPosition() = at;
5401:     for (auto& w : open)
5402:         if (! w->isModulation())
5403:             w->setTopLeftPosition (at);
5404:     reference->toFront (true);
5405: }
5406: 
5407: void openChannelSettings (AudioEngine& engine, int channel)
5408: {
5409:     openWindow (engine, channel, false);
5410: }
5411: 
5412: void openChannelMisc (AudioEngine& engine, int channel)
5413: {
5414:     openWindow (engine, channel, false);
5415:     for (auto& w : windows())
5416:         if (w->getChannel() == channel && ! w->isModulation())
5417:             if (auto* frame = dynamic_cast<ChannelFrame*> (w->getContentComponent()))
5418:                 frame->showTab (frame->tabCount() - 1);
5419: }
5420: 
5421: void openChannelModulation (AudioEngine& engine, int channel)
5422: {
5423:     openWindow (engine, channel, true);
5424: }
5425: 
5426: void closeChannelSettings()
5427: {
5428:     windows().clear();
5429: }

=== src/ui/Icons.h ===
1: #pragma once
2: 
3: #include <juce_gui_basics/juce_gui_basics.h>
4: 
5: // LYNX's own vector icons, drawn with juce::Path on a 24x24 grid. Original designs, one family:
6: // - live area 3..21 (a 3-unit margin), coordinates on half units so 24 px and 16 px stay crisp;
7: // - line icons share one fine stroke (strokeWidth(): about 1.25-1.5 px at toolbar sizes), round caps and joins;
8: // - transport symbols and small accents (a lit step, a note, a dot) are solid fills.
9: namespace lynx
10: {
11: enum class Icon
12: {
13:     none,
14:     play, pause, stop, record, chevronDown, more, plus, check, close, minimise, maximise,
15:     pattern, song, metronome,
16:     newFile, open, save, exportAudio, settings, help, undo, redo,
17:     channelRack, pianoRoll, playlist, mixer, browser,
18:     folder, file, audioFile, plugin, preset, project,
19:     draw, erase, select, slice, mute, link, magnet, scissors,
20:     // workspace and control contract
21:     detach, dock, restore, keyboard, automate, rightClick, tap,
22:     brush, knife, zoom, listen, slip, ghost, // editor tools (Piano Roll / Playlist)
23:     panelMenu, pluginPicker, mic, // window options menu, plugin picker, recording options
24:     countIn, waitInput, // recording: count-in and wait for input
25:     mouseLeft, mouseMiddle, mouseWheel, // hint bar gestures (with rightClick)
26:     tuningFork, // master pitch
27:     drumBrush,  // Piano Roll: the drum paint tool
28:     graph,      // Channel Rack: the step graph editor (velocity, pan... bars)
29:     follow,     // Playlist / Piano Roll: the view follows the playhead (auto scrolling)
30:     loop,       // Channel Rack: channel looping (round 165f-rack)
31:     dice,       // Channel Rack: Loop Starter (round 168b)
32:     stretch,    // Playlist: stretch audio when resizing audio clips (round 168f)
33:     scaleSnap,  // Piano Roll: snap to the scale (a small magnet over keys; round 170e)
34:     tweak,      // the toolbar's last tweaked parameter: a knob with a turning arrow (round 170e)
35:     slide,      // Piano Roll: new notes slide (a note with a glide arrow; round 170e)
36:     chord,      // Piano Roll: the chord stamp (three stacked notes; round 170e)
37:     span,       // Channel Rack: the pattern length (an arrow between two bar lines; round 170e)
38:     blend,      // recording: blend recorded notes into the pattern (two lines merging into one; round 170e2)
39:     filter,     // a funnel: filters a list (Channel Rack's channel filter group; round 170e2)
40:     tapTempo,   // the tempo tapper: a fingertip tapping a pad (round 170e2)
41:     loopRecord, // recording: loop record (a loop arrow around a record dot; round 170e3)
42:     stepEdit,   // recording: step edit (steps typed one after another, an arrow to the next; round 170e3)
43:     punch,      // recording: punch in/out on the loop region (a record dot between two brackets; round 170e3)
44:     copy,       // Piano Roll: copy notes
45:     paste       // Piano Roll: paste notes
46: };
47: constexpr int iconCount = (int) Icon::paste + 1;
48: 
49: namespace icons
50: {
51: struct Shape
52: {
53:     juce::Path stroke; // drawn with a line (stroke width scales with the size)
54:     juce::Path fill;   // solid parts
55: };
56: 
57: const Shape& get (Icon icon);
58: 
59: // The line width of the icons drawn `size` pixels square.
60: float strokeWidth (float size);
61: 
62: // Draws `icon` fitted (centred, square) into `area`.
63: void draw (juce::Graphics& g, Icon icon, juce::Rectangle<float> area, juce::Colour colour);
64: } // namespace icons
65: 
66: // A flat button that shows an icon, optionally followed by text. Uses the theme colours:
67: // neutral at rest, red accent when toggled on (or when `primary`).
68: //
69: // Hover and press states fade in and out (about 120 ms) instead of switching at once.
70: class IconButton : public juce::Button,
71:                    private juce::Timer
72: {
73: public:
74:     IconButton (const juce::String& name, Icon icon, const juce::String& text = {});
75: 
76:     void setIcon (Icon newIcon);
77:     void setText (const juce::String& newText);
78:     void setPrimary (bool shouldBePrimary);              // filled with the accent even when off
79:     void setOnColour (juce::Colour colour);              // background when toggled on (default: accent)
80:     void setIconColourWhenOn (juce::Colour colour);      // icon/text colour when on (default: white)
81:     void setIconColour (juce::Colour colour);            // icon colour at rest (default: the theme's secondary text)
82:     void setFlat (bool shouldBeFlat);                    // no background at rest (toolbar style)
83:     void setCorners (bool roundLeft, bool roundRight);   // for segmented groups
84:     int getIdealWidth (int height) const;
85:     // The icon's box: `fraction` of the button's height, at most `maxPx` (default 0.66 and 20 px). Icons are
86:     // drawn on whole pixels, so 16, 20 and 24 px boxes stay crisp.
87:     void setIconSizing (float fraction, float maxPx);
88: 
89:     // Optional extra gestures: when set, a right-click calls onRightClick instead of clicking the button,
90:     // and a double-click calls onDoubleClick (after the two single clicks).
91:     std::function<void()> onRightClick, onDoubleClick;
92: 
93:     void mouseDown (const juce::MouseEvent&) override;
94:     void mouseUp (const juce::MouseEvent&) override;
95:     void mouseDoubleClick (const juce::MouseEvent&) override;
96: 
97:     void paintButton (juce::Graphics&, bool highlighted, bool down) override;
98:     void buttonStateChanged() override;
99: 
100: private:
101:     void timerCallback() override;
102:     float hover = 0.0f; // 0 = at rest, 1 = under the mouse (animated)
103:     Icon icon;
104:     juce::String text;
105:     bool primary = false, flat = false, roundLeft = true, roundRight = true;
106:     float iconFraction = 0.66f, iconMax = 20.0f;
107:     juce::Colour onColour, iconOnColour, iconColour;
108: };
109: } // namespace lynx


=== src/ui/Icons.cpp ===
1: #include "ui/Icons.h"
2: 
3: #include <cmath>
4: #include <map>
5: 
6: #include "ui/Theme.h"
7: 
8: namespace lynx
9: {
10: namespace icons
11: {
12: namespace
13: {
14: constexpr float pi = juce::MathConstants<float>::pi;
15: 
16: void line (juce::Path& p, float x1, float y1, float x2, float y2)
17: {
18:     p.startNewSubPath (x1, y1);
19:     p.lineTo (x2, y2);
20: }
21: 
22: void roundRect (juce::Path& p, float x, float y, float w, float h, float r)
23: {
24:     p.addRoundedRectangle (x, y, w, h, r);
25: }
26: 
27: void polyline (juce::Path& p, std::initializer_list<juce::Point<float>> points, bool close = false)
28: {
29:     bool first = true;
30:     for (auto pt : points)
31:     {
32:         if (first)
33:             p.startNewSubPath (pt);
34:         else
35:             p.lineTo (pt);
36:         first = false;
37:     }
38:     if (close)
39:         p.closeSubPath();
40: }
41: 
42: Shape make (Icon icon)
43: {
44:     Shape s;
45:     auto& st = s.stroke;
46:     auto& fi = s.fill;
47: 
48:     // a page with a folded corner (file icons)
49:     auto page = [&st] {
50:         juce::Path p;
51:         polyline (p, { { 6.0f, 3.5f }, { 13.5f, 3.5f }, { 18.0f, 8.0f }, { 18.0f, 20.5f }, { 6.0f, 20.5f } }, true);
52:         st.addPath (p.createPathWithRoundedCorners (1.5f));
53:         polyline (st, { { 13.5f, 3.5f }, { 13.5f, 8.0f }, { 18.0f, 8.0f } });
54:     };
55: 
56:     // a speaker cone (mute and playback tools)
57:     auto speaker = [&st] {
58:         juce::Path p;
59:         polyline (p, { { 3.5f, 9.5f }, { 7.0f, 9.5f }, { 11.5f, 5.5f }, { 11.5f, 18.5f }, { 7.0f, 14.5f }, { 3.5f, 14.5f } }, true);
60:         st.addPath (p.createPathWithRoundedCorners (1.2f));
61:     };
62: 
63:     // a window outline with a title band (view icons)
64:     auto window = [&st] {
65:         roundRect (st, 3.5f, 4.5f, 17.0f, 15.0f, 2.0f);
66:     };
67: 
68:     // a horseshoe magnet with solid poles, opening downwards (the way FL users know the snap), scaled by `k` and moved
69:     // by (dx, dy) (snap icons)
70:     auto magnet = [&st, &fi] (float k, float dx, float dy) {
71:         const auto t = juce::AffineTransform::verticalFlip (24.0f).scaled (k).translated (dx, dy);
72:         juce::Path m;
73:         m.startNewSubPath (4.5f, 4.0f);
74:         m.lineTo (4.5f, 12.5f);
75:         m.addCentredArc (12.0f, 12.5f, 7.5f, 7.5f, 0.0f, pi * 1.5f, pi * 0.5f, false);
76:         m.lineTo (19.5f, 4.0f);
77:         m.lineTo (15.0f, 4.0f);
78:         m.lineTo (15.0f, 12.5f);
79:         m.addCentredArc (12.0f, 12.5f, 3.0f, 3.0f, 0.0f, pi * 0.5f, pi * 1.5f, false);
80:         m.lineTo (9.0f, 4.0f);
81:         m.closeSubPath();
82:         st.addPath (m, t);
83:         juce::Path poles;
84:         poles.addRectangle (4.5f, 4.0f, 4.5f, 3.5f);
85:         poles.addRectangle (15.0f, 4.0f, 4.5f, 3.5f);
86:         fi.addPath (poles, t);
87:     };
88: 
89:     switch (icon)
90:     {
91:         case Icon::none:
92:             break;
93: 
94:         // --- transport (solid) ---
95:         case Icon::play:
96:         {
97:             juce::Path tri;
98:             tri.addTriangle (7.0f, 4.5f, 7.0f, 19.5f, 19.5f, 12.0f);
99:             fi = tri.createPathWithRoundedCorners (2.0f);
100:             break;
101:         }
102:         case Icon::pause:
103:             fi.addRoundedRectangle (6.0f, 5.0f, 4.5f, 14.0f, 1.25f);
104:             fi.addRoundedRectangle (13.5f, 5.0f, 4.5f, 14.0f, 1.25f);
105:             break;
106:         case Icon::stop:

108:             break;

111:             break;

116:             break;

127:             break;
128:         case Icon::close:

131:             break;

138:             break;

142:             break;

=== src/ui/PlaylistComponent.h ===
1: #pragma once
2: 
3: #include <functional>
4: #include <map>
5: #include <memory>
6: #include <string>

17: 
18: namespace lynx
19: {
20: class PatternBlocksView;
21: 
22: // FL-style Playlist (arrangement): tracks vertically, time horizontally.
23: //  - a tool strip (Pencil, Brush, Eraser, Mute, Knife, Lasso, Zoom, Listen, Slip); with the Pencil:
24: //    left click on empty space "paints" the current source (and starts dragging it), dragging a clip moves
25: //    it (time and track), its edges trim it without moving the content (Shift+edge too), and the fade
26: //    handles of audio clips set the fades
27: //  - every tool: right click deletes (dragging deletes several; press and hold on empty space = Knife),
28: //    Ctrl+drag selects (Ctrl+click adds), Alt ignores the grid; Shift+drag on a clip drags a copy;
29: //    Alt+Shift+drag inside a clip slips its content
30: //  - clicking a clip makes its pattern the current one; double click opens it in the Channel Rack
31: //  - ruler: click sets the playback position (Song mode); Ctrl+drag makes a time selection (it loops and
32: //    selects the clips it touches); a double click clears it
33: //  - dragging an audio file into the Playlist creates an audio clip
34: //  - wheel scrolls (smoothly), Shift+wheel horizontal, Ctrl+wheel zooms at the mouse, Shift+Alt+wheel
35: //    nudges the selection; Ctrl+right-click on empty space zooms to everything and back
36: //  - the clip under the mouse is outlined, its edges show grips and its title strip a menu button; the
37: //    cursor and the hint bar tell what a click would do (core/EditorGestures.h)
38: //  - right click on a clip's name strip (or its menu button) opens its menu (split, make unique, etc.)
39: //  - track header (FL's layout: name, group arrow, LED on the right): click selects the track, the LED mutes it
40: //    (Ctrl+click or right-click on it: solo), the arrow of a group header folds / unfolds its group; an audio
41: //    track's record button arms / disarms its mixer track and its input button picks the input (FL 2026); right
42: //    click elsewhere opens the track menu (FL's order); double click opens the source of the track's first
43: //    clip; Shift+wheel moves the track up / down. Grouped tracks are indented under their header (REA round 104)
44: //  - keys (via handleKey): Delete, Backspace (global grid on/off), Ctrl+A/C/X/V, Ctrl+B (repeats the
45: //    time selection when there is one), Ctrl+Ins / Ctrl+Del insert / remove the time selection, Esc
46: class PlaylistComponent final : public juce::Component,
47:                                 public SharesTitleBar,
48:                                 public juce::FileDragAndDropTarget,
49:                                 public juce::DragAndDropTarget,
50:                                 public ui::HintSource,
51:                                 private juce::ChangeListener,
52:                                 private juce::ScrollBar::Listener,
53:                                 private juce::Timer

61:     };
62:     Brush brush;

193:     // The view kept as an image, the playhead drawn over it (round 174e): a playhead frame only blits its column of the
194:     // image and draws the line, instead of painting the lanes, clips and waveforms under it again 144 times a second.
195:     // Any other repaint (an edit, a scroll, a child) refreshes its area of the image. LYNX_PLAYLIST_CACHE=0: off.
196:     class PlayheadCache;
197:     bool playheadOverlay = false;      // the cache is on: the playhead's own repaints keep its image
198:     bool playheadRepaintOnly = false;  // set around the playhead's own repaints: the image stays valid
199:     bool cachePainting = false;        // paint() is filling the image: no playhead in it
200:     bool scrollRepaintOnly = false;    // set around a scroll's repaint: the cache shifts its picture (round 174e2)
201:     struct CacheView
202:     {

205:         double pixelsPerTick = 0.0;
206:         int tracksHeight = 0, tracks = 0;
207:         bool overlays = false; // the Ripple / Performance tags in the grid's corner (they do not scroll)
208:         int width = 0, height = 0;
209:         bool operator== (const CacheView&) const = default;
210:     };
211:     struct CacheShift

214:         int dx = 0, dy = 0;
215:     };

456: // Playlist "Picker" (as in FL): three tabs at the top (patterns, audio clips, automation clips) and the list
457: // of that kind; clicking chooses what the Playlist pencil paints, double click opens a pattern; right click on
458: // a pattern opens the pattern menu; "+" at the bottom of the pattern list adds a pattern (REA round 104).
459: class PatternPicker final : public juce::Component,
460:                             public ui::HintSource,
461:                             private juce::ChangeListener
462: {

472:     void mouseMove (const juce::MouseEvent& e) override;
473:     void mouseExit (const juce::MouseEvent& e) override;
474:     ui::Hint hintAt (juce::Point<int> position) override;
475: 
476:     // The patterns picked in the list (FL's picker selection: click, Ctrl+click, Shift+click; round 169k), in the
477:     // order they were picked; empty when none is picked.
478:     std::vector<int> pickedPatterns() const;
479: 
480:     std::function<void()> onOpenPattern;
481:     std::function<void()> onPatternMenu; // right click on a pattern (it is the current one by then)
482:     std::function<void()> onNewPattern;  // the "+" under the pattern list
483:     static constexpr int width = 150;
484: 
485:     // the kind listed: 0 = patterns, 1 = audio clips, 2 = automation clips
486:     void setView (int newView);
487:     int getView() const noexcept { return view; }
488: 
489: private:
490:     void changeListenerCallback (juce::ChangeBroadcaster*) override;
491:

493:     {
494:         ClipKind kind;
495:         int index;            // pattern, audio or automation channel
496:         juce::String name;

498:     };
499:     std::vector<Row> rows() const;

518: // The Playlist tab: the Picker on the left and the Playlist (does not own them).
519: class PlaylistArea final : public juce::Component,
520:                            public SharesTitleBar
521: {
522: public:
523:     PlaylistArea (PatternPicker& p, PlaylistComponent& l) : picker (p), playlist (l)
524:     {
525:         addAndMakeVisible (picker);
526:         addAndMakeVisible (playlist);
527:     }
528: 
529:     // the toolbar row runs across the whole window (as the title bar); the picker starts under it
530:     void setTitleBarInsets (int left, int right) override
531:     {
532:         titleLeft = left;
533:         playlist.setTitleBarInsets (juce::jmax (0, left - PatternPicker::width), right);
534:         resized();
535:     }
536: 
537:     void resized() override
538:     {
539:         auto area = getLocalBounds();
540:         auto column = area.removeFromLeft (PatternPicker::width);
541:         picker.setBounds (titleLeft > 0 ? column.withTrimmedTop (PlaylistComponent::toolbarHeight) : column);
542:         playlist.setBounds (area);
543:     }
544: 
545:     void paint (juce::Graphics& g) override
546:     {
547:         g.setColour (theme::colour::bg3); // the toolbar band above the picker
548:         g.fillRect (0, 0, PatternPicker::width, PlaylistComponent::toolbarHeight);
549:         g.setColour (theme::colour::border);
550:         g.fillRect (0, PlaylistComponent::toolbarHeight - 1, PatternPicker::width, 1);
551:     }
552: 
553: private:
554:     PatternPicker& picker;
555:     PlaylistComponent& playlist;
556:     int titleLeft = 0;
557: };
558: } // namespace lynx

50: namespace lynx
51: {
52: namespace
53: {
54: constexpr float clipTitleHeight = 13.0f;
55: 
56: // --- clip look -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
57: // One recipe for every clip kind (studied from FL's Playlist, drawn with LYNX's own code and palette): a muted
58: // header bar holding a small kind glyph and the name, a body in a deep tint of the clip colour, and the
59: // content (waveform, notes, curve) in a light tint of it; a thin dark edge separates neighbours. Few elements,
60: // one hue per clip, so a busy Playlist stays calm and every clip reads at a glance.
61: 
62: enum class ClipGlyph { pattern, audio, automation };
63: 
64: // Text colour readable on a clip's header bar.
65: juce::Colour clipTitleText (juce::Colour band)
66: {
67:     return band.getPerceivedBrightness() > 0.62f ? theme::colour::bg0 : juce::Colours::white.withAlpha (0.92f);
68: }
69: 
70: // the header keeps a hint of the clip colour, greyed so the names read and the bodies carry the colour
71: juce::Colour clipHeaderColour (juce::Colour base) { return base.interpolatedWith (juce::Colour (0xff4b525a), 0.5f); }
72: juce::Colour clipBodyColour (juce::Colour base) { return base.interpolatedWith (theme::colour::bg1, 0.72f); }
73: juce::Colour clipContentColour (juce::Colour base) { return base.interpolatedWith (juce::Colours::white, 0.7f); }
74: 
75: float clipHeaderHeight (juce::Rectangle<float> r) { return juce::jmin (clipTitleHeight, juce::jmax (0.0f, r.getHeight() - 8.0f)); }
76: 
77: // an audio clip's gain as FL's gain handle shows it (round 166e): "-INF" at the bottom of the range
78: juce::String gainText (float gain)
79: {
80:     const auto db = clipops::clipGainToDb (gain);
81:     return db <= clipops::clipGainMinDb + 1.0e-6 ? juce::String ("-INF dB") : juce::String (db, 1) + " dB";
82: }
83: 
84: // The kind glyph at the header's left (the clip menu opens from it): LYNX's own small shapes.
85: void paintClipGlyph (juce::Graphics& g, juce::Rectangle<float> box, ClipGlyph glyph, juce::Colour colour)
86: {
87:     g.setColour (colour);
88:     const auto c = box.getCentre();
89:     switch (glyph)
90:     {
91:         case ClipGlyph::pattern: // three note bars, stepped
92:             for (int i = 0; i < 3; ++i)
93:                 g.fillRect (juce::Rectangle<float> (c.x - 4.0f + (float) i * 2.0f, c.y - 3.5f + (float) i * 3.0f, 5.0f, 1.5f));
94:             break;
95:         case ClipGlyph::audio: // a small mirrored waveform
96:         {
97:             const float heights[] = { 2.0f, 6.0f, 9.0f, 5.0f, 3.0f };
98:             for (int i = 0; i < 5; ++i)
99:                 g.fillRect (juce::Rectangle<float> (c.x - 4.5f + (float) i * 2.0f, c.y - heights[i] * 0.5f, 1.2f, heights[i]));
100:             break;
101:         }
102:         case ClipGlyph::automation: // a rising curve
103:         {
104:             juce::Path p;
105:             p.startNewSubPath (c.x - 4.5f, c.y + 3.5f);
106:             p.quadraticTo (c.x + 1.0f, c.y + 3.5f, c.x + 4.5f, c.y - 3.5f);
107:             g.strokePath (p, juce::PathStrokeType (1.3f));
108:             break;
109:         }
110:     }
111: }
112: 
113: // Clip body with the header bar, glyph and name; returns the body below the header.
114: juce::Rectangle<float> paintClipFrame (juce::Graphics& g, juce::Rectangle<float> r, juce::Colour base, const juce::String& name,
115:                                        ClipGlyph glyph = ClipGlyph::pattern)
116: {
117:     using namespace theme;
118:     const uitrace::Part tally (uitrace::part::frame);
119:     const auto title = r.withHeight (clipHeaderHeight (r));
120: 
121:     // zoomed far out (a clip a few pixels wide, often a thousand of them on screen): plain rectangles, the same
122:     // colours. Rounded shapes and gradients cost a path each on the GPU renderer (round 151: 1 350 clips of
123:     // Thief's chopped vocals took 20-40 ms a frame), and at this size they look the same.
124:     if (r.getWidth() < 8.0f)
125:     {
126:         g.setColour (clipBodyColour (base));
127:         g.fillRect (r);
128:         if (title.getHeight() >= 4.0f)
129:         {
130:             g.setColour (clipHeaderColour (base));
131:             g.fillRect (title);
132:         }
133:         return r.withTrimmedTop (title.getHeight());
134:     }
135: 
136:     g.setColour (clipBodyColour (base));
137:     g.fillRoundedRectangle (r, radius::sm);
138: 
139:     if (title.getHeight() >= 4.0f)
140:     {
141:         // the header band: rounded top corners, square bottom; a lighter top half gives it the slight sheen of a
142:         // gradient (round 151: drawn with the renderer's rounded-rectangle and rectangle primitives, not a path and
143:         // a gradient brush, which cost a GPU geometry each for every clip)
144:         const auto header = clipHeaderColour (base);
145:         g.setColour (header);
146:         g.fillRoundedRectangle (title, radius::sm);
147:         g.fillRect (title.withTrimmedTop (juce::jmin (radius::sm, title.getHeight() * 0.5f)));
148:         g.setColour (header.brighter (0.06f));
149:         g.fillRect (title.reduced (juce::jmin (radius::sm, title.getWidth() * 0.5f), 0.0f).withHeight (title.getHeight() * 0.45f).withTrimmedTop (1.0f));
150:         g.setColour (theme::colour::bg0.withAlpha (0.35f)); // the header's lower edge
151:         g.fillRect (title.getX(), title.getBottom() - 1.0f, title.getWidth(), 1.0f);
152: 
153:         if (r.getWidth() > 18.0f && title.getHeight() >= 9.0f)
154:         {
155:             auto text = title.withTrimmedLeft (3.0f).withTrimmedRight (2.0f);
156:             if (r.getWidth() >= 40.0f) // room for the glyph (where the clip menu opens)
157:                 paintClipGlyph (g, text.removeFromLeft (13.0f), glyph, clipTitleText (header).withMultipliedAlpha (0.8f));
158:             const uitrace::Part tallyTitle (uitrace::part::title);
159:             g.setColour (clipTitleText (header));
160:             g.setFont (font (type::caption, true));
161:             textcache::drawText (g, name, text.withTrimmedLeft (2.0f).toNearestInt(), juce::Justification::centredLeft, true);
162:         }
163:     }
164: 
165:     return r.withTrimmedTop (title.getHeight());
166: }
167: 
168: void paintClipOutline (juce::Graphics& g, juce::Rectangle<float> r, juce::Colour base, bool selected, bool current)
169: {
170:     using namespace theme;
171:     const uitrace::Part tally (uitrace::part::outline);
172:     if (r.getWidth() < 8.0f && ! selected) // tiny clip (see paintClipFrame): a dark right edge keeps neighbours apart
173:     {
174:         g.setColour (current ? colour::textPrimary.withAlpha (0.55f) : colour::bg0.withAlpha (0.85f));
175:         g.fillRect (r.getRight() - 1.0f, r.getY(), 1.0f, r.getHeight());
176:         return;
177:     }
178:     if (selected)
179:     {
180:         g.setColour (colour::selection);
181:         g.drawRoundedRectangle (r.reduced (0.5f), radius::sm, 2.0f);
182:     }
183:     else
184:     {
185:         g.setColour (current ? colour::textPrimary.withAlpha (0.55f) : colour::bg0.withAlpha (0.85f));
186:         g.drawRoundedRectangle (r.reduced (0.5f), radius::sm, current ? 1.2f : 1.0f);
187:     }
188: }

192: // Zoomed far out, a song of chopped audio shows a thousand clips a few pixels wide. Painted one by one, each is a
193: // handful of rounded paths (frame, header, waveform, outline), and the GPU renderer pays per path: Thief's Playlist
194: // took 33-47 ms a frame to scroll (round 151). These clips are gathered here, rectangles per colour and waveforms per
195: // colour as one path, and filled together after the other clips: a few calls a frame. At this size they look the
196: // same as the full recipe.
197: struct PlaylistComponent::SmallClips
198: {
199:     static constexpr float maxWidth = 12.0f;
200:     std::map<juce::uint32, juce::RectangleList<float>> bodies, contents, edges;
201:     std::map<juce::uint32, juce::Path> waves;
202: 
203:     void fill (juce::Graphics& g)
204:     {
205:         // (rectangle by rectangle: the GPU renderer turns a rectangle list into a geometry, which costs more)
206:         for (auto* layer : { &bodies, &contents })
207:             for (auto& [argb, list] : *layer)
208:             {
209:                 g.setColour (juce::Colour (argb));
210:                 for (const auto& r : list)
211:                     g.fillRect (r);
212:             }
213:         const uitrace::Part tally (uitrace::part::batchWaves);
214:         for (auto& [argb, path] : waves)
215:         {
216:             g.setColour (juce::Colour (argb));
217:             g.fillPath (path);
218:         }
219:         for (auto& [argb, list] : edges)
220:         {
221:             g.setColour (juce::Colour (argb));
222:             for (const auto& r : list)
223:                 g.fillRect (r);
224:         }
225:     }
226: };
227: 
228: // The Playlist's image cache (round 174e): JUCE's buffered-to-image cache, plus the playhead drawn over the image and
229: // the playhead's own repaints leaving the image valid
230: class PlaylistComponent::PlayheadCache final : public juce::CachedComponentImage
231: {
232: public:
233:     explicit PlayheadCache (PlaylistComponent& o) : owner (o) {}
234: 
235:     void paint (juce::Graphics& g) override
236:     {
237:         const auto scale = g.getInternalContext().getPhysicalPixelScaleFactor();
238:         const auto bounds = owner.getLocalBounds();
239:         const auto imageBounds = bounds * scale;
240:         const auto nowMs = juce::Time::getMillisecondCounterHiRes();
241:         const auto clip = g.getClipBounds();
242: 
243:         if (image.isNull() || image.getBounds() != imageBounds)
244:         {
245:             image = juce::Image (juce::Image::RGB, juce::jmax (1, imageBounds.getWidth()), juce::jmax (1, imageBounds.getHeight()), false);
246:             spare = {};
247:             valid.clear();
248:         }
249: 
250:         // the view moved since the picture was drawn. A scroll by whole pixels (round 174e2): the picture is shifted
251:         // and only the columns / rows it uncovered are painted (FL scrolls at ~120 fps). A zoom, a track resize, a
252:         // fractional scroll: everything is painted again.
253:         const auto view = owner.cacheView();
254:         CacheShift shift;
255:         if (view != drawnView)
256:         {
257:             shift = owner.cacheShift (drawnView, view, scale);
258:             if (shift.area.isEmpty())
259:                 valid.clear();
260:         }
261:         auto validNow = valid;
262:         if (! shift.area.isEmpty())
263:         {
264:             juce::RectangleList<int> moved;
265:             for (const auto& r : valid)
266:                 if (const auto in = r.getIntersection (shift.area); ! in.isEmpty())
267:                     moved.addWithoutMerging (in.translated (shift.dx, shift.dy).getIntersection (shift.area));
268:             validNow.subtract (shift.area);
269:             validNow.subtract (shift.alsoChanged);
270:             validNow.add (moved);
271:         }
272: 
273:         // a repaint of (almost) everything (a zoom, an edit): painted straight to the window (through the picture it costs
274:         // a render and a full blit); the picture is rebuilt once the view has been still for a moment, by the next
275:         // playhead frame. Not a scroll: it goes through the picture, so the next frame can shift it (painted straight,
276:         // every scroll frame found the picture gone and painted everything again)
277:         auto invalid = juce::RectangleList<int> (bounds);
278:         invalid.subtract (validNow);
279:         const auto half = 0.5 * bounds.getWidth() * bounds.getHeight();
280:         if ((double) clip.getWidth() * clip.getHeight() > half && area (invalid) > half)
281:             lastLargeMs = nowMs;
282:         if (nowMs - lastLargeMs < 300.0 && shift.area.isEmpty() && ! validNow.containsRectangle (clip))
283:         {
284:             if (view != drawnView)
285:             {
286:                 valid.clear(); // (the picture is of another view now; later invalidations are in this one's coordinates)
287:                 drawnView = view;
288:             }
289:             owner.paintEntireComponent (g, false);
290:             return;
291:         }
292: 
293:         const auto shifting = ! shift.area.isEmpty();
294:         if (shifting || ! invalid.isEmpty())
295:         {
296:             if (shifting && (spare.isNull() || spare.getBounds() != image.getBounds()))
297:                 spare = juce::Image (juce::Image::RGB, image.getWidth(), image.getHeight(), false);
298:             auto& target = shifting ? spare : image;
299:             const ui::ScopedNoImageReadback noReadback; // (the picture is only ever drawn to the window)
300:             juce::Graphics imageGraphics (target);
301:             if (shifting)
302:             {
303:                 const auto physical = (shift.area.toFloat() * scale).getSmallestIntegerContainer();
304:                 {
305:                     const juce::Graphics::ScopedSaveState state (imageGraphics);
306:                     imageGraphics.excludeClipRegion (physical);
307:                     imageGraphics.drawImageAt (image, 0, 0);
308:                 }
309:                 const juce::Graphics::ScopedSaveState state (imageGraphics);
310:                 if (imageGraphics.reduceClipRegion (physical))
311:                     imageGraphics.drawImageAt (image, juce::roundToInt ((float) shift.dx * scale), juce::roundToInt ((float) shift.dy * scale));
312:             }
313:             if (! invalid.isEmpty())
314:             {
315:                 auto& context = imageGraphics.getInternalContext();
316:                 context.addTransform (juce::AffineTransform::scale (scale));
317:                 for (const auto& r : validNow)
318:                     context.excludeClipRectangle (r);
319:                 owner.cachePainting = true; // (the picture without the playhead: drawn over it below)
320:                 owner.paintEntireComponent (imageGraphics, true);
321:                 owner.cachePainting = false;
322:             }
323:         }
324:         if (shifting)
325:             std::swap (image, spare);
326:         valid = bounds;
327:         drawnView = view;
328: 
329:         g.setColour (juce::Colours::black);
330:         g.drawImageTransformed (image,
331:                                 juce::AffineTransform::scale ((float) bounds.getWidth() / (float) imageBounds.getWidth(),
332:                                                               (float) bounds.getHeight() / (float) imageBounds.getHeight()),
333:                                 false);
334:         owner.paintPlayhead (g);
335:     }
336: 
337:     bool invalidateAll() override // (JUCE's repaint() of the whole component comes here, not to invalidate())
338:     {
339:         if (! owner.playheadRepaintOnly && ! owner.scrollRepaintOnly)
340:             valid.clear();
341:         return true;
342:     }
343:     bool invalidate (const juce::Rectangle<int>& r) override
344:     {
345:         if (! owner.playheadRepaintOnly && ! owner.scrollRepaintOnly)
346:             valid.subtract (r);
347:         return true; // (the window repaints the area either way)
348:     }
349:     void releaseResources() override
350:     {
351:         image = {};
352:         spare = {};
353:     }
354: 
355: private:
356:     static double area (const juce::RectangleList<int>& list)
357:     {
358:         double a = 0.0;
359:         for (const auto& r : list)
360:             a += (double) r.getWidth() * r.getHeight();
361:         return a;
362:     }
363: 
364:     PlaylistComponent& owner;
365:     juce::Image image, spare; // spare: the shifted copy is drawn into it, then they swap
366:     juce::RectangleList<int> valid;
367:     CacheView drawnView;
368:     double lastLargeMs = 0.0;
369: };
370: 
371: // What the cached picture depends on beyond what repaints say (round 174e2)
372: PlaylistComponent::CacheView PlaylistComponent::cacheView() const
373: {
374:     return { scrollTicks, scrollY, pixelsPerTick, trackTops.empty() ? 0 : trackTops.back(), (int) trackTops.size(),
375:              ripple || engine.getSong().performanceMode, getWidth(), getHeight() };
376: }
377: 
378: // The picture of view `from` shown as `to` by shifting it: the area that moves and by how much (whole physical pixels).
379: // Horizontal: the ruler and the lanes (the headers stay); vertical: the headers and the lanes (the ruler stays).
380: // Empty area: not a plain scroll (zoom, resize, track heights, both directions at once, the corner tags).
381: PlaylistComponent::CacheShift PlaylistComponent::cacheShift (const CacheView& from, const CacheView& to, float scale) const
382: {
383:     if (from.pixelsPerTick != to.pixelsPerTick || from.tracksHeight != to.tracksHeight || from.tracks != to.tracks
384:         || from.overlays || to.overlays || from.width != to.width || from.height != to.height)
385:         return {};
386:     const auto grid = gridArea();
387:     const auto dxExact = (from.scrollTicks - to.scrollTicks) * to.pixelsPerTick;
388:     const auto dx = (int) std::lround (dxExact), dy = from.scrollY - to.scrollY;
389:     const auto whole = [scale] (int d) { return std::abs ((float) d * scale - (float) juce::roundToInt ((float) d * scale)) < 0.01f; };
390:     if (std::abs (dxExact - dx) > 0.01 || (dx != 0 && dy != 0) || ! whole (dx) || ! whole (dy))
391:         return {};
392:     CacheShift shift;
393:     if (dx != 0)
394:     {
395:         shift.area = { headerWidth, rulerTop, grid.getRight() - headerWidth, grid.getBottom() - rulerTop };
396:         shift.alsoChanged = { headerWidth, grid.getBottom(), grid.getWidth(), getHeight() - grid.getBottom() }; // (the pattern blocks)
397:     }
398:     else
399:         shift.area = { 0, headerHeight, grid.getRight(), grid.getHeight() };
400:     shift.dx = dx;
401:     shift.dy = dy;
402:     if (std::abs (dx) >= shift.area.getWidth() || std::abs (dy) >= shift.area.getHeight())
403:         return {};
404:     return shift;
405: }
406: 
407: // A repaint after the view scrolled: the cached picture is shifted instead of painted again (round 174e2)
408: void PlaylistComponent::repaintScrolled()
409: {
410:     scrollRepaintOnly = playheadOverlay;
411:     repaint();
412:     scrollRepaintOnly = false;
413: }

622: juce::Rectangle<int> PlaylistComponent::gridArea() const
623: {

627: 
628: int PlaylistComponent::blocksSectionHeight() const
629: {
630:     if (! engine.getSong().patternBlocksOn)
631:         return 0;
632:     const auto available = getHeight() - headerHeight;
633:     const auto least = PatternBlocksView::dividerHeight + 3 * PatternBlocksView::rowHeight;
634:     const auto most = juce::jmax (least, available - 2 * trackHeight); // the clip tracks keep two rows
635:     // by default a third of the grid, or less when every row (and a spare one) fits in less
636:     const auto rows = PatternBlocksView::dividerHeight + ((int) engine.getSong().patterns.size() + 1) * PatternBlocksView::rowHeight;
637:     const auto wanted = blocksHeight > 0 ? blocksHeight : juce::jmin (available / 3, rows);
638:     return juce::jlimit (least, most, wanted);
639: }

642: {
643:     blocksHeight = juce::jmax (1, height);
644:     resized();
645:     repaint();
646: }

655: void PlaylistComponent::togglePatternBlocks()
656: {
657:     const auto& song = engine.getSong();
658:     if (! song.patternBlocksOn)
659:     {
660:         engine.editSong ([] (Song& s) { s.patternBlocksOn = true; });
661:         return;
662:     }
663:     auto any = false;
664:     for (const auto& a : song.arrangements)
665:         any = any || ! a.blocks.empty();
666:     if (song.blocks.empty() && ! any)
667:     {
668:         engine.editSong ([] (Song& s) { s.patternBlocksOn = false; });
669:         return;
670:     }
671:     // blocks that stay hidden would still be in the project but silent: FL's own way out was to turn them into clips
672:     auto options = juce::MessageBoxOptions()
673:                        .withIconType (juce::MessageBoxIconType::QuestionIcon)
674:                        .withTitle (tr ("Pattern blocks"))
675:                        .withMessage (tr ("This project has pattern blocks. Turn them into clips (one track per pattern) so they keep playing, or delete them?"))
676:                        .withButton (tr ("Turn them into clips"))

693: {

701: }

703: double PlaylistComponent::tickToX (double tick) const { return headerWidth + (tick - scrollTicks) * pixelsPerTick; }
704: double PlaylistComponent::xToTick (int x) const { return (x - headerWidth) / pixelsPerTick + scrollTicks; }
705: int PlaylistComponent::heightOf (int track) const
706: {
707:     const auto& tracks = engine.getSong().playlistTracks;
708:     if (noteedit::isFoldedAway (engine.getSong(), track))
709:         return foldedHeight;
710:     const auto h = track >= 0 && track < (int) tracks.size() ? tracks[(std::size_t) track].height : 0;
711:     // "Lock to this size" (FL +0x14, round 168f): the row keeps its px height whatever the vertical zoom
712:     if (const auto locked = track >= 0 && track < (int) tracks.size() ? tracks[(std::size_t) track].lockedHeight : -1; locked > 0)
713:         return polish::clampTrackHeight (locked);
714:     return scaledHeight (h > 0 ? polish::clampTrackHeight (h) : trackHeight);
715: }

721: }

723: void PlaylistComponent::setTrackZoom (double zoom, int anchorY)
724: {
725:     zoom = juce::jlimit (0.5, 4.0, zoom);
726:     if (std::abs (zoom - trackZoom) < 1.0e-4)
727:         return;
728: 
729:     // the track under `anchorY` stays where it is
730:     anchorY = juce::jlimit (headerHeight, juce::jmax (headerHeight, gridArea().getBottom() - 1), anchorY);
731:     const auto track = yToTrack (anchorY);
732:     const auto within = heightOf (track) > 0 ? (double) (anchorY - trackToY (track)) / heightOf (track) : 0.0;
733:     trackZoom = zoom;
734:     refreshTrackLayout();
735:     const auto top = trackToY (track) + scrollY - headerHeight; // the track's top in the content
736:     scrollY = juce::jmax (0, top + (int) std::lround (within * heightOf (track)) - (anchorY - headerHeight));
737:     updateScrollBars();
738:     repaint();
739:     ui::postHint ({ tr ("Track height (vertical zoom)"), juce::String (juce::roundToInt (trackZoom * 100.0)) + "%", false, false });
740: }
741: 
742: void PlaylistComponent::refreshTrackLayout()
743: {
744:     const auto count = numTracks();
745:     trackTops.assign ((std::size_t) count + 1, 0);
746: 
747:     for (int t = 0; t < count; ++t)
748:         trackTops[(std::size_t) t + 1] = trackTops[(std::size_t) t] + heightOf (t);
749: }

752: {

756: int PlaylistComponent::trackToY (int track) const
757: {
758:     // past the cached rows (the list grew during a drag): default heights
759:     const auto last = (int) trackTops.size() - 1;
760:     const auto top = trackTops.empty() ? track * defaultRowHeight()
761:                      : track <= last   ? trackTops[(std::size_t) juce::jmax (0, track)]
762:                                        : trackTops.back() + (track - last) * defaultRowHeight();
763:     return headerHeight + top - scrollY;

713:         return polish::clampTrackHeight (locked);
