766: int PlaylistComponent::yToTrack (int y) const
767: {
768:     const auto offset = y - headerHeight + scrollY;
769: 
770:     if (trackTops.size() < 2)
771:         return juce::jlimit (0, numTracks() - 1, offset / defaultRowHeight());
772: 
773:     const auto it = std::upper_bound (trackTops.begin(), trackTops.end(), offset);
774:     const auto index = (int) (it - trackTops.begin()) - 1;
775:     return juce::jlimit (0, numTracks() - 1, index);
776: }
777: 
778: int PlaylistComponent::dividerAt (juce::Point<int> p) const
779: {
780:     // the bottom edge of a track header (4 px band) resizes that track
781:     if (p.x >= headerWidth || p.y < headerHeight || p.y >= gridArea().getBottom())
782:         return -1;
783: 
784:     const auto track = yToTrack (p.y);
785: 
786:     for (auto t : { track - 1, track })
787:         if (t >= 0 && ! noteedit::isFoldedAway (engine.getSong(), t) && std::abs (p.y - (trackToY (t) + heightOf (t))) <= 3)
788:             return t;
789: 
790:     return -1;
791: }
-- src/ui/PlaylistComponent.cpp:768 --
740: }
741: 
742: void PlaylistComponent::refreshTrackLayout()
743: {
744:     const auto count = numTracks();
745:     trackTops.assign ((std::size_t) count + 1, 0);
746: 
747:     for (int t = 0; t < count; ++t)
748:         trackTops[(std::size_t) t + 1] = trackTops[(std::size_t) t] + heightOf (t);
749: }
750: 
751: int PlaylistComponent::tracksHeight() const
752: {
753:     return trackTops.empty() ? numTracks() * defaultRowHeight() : trackTops.back();
754: }
755: 
756: int PlaylistComponent::trackToY (int track) const
757: {
758:     // past the cached rows (the list grew during a drag): default heights
759:     const auto last = (int) trackTops.size() - 1;
760:     const auto top = trackTops.empty() ? track * defaultRowHeight()
761:                      : track <= last   ? trackTops[(std::size_t) juce::jmax (0, track)]
762:                                        : trackTops.back() + (track - last) * defaultRowHeight();
763:     return headerHeight + top - scrollY;
764: }
765: 
766: int PlaylistComponent::yToTrack (int y) const
767: {
768:     const auto offset = y - headerHeight + scrollY;
769: 
770:     if (trackTops.size() < 2)
771:         return juce::jlimit (0, numTracks() - 1, offset / defaultRowHeight());
772: 
773:     const auto it = std::upper_bound (trackTops.begin(), trackTops.end(), offset);
774:     const auto index = (int) (it - trackTops.begin()) - 1;
775:     return juce::jlimit (0, numTracks() - 1, index);
776: }

778: int PlaylistComponent::dividerAt (juce::Point<int> p) const
779: {
780:     // the bottom edge of a track header (4 px band) resizes that track
781:     if (p.x >= headerWidth || p.y < headerHeight || p.y >= gridArea().getBottom())
782:         return -1;
783: 
784:     const auto track = yToTrack (p.y);
785: 
786:     for (auto t : { track - 1, track })
787:         if (t >= 0 && ! noteedit::isFoldedAway (engine.getSong(), t) && std::abs (p.y - (trackToY (t) + heightOf (t))) <= 3)
788:             return t;
789: 
790:     return -1;
791: }

794: {
795:     engine.editSong ([tracks, height] (Song& s) {
796:         for (auto t : tracks)

856: 
857: std::int64_t PlaylistComponent::snap (double tick, bool enabled) const
858: {
859:     if (! enabled)
860:         return (std::int64_t) std::floor (tick);
861: 
862:     const auto& option = grid::resolve (snapBox);
863:     const auto step = gridTicks();
864: 
865:     if (option.kind == grid::Kind::nearest)
866:         return noteedit::snapNearest (tick, snapCandidates(), 10.0 / pixelsPerTick, step);
867: 
868:     // whole bars respect time signature changes
869:     if (step == (std::int64_t) ppq * juce::jmax (1, engine.getSong().beatsPerBar))
870:         return engine.getSong().barAt ((std::int64_t) std::max (0.0, std::floor (tick))).tick;
871: 
872:     return noteedit::snapDown (tick, step);
873: }
874: 
875: void PlaylistComponent::updateScrollBars()
876: {
877:     const auto& song = engine.getSong();
878:     const auto grid = gridArea();
879:     const auto bar = (double) ppq * song.beatsPerBar;
880:     const auto total = juce::jmax ((double) song.songLength() + 16 * bar, grid.getWidth() / pixelsPerTick);
881: 
882:     hBar.setRangeLimits (0.0, total, juce::dontSendNotification);
883:     hBar.setCurrentRange (scrollTicks, grid.getWidth() / pixelsPerTick, juce::dontSendNotification);
884:     vBar.setRangeLimits (0.0, (double) tracksHeight(), juce::dontSendNotification);
885:     vBar.setCurrentRange (scrollY, grid.getHeight(), juce::dontSendNotification);
886:     scrollTicks = hBar.getCurrentRangeStart();
887:     scrollY = (int) vBar.getCurrentRangeStart();
888:     overview.minLength = grid.getWidth() / (800.0 / (4 * ppq));
889:     overview.setRange (total, scrollTicks, grid.getWidth() / pixelsPerTick);
890: }
891: 
892: void PlaylistComponent::setTitleBarInsets (int left, int right)
893: {
894:     if (left != titleInsetLeft || right != titleInsetRight)
895:     {
896:         titleInsetLeft = left;
897:         titleInsetRight = right;
898:         resized();
899:     }
900: }

902: void PlaylistComponent::resized()
903: {
904:     const auto h = toolbarHeight - 6;
905:     const auto avail = juce::jmax (0, getWidth() - 8 - titleInsetLeft - titleInsetRight);
906:     const auto compactBar = avail < 680;
907:     copyButton.setText (compactBar ? juce::String() : tr ("Copy"));
908:     pasteButton.setText (compactBar ? juce::String() : tr ("Paste"));
909:     auto bar = juce::Rectangle<int> (4 + titleInsetLeft, 3, avail, h);
910:     auto place = [&bar] (juce::Component& c, int w) { c.setBounds (bar.removeFromLeft (juce::jmin (w, bar.getWidth()))); bar.removeFromLeft (juce::jmin (6, bar.getWidth())); };
911:     place (arrangementBox, compactBar ? 88 : juce::jmin (150, getWidth() / 5));
